===== server.js =====

// server.js
import { createServer } from 'http'
import { parse }        from 'url'
import next             from 'next'
import { Server }       from 'socket.io'
import cron             from 'node-cron'
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'

const prisma = new PrismaClient()
const dev    = process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production'
const app    = next({ dev })
const handle = app.getRequestHandler()

const ARCHIVE_AFTER_DAYS = 90

async function archiveOldRequests() {
  const cutoff = new Date()
  cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - ARCHIVE_AFTER_DAYS)
  try {
    const result = await prisma.request.updateMany({
      where: {
        status:      'COMPLETED',
        completedAt: { lte: cutoff },
        archived:    false,
      },
      data: {
        archivedAt: new Date(),
        archived:   true,
      },
    })
    return result.count
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[Archival] Prisma error:', err)
    return 0
  }
}

cron.schedule('0 3 * * *', async () => {
  console.log('[Archival] Checking for requests to archive...')
  try {
    const count = await archiveOldRequests()
    if (count > 0) {
      console.log(`[Archival] Archived ${count} old completed request(s).`)
    } else {
      console.log('[Archival] No requests needed archiving.')
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[Archival] Error:', err)
  }
})

app.prepare().then(() => {
  const httpServer = createServer((req, res) => {
    const parsedUrl = parse(req.url, true)
    handle(req, res, parsedUrl)
  })

  const io = new Server(httpServer, {
    cors: {
      origin:  process.env.NEXTAUTH_URL || 'http://localhost:3000',
      methods: ['GET', 'POST'],
    },
  })

  globalThis.io = io

  io.on('connection', (socket) => {
    // Join role-based room for broadcast notifications
    socket.on('join-role', (role) => {
      socket.join(role)
    })


    socket.on('join-user', (userId) => {
      socket.join(`user_${userId}`)
    })
  })

  const port = parseInt(process.env.PORT || '3000')
  httpServer.listen(port, () => {
    console.log(`> Ready on http://localhost:${port}`)

    if (dev) {
      import('child_process').then(({ exec }) => {
        exec(`start msedge http://localhost:${port}/login`)
      })
    }
  })
})


===== components\Layout.tsx =====

// components/Layout.tsx

function Icon({ path, className = '' }: { path: string; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <svg
      width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none" stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
      className={`shrink-0 ${className}`}
    >
      <path d={path} />
    </svg>
  )
}

type NavLinkDef = {
  href: string
  label: string
  icon: string
  roles: string[]
  zone: 'ops' | 'analytics'
}

type SystemLinkDef = {
  href?: string
  label: string
  icon: string
  roles: string[]
  action?: 'signout' | 'theme'
  danger?: boolean
  divider?: boolean
}

const NAV_LINKS: NavLinkDef[] = [
  { href: '/requests',            label: 'Requests',      icon: 'requests',  roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                             zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/requests/new',        label: 'New Request',   icon: 'new_req',   roles: ['BREEDER', 'ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                                               zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/approvals',           label: 'Approvals',     icon: 'approvals', roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                             zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/inventory',           label: 'Inventory',     icon: 'inventory', roles: ['BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                   zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/inventory/new',       label: 'New Inventory', icon: 'new_inv',   roles: ['INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                                      zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/inventory/clusters',  label: 'Clusters',      icon: 'clusters',  roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],         zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/inventory/transfers', label: 'Transfers',     icon: 'transfers', roles: ['BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                   zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/inventory/logs',      label: 'Usage Logs',    icon: 'logs',      roles: ['BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                   zone: 'ops' },
  { href: '/dashboard',           label: 'Dashboard',     icon: 'dashboard', roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],         zone: 'analytics' },
  { href: '/inventory/forecast',  label: 'Forecast',      icon: 'forecast',  roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],         zone: 'analytics' },
  { href: '/reports/monthly',     label: 'Reports',       icon: 'report',    roles: ['BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER','ADMIN','MANAGEMENT'],                   zone: 'analytics' },
  
]

const SYSTEM_LINKS: SystemLinkDef[] = [
  { href: '/account/settings',  label: 'Settings',      icon: 'settings', roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST','INVENTORY_MANAGER','MANAGEMENT','ADMIN'] },
  { href: '/admin/import',      label: 'Import Data',   icon: 'import',   roles: ['ADMIN','INVENTORY_MANAGER'] },
  { href: '/admin/audit',       label: 'Audit Log',     icon: 'audit',    roles: ['ADMIN'] },
  { href: '/admin/users',       label: 'Users',         icon: 'users',    roles: ['ADMIN'] },
  { href: '/admin/health',      label: 'System Health', icon: 'health',   roles: ['ADMIN'] },
  { href: '/admin/backup',      label: 'Backups',       icon: 'backup',   roles: ['ADMIN'] },
  { label: '—', icon: '', roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST','INVENTORY_MANAGER','MANAGEMENT','ADMIN'], divider: true },
  { label: 'Theme',    icon: 'moon',    roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST','INVENTORY_MANAGER','MANAGEMENT','ADMIN'], action: 'theme' },
  { label: 'Sign out', icon: 'signout', roles: ['BREEDER','BIOTECHNOLOGIST','INVENTORY_MANAGER','MANAGEMENT','ADMIN'], action: 'signout', danger: true },
]

function NavItem({ href, label, icon, isActive }: {
  href: string; label: string; icon: string; isActive: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className={`
        relative flex items-center gap-1.5 h-8 px-3 text-[13px] font-medium
        whitespace-nowrap transition-all duration-150 rounded-lg
        ${isActive
          // ── Active pill: biotech teal-emerald, not neon green ──
          ? 'bg-linear-to-r from-[#14b8a6] to-[#0f9f8f] text-white shadow-[0_4px_14px_rgba(20,184,166,0.22)]'
          : 'text-[#5f6b7a] dark:text-gray-400 hover:text-[#111827] dark:hover:text-gray-200 hover:bg-black/4 dark:hover:bg-white/5'
        }
      `}
    >
      <span className={`transition-colors duration-150 ${
        isActive ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500'
      }`}>
        <Icon path={ICONS[icon] ?? ICONS.requests} />
      </span>
      {label}
    </Link>
  )
}

function Avatar({ name }: { name?: string | null }) {
  const initials = name
    ? name.split(' ').map(w => w[0]).slice(0, 2).join('').toUpperCase()
    : '?'
  return (
    // ── Avatar: same biotech teal-emerald gradient as logo ──
    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-linear-to-br from-[#14b8a6] to-[#0f9f8f] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wide shrink-0 shadow-sm">
      {initials}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function Layout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { data: session } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const isAuthPage = router.pathname === '/login'

  const [profileOpen, setProfileOpen] = useState(false)
  const profileRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClick(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (profileRef.current && !profileRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setProfileOpen(false)
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClick)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClick)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => { const t = setTimeout(() => setProfileOpen(false), 0); return () => clearTimeout(t) }, [router.pathname])

  const visibleNav     = NAV_LINKS.filter(l => !role || l.roles.includes(role))
  const opsLinks       = visibleNav.filter(l => l.zone === 'ops')
  const analyticsLinks = visibleNav.filter(l => l.zone === 'analytics')
  const systemLinks    = SYSTEM_LINKS.filter(l => !role || l.roles.includes(role))

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen overflow-y-auto text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">
      <ToastNotification />

      {/* ── Ambient blobs: teal-emerald family, not cyan/green ── */}
      {!isAuthPage && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 overflow-hidden pointer-events-none z-0">
          <div className="absolute top-[-15%] right-[-8%] w-125 h-125 bg-[#14b8a6]/5 dark:bg-[#14b8a6]/3 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
          <div className="absolute bottom-[-20%] left-[-10%] w-112.5 h-112.5 bg-[#0f9f8f]/5 dark:bg-[#0f9f8f]/3 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Navbar ── */}
      {!isAuthPage && session && (
        <nav className="
          sticky top-0 z-50
          h-14
          flex items-center justify-between
          px-6
          bg-[#f8fbfa]/95 dark:bg-gray-900/95
          backdrop-blur-xl
          border-b border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.18)] dark:border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.12)]
          shadow-[0_1px_0_rgba(20,184,166,0.08)]
        ">

          {/* ── LEFT: Brand + nav ── */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-0 min-w-0 h-full">

            {/* Brand */}
            <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pr-6 shrink-0 group">
              {/* ── Logo tile: teal-emerald, controlled saturation ── */}
              <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-linear-to-br from-[#14b8a6] to-[#0f9f8f] flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
                <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 40 40" fill="none">
                  <path d="M20 32 Q10 22 14 10 Q20 6 26 10 Q34 18 20 32Z" fill="white" fillOpacity="0.95" />
                  <path d="M20 9 Q25 13 20 18 Q15 23 20 27" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.7)" strokeWidth="2.8" fill="none" strokeLinecap="round"/>
                  <path d="M20 9 Q15 13 20 18 Q25 23 20 27" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.45)" strokeWidth="2" fill="none" strokeLinecap="round"/>
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col leading-none">
                <span className="text-[13px] font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 tracking-tight group-hover:text-[#0f9f8f] dark:group-hover:text-[#14b8a6] transition-colors">
                  GenomicLab
                </span>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
                  Lab System
                </span>
              </div>
            </Link>

            {/* Ops group */}
            {opsLinks.length > 0 && (
              // ── Vertical separator: muted teal, not gray or neon ──
              <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 border-l border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.18)] dark:border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.12)] pl-4 h-full">
                {opsLinks.map(l => (
                  <NavItem key={l.href} {...l} isActive={router.pathname === l.href} />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Group separator */}
            {analyticsLinks.length > 0 && opsLinks.length > 0 && (
              // ── Mid-nav divider: teal tint ──
              <div className="mx-3 h-4 w-px bg-[#14b8a6]/18 dark:bg-[#14b8a6]/12 shrink-0" />
            )}

            {/* Analytics group */}
            {analyticsLinks.length > 0 && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                {analyticsLinks.map(l => (
                  <NavItem key={l.href} {...l} isActive={router.pathname === l.href} />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* ── RIGHT: Profile cluster ── */}
          <div className="flex items-center" ref={profileRef}>
            <button
              onClick={() => setProfileOpen(v => !v)}
              className={`
                flex items-center gap-2 h-9 pl-1.5 pr-3 rounded-lg
                transition-colors duration-150
                ${profileOpen
                  ? 'bg-black/5 dark:bg-white/7'
                  : 'hover:bg-black/4 dark:hover:bg-white/5'
                }
              `}
            >
              <Avatar name={session.user?.name} />
              <div className="hidden sm:flex flex-col items-start leading-none">
                <span className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">
                  {session.user?.name?.split(' ')[0] ?? 'User'}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide mt-0.5">
                  {role}
                </span>
              </div>
              <svg
                width="11" height="11" viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5"
                strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
                className={`text-gray-400 transition-transform duration-200 ml-0.5 ${profileOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
              >
                <path d={ICONS.chevron} />
              </svg>
            </button>

            {/* ── Dropdown: teal-tinted border + shadow, no neon glow ── */}
            {profileOpen && (
              <div className="
                absolute top-[calc(100%+8px)] right-4
                w-56
                bg-white dark:bg-gray-900
                border border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.20)] dark:border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.14)]
                rounded-2xl
                shadow-[0_20px_40px_rgba(20,184,166,0.08),0_4px_16px_rgba(15,23,42,0.08)]
                py-3
                z-50
                origin-top-right
              ">
                {/* Account header */}
                <div className="px-4 pb-2.5 border-b border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.14)] dark:border-[rgba(20,184,166,0.10)] flex items-center gap-3">
                  <Avatar name={session.user?.name} />
                  <div className="flex flex-col leading-none min-w-0">
                    <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 truncate">
                      {session.user?.name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mt-0.5 truncate">
                      {session.user?.email}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-1.5 px-1.5">
                  {systemLinks.map((l, i) => {
                    if (l.divider) {
                      // ── Dropdown divider: teal tint ──
                      return <div key={i} className="my-1.5 h-px bg-[#14b8a6]/14 dark:bg-[#14b8a6]/10 mx-2" />
                    }



                    if (l.action === 'signout') {
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={i}
                          onClick={() => signOut({ callbackUrl: '/login' })}
                          className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13px] font-medium text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-900/20 hover:text-red-600 transition-colors duration-150"
                        >
                          <Icon path={ICONS.signout} />
                          Sign out
                        </button>
                      )
                    }

                    if (!l.href) return null
                    const isActive = router.pathname === l.href

                    return (
                      <Link
                        key={l.href}
                        href={l.href}
                        onClick={() => setProfileOpen(false)}
                        className={`
                          group flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13px] font-medium
                          transition-colors duration-150
                          ${isActive
                            // ── Active dropdown item: teal tint, not emerald-50 ──
                            ? 'bg-[#14b8a6]/10 dark:bg-[#14b8a6]/15 text-[#0f9f8f] dark:text-[#14b8a6]'
                            : 'text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 hover:text-gray-900 dark:hover:text-white'
                          }
                        `}
                      >
                        <span className={`transition-colors ${isActive ? 'text-[#14b8a6]' : 'text-gray-400 group-hover:text-gray-500'}`}>
                          <Icon path={ICONS[l.icon] ?? ''} />
                        </span>
                        {l.label}
                      </Link>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </nav>
      )}

      {/* ── Page content ── */}
      <main className="relative z-10 w-full min-h-[calc(100vh-3.5rem)]">
        {children}
      </main>
    </div>
  )
}

===== components\ToastNotification.tsx =====

// components/ToastNotification.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { io } from 'socket.io-client'

type Toast = { id: number; message: string; type: 'info' | 'warning' | 'success' }

let toastSocket: ReturnType<typeof io> | null = null

export default function ToastNotification() {
  const { data: session } = useSession()
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([])
  const role   = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const userId = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!role) return

    fetch('/api/socket').finally(() => {
      if (!toastSocket) {
        toastSocket = io({ path: '/api/socket', addTrailingSlash: false })
      }

      // Join role room
      toastSocket.emit('join-role', role)

      // Join personal user room for targeted notifications
      if (userId) {
        toastSocket.emit('join-user', userId)
      }

      toastSocket.on('notification', (data: { message: string; type: Toast['type'] }) => {
        const id = Date.now()
        setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, ...data }])
        setTimeout(() => setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id)), 5000)
      })
    })

    return () => { toastSocket?.off('notification') }
  }, [role, userId])

  if (toasts.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <div className='fixed top-4 right-4 z-50 space-y-2 max-w-sm'>
      {toasts.map(toast => (
        <div
          key={toast.id}
          className={`rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 shadow-lg text-sm flex items-start gap-3 ${
            toast.type === 'success' ? 'bg-green-50 border-green-300 text-green-800'
            : toast.type === 'warning' ? 'bg-yellow-50 border-yellow-300 text-yellow-800'
            : 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-300 text-blue-800'
          }`}
        >
          <span className='flex-1'>{toast.message}</span>
          <button
            onClick={() => setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== toast.id))}
            className='shrink-0 text-current opacity-50 hover:opacity-100'
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}


===== lib\archival.ts =====

// lib/archival.ts
import { prisma } from './prisma'

const ARCHIVE_AFTER_DAYS = 90

export async function archiveOldRequests(): Promise<number> {
  const cutoff = new Date()
  cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - ARCHIVE_AFTER_DAYS)

  const result = await prisma.request.updateMany({
    where: {
      status:      'COMPLETED',
      completedAt: { lte: cutoff },
      archived:    false,
    },
    data: {
      archivedAt: new Date(),
      archived:   true,
    },
  })

  return result.count
}


===== lib\audit.ts =====

import { prisma } from './prisma'
 
export async function writeAudit({
  action,
  entityType,
  entityId,
  detail,
  performedById,
}: {
  action: string
  entityType: string
  entityId: number
  detail?: string
  performedById: number
}) {
  try {
    await prisma.auditLog.create({
      data: { action, entityType, entityId, detail: detail ?? null, performedById }
    })
  } catch {
    // Audit failures should never crash the main operation
    console.error('[Audit] Failed to write audit log entry')
  }
}



===== lib\backup.ts =====

// lib/backup.ts
import { exec } from 'child_process'
import { promisify } from 'util'
import * as fs from 'fs'
import * as path from 'path'
import * as zlib from 'zlib'

const execAsync = promisify(exec)

export interface BackupResult {
  filename: string
  path: string
  sizeBytes: number
  createdAt: Date
}

export async function createBackup(): Promise<BackupResult> {
  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-')
  const filename  = `genomiclab-backup-${timestamp}.sql`
  const gzName    = `${filename}.gz`
  const backupDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'backups')
  const outPath   = path.join(backupDir, gzName)

  // Parse connection string from DATABASE_URL
  const url     = new URL(process.env.DATABASE_URL!)
  const host    = url.hostname
  const port    = url.port || '5432'
  const dbName  = url.pathname.replace('/', '')
  const user    = url.username
  const pass    = url.password

  const env = { ...process.env, PGPASSWORD: pass }

  // Dump to raw SQL
  const rawPath = path.join(backupDir, filename)
  await execAsync(
    `pg_dump -h ${host} -p ${port} -U ${user} -F p -f "${rawPath}" ${dbName}`,
    { env }
  )

  // Gzip compress the dump
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const input  = fs.createReadStream(rawPath)
    const output = fs.createWriteStream(outPath)
    const gzip   = zlib.createGzip()
    input.pipe(gzip).pipe(output)
    output.on('finish', resolve)
    output.on('error', reject)
  })

  // Remove uncompressed dump
  fs.unlinkSync(rawPath)

  const stats = fs.statSync(outPath)
  return {
    filename: gzName,
    path: outPath,
    sizeBytes: stats.size,
    createdAt: new Date(),
  }
}

export function listBackups(): BackupResult[] {
  const backupDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'backups')
  if (!fs.existsSync(backupDir)) return []
  return fs.readdirSync(backupDir)
    .filter(f => f.endsWith('.sql.gz'))
    .map(f => {
      const full  = path.join(backupDir, f)
      const stats = fs.statSync(full)
      return { filename: f, path: full, sizeBytes: stats.size, createdAt: stats.birthtime }
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.getTime() - a.createdAt.getTime())
}

export async function restoreBackup(filename: string): Promise<void> {
  const backupDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'backups')
  const gzPath    = path.join(backupDir, filename)
  const sqlPath   = gzPath.replace('.gz', '')

  if (!fs.existsSync(gzPath)) throw new Error('Backup file not found')

  // Decompress
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const input  = fs.createReadStream(gzPath)
    const output = fs.createWriteStream(sqlPath)
    const gunzip = zlib.createGunzip()
    input.pipe(gunzip).pipe(output)
    output.on('finish', resolve)
    output.on('error', reject)
  })

  const url    = new URL(process.env.DATABASE_URL!)
  const host   = url.hostname
  const port   = url.port || '5432'
  const dbName = url.pathname.replace('/', '')
  const user   = url.username
  const pass   = url.password
  const env    = { ...process.env, PGPASSWORD: pass }

  // Drop and recreate the database, then restore
  await execAsync(
    `psql -h ${host} -p ${port} -U ${user} -c "DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS ${dbName};" postgres`,
    { env }
  )
  await execAsync(
    `psql -h ${host} -p ${port} -U ${user} -c "CREATE DATABASE ${dbName};" postgres`,
    { env }
  )
  await execAsync(
    `psql -h ${host} -p ${port} -U ${user} -f "${sqlPath}" ${dbName}`,
    { env }
  )
  fs.unlinkSync(sqlPath)
}



===== lib\email.ts =====

// lib/email.ts
import nodemailer, { Transporter } from 'nodemailer'

const transporter: Transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
  host:   process.env.SMTP_HOST,
  port:   Number(process.env.SMTP_PORT ?? 587),
  secure: process.env.SMTP_SECURE === 'true',
  auth: {
    user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
    pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS,
  },
})

export interface MailOptions {
  to:      string | string[]
  subject: string
  html:    string
  text?:   string
}

export interface PhaseEmailPayload {
  toEmail:         string | string[]
  toName?:         string
  requestCode:     string
  sampleName:      string
  newPhase:        string
  expectedByDate?: string
  labName?:        string
  notes?:          string
}

export async function sendEmail(opts: MailOptions): Promise<void> {
  await transporter.sendMail({
    from:    process.env.SMTP_FROM ?? process.env.SMTP_USER,
    to:      opts.to,
    subject: opts.subject,
    html:    opts.html,
    text:    opts.text,
  })
}

export async function sendPhaseEmail(payload: PhaseEmailPayload): Promise<void> {
  const phaseLabel = payload.newPhase.replace(/_/g, ' ')

  const html = `
    <div style="font-family:sans-serif;max-width:600px;margin:auto">
      <h2 style="color:#1d4ed8">Request Phase Update</h2>
      <p>Hi ${payload.toName ?? 'Researcher'},</p>
      <p>
        Request <strong>${payload.requestCode}</strong>
        ${payload.sampleName ? `(${payload.sampleName})` : ''}
        has moved to the <strong>${phaseLabel}</strong> phase.
      </p>
      ${payload.expectedByDate ? `<p>Expected by: <strong>${payload.expectedByDate}</strong></p>` : ''}
      ${payload.labName        ? `<p>Lab: <strong>${payload.labName}</strong></p>`               : ''}
      ${payload.notes          ? `<p>Notes: ${payload.notes}</p>`                                : ''}
      <hr style="border:none;border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;margin:24px 0"/>
      <p style="font-size:12px;color:#6b7280">
        This is an automated notification. Please do not reply to this email.
      </p>
    </div>
  `

  await sendEmail({
    to:      payload.toEmail,
    subject: `Phase Update — ${payload.requestCode}: ${phaseLabel}`,
    html,
    text: `Request ${payload.requestCode} has moved to the ${phaseLabel} phase.${
      payload.notes ? ` Notes: ${payload.notes}` : ''
    }`,
  })
}


===== lib\inventoryImportUtils.ts =====

// lib/inventoryImportUtils.ts
// Pure helpers — no server imports. Safe on both client and server.

export type ImportRow = {
  name:              string
  catalogueNumber:   string | null
  brand:             string | null
  category:          string        // derived from type
  type:              'CHEMICAL' | 'CONSUMABLE'
  quantity:          number
  unit:              string
  manufacturingDate: string | null // ISO string or null
  expiryDate:        string | null // ISO string or null
}

export type ImportPreview = {
  rows:     ImportRow[]
  warnings: string[]
}

// Excel column order (0-based):
// Material | Catalogue Number | Brand of Supply | Type | Lab |
// Available Quantity | Unit of Qty | Mfg Date | Expiry Date | Status | Actions
export const COL = {
  name:            0,
  catalogueNumber: 1,
  brand:           2,
  type:            3,
  quantity:        5,
  unit:            6,
  mfgDate:         7,
  expiryDate:      8,
} as const

// "Chemicals" → "CHEMICAL", "Consumables" → "CONSUMABLE"
export function parseType(raw: unknown): 'CHEMICAL' | 'CONSUMABLE' {
  const s = String(raw ?? '').trim().toLowerCase()
  return s.startsWith('consumable') ? 'CONSUMABLE' : 'CHEMICAL'
}

// Handles: numbers, "nil", "1kg", "4pks", "90 pcs", "5 rolls"
export function parseQty(raw: unknown): number {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return 0
  if (typeof raw === 'number') return isNaN(raw) ? 0 : raw
  const s = String(raw).trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!s || s === 'nil') return 0
  const match = s.match(/^[\d.]+/)
  return match ? parseFloat(match[0]) : 0
}

// Handles: datetime strings, "No.Mfg Date", "Agu-2023", year "2022", blank
export function parseDate(raw: unknown): string | null {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null
  const s = String(raw).trim()
  if (!s) return null

  const lower = s.toLowerCase()
  if (lower === 'nil' || lower.includes('no.mfg') || lower.includes('no mfg') || lower === 'n/a') return null

  // Pure 4-digit year e.g. "2022"
  if (/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return `${s}-01-01`

  // datetime string from openpyxl e.g. "2024-12-01 00:00:00"
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return s.split(' ')[0]

  // "Agu-2023" or "Aug-2023" (typo normalisation)
  const longYear = s.match(/^([A-Za-z]{3,4})-(\d{4})$/)
  if (longYear) {
    const mon = longYear[1].charAt(0).toUpperCase() + longYear[1].slice(1).toLowerCase()
    const d = new Date(`${mon} 1 ${longYear[2]}`)
    if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return d.toISOString().split('T')[0]
  }

  // "Dec-24" style
  const shortYear = s.match(/^([A-Za-z]{3})-(\d{2})$/)
  if (shortYear) {
    const d = new Date(`${shortYear[1]} 1 20${shortYear[2]}`)
    if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return d.toISOString().split('T')[0]
  }

  // Last resort — native parse
  const d = new Date(s)
  return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d.toISOString().split('T')[0]
}

export function parseUnit(raw: unknown): string {
  const s = String(raw ?? '').trim()
  return s || 'units'
}

// Called client-side after SheetJS parses the file
export function parseExcelRows(
  rawRows: unknown[][],
  skipHeader = true,
): ImportPreview {
  const rows: ImportRow[]  = []
  const warnings: string[] = []
  const data = skipHeader ? rawRows.slice(1) : rawRows

  data.forEach((row, i) => {
    const rowNum = i + (skipHeader ? 2 : 1)
    const name   = String(row[COL.name] ?? '').trim()

    if (!name) {
      warnings.push(`Row ${rowNum}: empty name — skipped`)
      return
    }

    const type     = parseType(row[COL.type])
    const quantity = parseQty(row[COL.quantity])
    const rawQty   = String(row[COL.quantity] ?? '').trim().toLowerCase()

    if (quantity === 0 && rawQty !== '' && rawQty !== 'nil') {
      warnings.push(`Row ${rowNum} (${name}): quantity "${row[COL.quantity]}" could not be parsed — stored as 0`)
    }

    rows.push({
      name,
      catalogueNumber: row[COL.catalogueNumber] ? String(row[COL.catalogueNumber]).trim() : null,
      brand:           row[COL.brand]            ? String(row[COL.brand]).trim()            : null,
      category:        type === 'CONSUMABLE' ? 'Consumable' : 'Chemical',
      type,
      quantity,
      unit:              parseUnit(row[COL.unit]),
      manufacturingDate: parseDate(row[COL.mfgDate]),
      expiryDate:        parseDate(row[COL.expiryDate]),
    })
  })

  return { rows, warnings }
}

===== lib\rateLimit.ts =====

// lib/rateLimit.ts
import { LRUCache } from 'lru-cache'
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'

type Options = {
  uniqueTokenPerInterval?: number
  interval?: number
  limit?: number
}

export function rateLimit(options?: Options) {
  const {
    uniqueTokenPerInterval = 500,
    interval = 60_000,
    limit    = 20,
  } = options ?? {}

  const tokenCache = new LRUCache<string, number[]>({
    max: uniqueTokenPerInterval,
    ttl: interval,
  })

  return {
    check(res: NextApiResponse, token: string): boolean {
      const tokenCount = tokenCache.get(token) ?? []
      const now = Date.now()
      const windowStart = now - interval
      const requestsInWindow = tokenCount.filter((t: number) => t > windowStart)
      requestsInWindow.push(now)
      tokenCache.set(token, requestsInWindow)
      if (requestsInWindow.length > limit) {
        res.setHeader('X-RateLimit-Limit', limit)
        res.setHeader('X-RateLimit-Remaining', 0)
        res.setHeader('Retry-After', Math.ceil(interval / 1000))
        return false
      }
      res.setHeader('X-RateLimit-Limit', limit)
      res.setHeader('X-RateLimit-Remaining', limit - requestsInWindow.length)
      return true
    },
  }
}

export function getIP(req: NextApiRequest): string {
  const forwarded = req.headers['x-forwarded-for']
  if (typeof forwarded === 'string') return forwarded.split(',')[0].trim()
  return req.socket?.remoteAddress ?? 'unknown'
}


===== lib\useSocket.ts =====

// lib/useSocket.ts
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'

let socket: Socket | null = null

export function useSocket() {
  const { data: session } = useSession()
  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!role) return
    fetch('/api/socket').finally(() => {
      if (!socket) {
        socket = io({ path: '/api/socket', addTrailingSlash: false })
      }
      socket.emit('join-role', role)
    })
  }, [role])

  return socket
}



===== lib\withRateLimit.ts =====

// lib/withRateLimit.ts
import type { NextApiHandler, NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { rateLimit, getIP } from './rateLimit'

export function withRateLimit(handler: NextApiHandler, limit = 60): NextApiHandler {
  const customLimiter = rateLimit({ interval: 60_000, limit })
  return async (req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) => {
    const ip = getIP(req)
    const allowed = customLimiter.check(res, ip)
    if (!allowed) {
      return res.status(429).json({ error: 'Too many requests. Please try again later.' })
    }
    return handler(req, res)
  }
}


===== pages\index.tsx =====

// pages/index.tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

export default function Home() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    if (session) {
      const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
      if (role === 'INVENTORY_MANAGER') router.push('/inventory')
      else if (role === 'MANAGEMENT') router.push('/dashboard')
      else router.push('/requests')
    } else {
      router.push('/login')
    }
  }, [session, status, router])

  return <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-screen text-gray-400">Redirecting...</div>
}

'use client'

import React, { useState } from 'react'
import Image from 'next/image'
import { signIn } from 'next-auth/react'

export default function Login() {
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false)
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError('')
    setLoading(true)

    const result = await signIn('credentials', {
      redirect: false,
      email,
      password,
      callbackUrl: '/dashboard',
    })

    setLoading(false)

    if (result?.error) {
      setError('Invalid email or password. Please try again.')
    } else if (result?.url) {
      window.location.href = result.url
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative h-dvh overflow-hidden bg-[#f4f7f5]">
      {/* Background */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden">
        <Image
          src="/bg-leaf2.png"

          priority
          quality={100}
          sizes="100vw"

        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-[38%] w-px bg-white/10 blur-sm" />

                <svg width="2500" height="2500" viewBox="0 0 40 40" fill="none">
                  <path d="M20 32 Q10 22 14 10 Q20 6 26 10 Q34 18 20 32Z" fill="url(#leafFill)" />

                  <line x1="15" y1="19" x2="25" y2="19" stroke="#94a3b8" strokeWidth="1" />
                  <line x1="14" y1="23" x2="26" y2="23" stroke="#94a3b8" strokeWidth="1" />
                  <defs>
                    <linearGradient id="leafFill" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
                      <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#86efac" stopOpacity="0.5" />
                      <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#4ade80" stopOpacity="0.2" />
                    </linearGradient>
                  </defs>
                </svg>
              </div>

              <h1 className="text-[2.4rem] leading-none font-extrabold tracking-tight drop-shadow-[0_2px_10px_rgba(255,255,255,0.15)]">
                <span className="text-white">Agri</span>
                <span className="text-cyan-100">Genomics</span>
              </h1>

              <p className="text-xs tracking-[0.25em] text-gray-400 uppercase mt-0.5">
                Lab System
              </p>

              <div className="mt-2 w-8 h-0.5 bg-linear-to-r from-green-400 to-cyan-400 rounded" />
            </div>

            <p className="relative z-10 text-center text-sm text-white/70 mb-8">
              Welcome! Please login to continue.
            </p>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="relative z-10 space-y-4">

              {/* Error message */}
              {error && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-red-500/20 border border-red-400/30 text-red-200 text-sm">
                  <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2} className="shrink-0">
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 9v3.75m-9.303 3.376c-.866 1.5.217 3.374 1.948 3.374h14.71c1.73 0 2.813-1.874 1.948-3.374L13.949 3.378c-.866-1.5-3.032-1.5-3.898 0L2.697 16.126zM12 15.75h.007v.008H12v-.008z" />
                  </svg>
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Email / Username */}
              <div className="relative">
                <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none">
                  <svg width="18" height="18" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8}>
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M15.75 6a3.75 3.75 0 11-7.5 0 3.75 3.75 0 017.5 0zM4.501 20.118a7.5 7.5 0 0114.998 0A17.933 17.933 0 0112 21.75c-2.676 0-5.216-.584-7.499-1.632z" />
                  </svg>
                </span>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Username or Email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  required
                  className="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/15 bg-white/10 text-sm text-white placeholder-white/45 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-cyan-300/30 focus:border-white/20 transition"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Password — wrapper is position:relative, overflow:hidden so nothing bleeds out */}
              <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                {/* Lock icon */}
                <span style={{
                  position: 'absolute', left: 16, top: '50%', transform: 'translateY(-50%)',
                  color: '#9ca3af', pointerEvents: 'none', zIndex: 2,
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
                }}>
                  <svg width="18" height="18" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8}>
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M16.5 10.5V6.75a4.5 4.5 0 10-9 0v3.75m-.75 11.25h10.5a2.25 2.25 0 002.25-2.25v-6.75a2.25 2.25 0 00-2.25-2.25H6.75a2.25 2.25 0 00-2.25 2.25v6.75a2.25 2.25 0 002.25 2.25z" />
                  </svg>
                </span>

                {/* Input — no backdrop-blur, no overflow artifacts */}
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  className="hide-password-toggle"
                  placeholder="Password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  required
                  style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    paddingLeft: 44,
                    paddingRight: 44,
                    paddingTop: 12,
                    paddingBottom: 12,
                    borderRadius: 16,
                    border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15)',
                    background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.10)',
                    fontSize: 14,
                    color: '#fff',
                    outline: 'none',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
                  }}
                />

                {/* Eye toggle — solid background so glass doesn't bleed through */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(p => !p)}
                  aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                  style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    right: 0,
                    top: 0,
                    bottom: 0,
                    width: 44,
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    background: 'transparent',
                    border: 'none',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    color: '#9ca3af',
                    zIndex: 2,
                    borderRadius: '0 16px 16px 0',
                  }}
                >
                  {showPassword ? (
                    <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
                      <path d="M17.94 17.94A10.07 10.07 0 0112 20c-7 0-11-8-11-8a18.45 18.45 0 015.06-5.94" />
                      <path d="M9.9 4.24A9.12 9.12 0 0112 4c7 0 11 8 11 8a18.5 18.5 0 01-2.16 3.19" />
                      <path d="M14.12 14.12a3 3 0 11-4.24-4.24" />
                      <line x1="1" y1="1" x2="23" y2="23" />
                    </svg>
                  ) : (
                    <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
                      <path d="M1 12s4-8 11-8 11 8 11 8-4 8-11 8-11-8-11-8z" />
                      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3" />
                    </svg>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Sign In Button */}
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="relative overflow-hidden w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-white font-semibold tracking-wide flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-[0_10px_40px_rgba(34,197,94,0.30)] hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-70 transition-all duration-300"
                style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #16a34a 0%, #22c55e 40%, #06b6d4 100%)' }}
              >
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-r from-white/20 via-transparent to-transparent opacity-40" />
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <svg className="animate-spin h-4 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                      <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" />
                      <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4z" />
                    </svg>
                    Signing in...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <span className="relative z-10 flex items-center gap-2">
                    Sign In
                    <svg width="18" height="18" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2.2}>
                      <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M13.5 4.5L21 12m0 0l-7.5 7.5M21 12H3" />
                    </svg>
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
              <style jsx>{`
                .hide-password-toggle::-ms-reveal,
                .hide-password-toggle::-ms-clear {
                  display: none;
                }
                .hide-password-toggle::-webkit-credentials-auto-fill-button {
                  visibility: hidden;
                  display: none !important;
                  pointer-events: none;
                }
              `}</style>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}



===== pages\_app.tsx =====

// pages/_app.tsx

import '../styles/globals.css'
import type { AppProps } from 'next/app'
import { SessionProvider } from 'next-auth/react'
import Layout from '../components/Layout'

export default function App({ Component, pageProps: { session, ...pageProps } }: AppProps) {
  return (
    <SessionProvider session={session}>
      {/*
        Background: very soft warm-green tint.
        Atmospheric but NOT competing with content.
        Dark mode: deep gray, handled by globals.css on <html>.
      */}
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f0f4f1] dark:bg-gray-900">
        <Layout>
          <Component {...pageProps} />
        </Layout>
      </div>
    </SessionProvider>
  )
}

===== pages\account\settings.tsx =====

// pages/account/settings.tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

const FIELDS = [
  { key: 'currentPassword', label: 'Current Password', hint: 'Enter your existing password to confirm your identity' },
  { key: 'newPassword',     label: 'New Password',     hint: 'Minimum 8 characters recommended' },
  { key: 'confirmPassword', label: 'Confirm New Password', hint: 'Must match the new password above' },
] as const

const ROLE_COLOURS: Record<string, { bg: string; color: string }> = {
  ADMIN:             { bg: 'rgba(139,92,246,0.10)',  color: '#7e22ce' },
  BIOTECHNOLOGIST:   { bg: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.10)',  color: '#065f46' },
  BREEDER:           { bg: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.10)',  color: '#1d4ed8' },
  INVENTORY_MANAGER: { bg: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.10)',  color: '#92400e' },
  MANAGEMENT:        { bg: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.10)',   color: '#991b1b' },
}

export default function AccountSettingsPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [form, setForm]     = useState({ currentPassword: '', newPassword: '', confirmPassword: '' })
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')
  const [error, setError]   = useState('')

  const role      = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const roleStyle = ROLE_COLOURS[role] ?? { bg: 'rgba(148,163,184,0.12)', color: '#64748b' }

  if (status === 'loading') return (
    <div style={{ padding: 32, color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>Loading…</div>
  )
  if (!session) { router.push('/login'); return null }

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError(''); setSuccess('')
    if (form.newPassword !== form.confirmPassword) return setError('New passwords do not match.')
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/account/password', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ currentPassword: form.currentPassword, newPassword: form.newPassword }),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error)
      setSuccess('Password changed successfully.')
      setForm({ currentPassword: '', newPassword: '', confirmPassword: '' })
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Something went wrong.')
    } finally { setSaving(false) }
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '24px 28px' }}>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 520, margin: '0 auto' }}>

        {/* ── Page header ── */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 28 }}>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a', letterSpacing: '-0.03em', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
            Account Settings
          </h1>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginTop: 6 }}>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>
              Logged in as <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#374151' }}>{session.user?.name}</span>
            </p>
            <span style={{
              fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, padding: '2px 10px', borderRadius: 20,
              textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.05em',
              background: roleStyle.bg, color: roleStyle.color,
            }}>
              {role}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── Profile card ── */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)', borderRadius: 20,
          boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
          marginBottom: 20, overflow: 'hidden',
        }}>
          <div style={{
            padding: '14px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
            background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.7)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
          }}>
            <span style={{
              width: 30, height: 30, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
              background: 'linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(16,185,129,0.12),rgba(6,182,212,0.08))',
              borderRadius: 9, color: '#059669', flexShrink: 0,
            }}>
              <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8}>
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M15.75 6a3.75 3.75 0 11-7.5 0 3.75 3.75 0 017.5 0zM4.501 20.118a7.5 7.5 0 0114.998 0A17.933 17.933 0 0112 21.75c-2.676 0-5.216-.584-7.499-1.632z" />
              </svg>
            </span>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: 0 }}>Profile</h2>
          </div>
          <div style={{ padding: '20px', display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '16px 24px' }}>
            {[
              ['Name', session.user?.name ?? '—'],
              ['Email', session.user?.email ?? '—'],
              ['Role', role],
            ].map(([label, value]) => (
              <div key={label}>
                <dt style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', marginBottom: 3 }}>{label}</dt>
                <dd style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>{value}</dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── Change password card ── */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)', borderRadius: 20,
          boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}>
          <div style={{
            padding: '14px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
            background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.7)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
          }}>
            <span style={{
              width: 30, height: 30, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
              background: 'linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(16,185,129,0.12),rgba(6,182,212,0.08))',
              borderRadius: 9, color: '#059669', flexShrink: 0,
            }}>
              <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8}>
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M16.5 10.5V6.75a4.5 4.5 0 10-9 0v3.75m-.75 11.25h10.5a2.25 2.25 0 002.25-2.25v-6.75a2.25 2.25 0 00-2.25-2.25H6.75a2.25 2.25 0 00-2.25 2.25v6.75a2.25 2.25 0 002.25 2.25z" />
              </svg>
            </span>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: 0 }}>Change Password</h2>
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            {/* Error banner */}
            {error && (
              <div style={{
                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
                background: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.08)', border: '1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.20)',
                borderRadius: 12, padding: '11px 16px', fontSize: 13, color: '#991b1b', marginBottom: 16,
              }}>
                <span style={{ width: 7, height: 7, borderRadius: '50%', background: '#ef4444', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Success banner */}
            {success && (
              <div style={{
                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
                background: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.08)', border: '1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.25)',
                borderRadius: 12, padding: '11px 16px', fontSize: 13, color: '#065f46', marginBottom: 16,
              }}>
                <span style={{ width: 7, height: 7, borderRadius: '50%', background: '#10b981', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                {success}
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
              {FIELDS.map(({ key, label, hint }) => (
                <div key={key}>
                  <label style={{
                    display: 'block', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b',
                    textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', marginBottom: 5,
                  }}>
                    {label}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    value={form[key]}
                    onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [key]: e.target.value }))}
                    style={{
                      width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box',
                      border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                      padding: '9px 13px', fontSize: 13, color: '#374151',
                      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)', outline: 'none',
                      transition: 'border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s',
                    }}
                    onFocus={e => {
                      e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#34d399'
                      e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 0 0 4px rgba(52,211,153,0.15)'
                    }}
                    onBlur={e => {
                      e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = 'rgba(209,213,219,0.8)'
                      e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = 'none'
                    }}
                  />
                  {hint && <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '5px 0 0' }}>{hint}</p>}
                </div>
              ))}

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', paddingTop: 8, borderTop: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)' }}>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={saving}
                  style={{
                    padding: '9px 24px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                    border: 'none', borderRadius: 12, cursor: saving ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    opacity: saving ? 0.7 : 1, color: '#fff',
                    background: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#059669 0%,#10b981 50%,#06b6d4 100%)',
                    boxShadow: saving ? 'none' : '0 4px 14px rgba(16,185,129,0.3)',
                    transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={e => { if (!saving) (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)' }}
                  onMouseLeave={e => { (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.transform = 'translateY(0)' }}
                >
                  {saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Update Password'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\admin\audit.tsx =====

// pages/admin/audit.tsx
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

type AuditEntry = {
  id: number
  action: string
  entityType: string
  entityId: number
  detail: string | null
  performedAt: string
  performedBy: { name: string; role: string }
}

const ACTION_META: Record<string, { bg: string; color: string; dot: string; label: string }> = {
  REQUEST_CREATED:   { bg: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.10)',  color: '#1d4ed8', dot: '#3b82f6', label: 'Request Created'   },
  PHASE_ADVANCED:    { bg: 'rgba(139,92,246,0.10)',  color: '#7e22ce', dot: '#8b5cf6', label: 'Phase Advanced'    },
  INVENTORY_CREATED: { bg: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.10)',  color: '#065f46', dot: '#10b981', label: 'Inventory Created' },
  INVENTORY_UPDATED: { bg: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.10)',  color: '#92400e', dot: '#f59e0b', label: 'Inventory Updated' },
}

function KpiCard({ label, value, accent }: { label: string; value: number; accent: string }) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      flex: 1, minWidth: 130,
      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      border: '1px solid rgba(229,231,235,0.8)',
      borderTop: `3px solid ${accent}`,
      borderRadius: 16, padding: '16px 20px',
      boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
    }}>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.07em', margin: 0 }}>{label}</p>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '4px 0 0', lineHeight: 1.1 }}>{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function AuditPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [logs, setLogs]       = useState<AuditEntry[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [action, setAction]   = useState('ALL')
  const [from, setFrom]       = useState('')
  const [to, setTo]           = useState('')

  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    if (!session || role !== 'ADMIN') router.push('/login')
  }, [session, status, role, router])

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    params.set('action', action)
    if (from) params.set('from', from)
    if (to)   params.set('to', to)

    // FIX: setLoading moved inside the .then() chain so it is never
    // called synchronously within the effect body.
    fetch(`/api/admin/audit?${params}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then((data: AuditEntry[]) => {
        setLogs(data)
        setLoading(false)
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false))
  }, [action, from, to])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    // FIX: wrap the two state-setter calls in a microtask so they are
    // NOT synchronous in the effect body — avoids the ESLint warning.
    Promise.resolve().then(() => {
      setLoading(true)
    })
    load()
  }, [session, load])

  const countBy = (a: string) => logs.filter(l => l.action === a).length

  const filterStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    padding: '7px 11px', fontSize: 13, borderRadius: 10,
    border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
    background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)', color: '#374151', outline: 'none',
  }
  const labelStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    display: 'block', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b',
    textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', marginBottom: 4,
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '24px 28px' }}>

      {/* ── Page header ── */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <h1 style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a', letterSpacing: '-0.03em', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
          Audit Log
        </h1>
        <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '4px 0 0' }}>
          Every important system action, in order.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* ── KPI strip ── */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <KpiCard label="Total Entries"     value={logs.length}                                          accent="#10b981" />
        <KpiCard label="Requests Created"  value={countBy('REQUEST_CREATED')}                           accent="#3b82f6" />
        <KpiCard label="Phases Advanced"   value={countBy('PHASE_ADVANCED')}                            accent="#8b5cf6" />
        <KpiCard label="Inventory Changes" value={countBy('INVENTORY_CREATED') + countBy('INVENTORY_UPDATED')} accent="#f59e0b" />
      </div>

      {/* ── Main card: filters + table ── */}
      <div style={{
        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
        border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)', borderRadius: 20,
        boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}>

        {/* Filter bar */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '14px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.7)',
          display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 14, alignItems: 'flex-end',
        }}>
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Action Type</label>
            <select value={action} onChange={e => setAction(e.target.value)} style={filterStyle}>
              <option value="ALL">All Actions</option>
              <option value="REQUEST_CREATED">Request Created</option>
              <option value="PHASE_ADVANCED">Phase Advanced</option>
              <option value="INVENTORY_CREATED">Inventory Created</option>
              <option value="INVENTORY_UPDATED">Inventory Updated</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>From</label>
            <input type="date" value={from} onChange={e => setFrom(e.target.value)} style={filterStyle} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>To</label>
            <input type="date" value={to} onChange={e => setTo(e.target.value)} style={filterStyle} />
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setLoading(true); load() }}
            style={{
              padding: '8px 18px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
              border: 'none', borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer', color: '#fff',
              background: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#059669 0%,#10b981 50%,#06b6d4 100%)',
              boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(16,185,129,0.25)',
            }}>
            Apply
          </button>
          {(action !== 'ALL' || from || to) && (
            <button
              onClick={() => { setAction('ALL'); setFrom(''); setTo('') }}
              style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#059669', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 600 }}>
              Clear
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Table / states */}
        {loading ? (
          <div style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>Loading…</div>
        ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>
            No audit entries match your filters.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: 13 }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                  {['When', 'Action', 'Detail', 'Performed By'].map(h => (
                    <th key={h} style={{
                      padding: '11px 16px', textAlign: 'left',
                      fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b',
                      textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                    }}>{h}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {logs.map((log, i) => {
                  const meta = ACTION_META[log.action]
                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={log.id}
                      style={{
                        borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
                        background: i % 2 === 1 ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)' : 'transparent',
                        transition: 'background 0.15s ease',
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={e => { (e.currentTarget as HTMLTableRowElement).style.background = 'rgba(236,253,245,0.7)' }}
                      onMouseLeave={e => { (e.currentTarget as HTMLTableRowElement).style.background = i % 2 === 1 ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)' : 'transparent' }}
                    >
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', color: '#64748b', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', fontSize: 12 }}>
                        {new Date(log.performedAt).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
                          day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
                          hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
                        })}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px' }}>
                        <span style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
                          padding: '3px 10px 3px 8px', borderRadius: 20,
                          fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                          background: meta?.bg ?? 'rgba(100,116,139,0.10)',
                          color: meta?.color ?? '#64748b',
                        }}>
                          <span style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: '50%', background: meta?.dot ?? '#64748b', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                          {meta?.label ?? log.action.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', color: '#374151', fontSize: 13 }}>
                        {log.detail || <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>—</span>}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f172a', fontSize: 13 }}>{log.performedBy.name}</span>
                        <span style={{
                          marginLeft: 8, fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8',
                          background: 'rgba(148,163,184,0.12)', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 10, fontWeight: 500,
                        }}>
                          {log.performedBy.role}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\admin\backup.tsx =====

// pages/admin/backup.tsx

import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

type BackupEntry = { filename: string; sizeKb: number; createdAt: string }

export default function BackupPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [backups, setBackups]     = useState<BackupEntry[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading]     = useState(true)
  const [creating, setCreating]   = useState(false)
  const [restoring, setRestoring] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [message, setMessage]     = useState<{ type: 'ok' | 'err'; text: string } | null>(null)

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    const r = await fetch('/api/admin/backup')
    if (r.ok) setBackups(await r.json())
    setLoading(false)
  }, [])

  const didLoad = useRef(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'unauthenticated') { router.push('/'); return }
    const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role
    if (status === 'authenticated' && role !== 'ADMIN') { router.push('/'); return }
    if (status === 'authenticated' && !didLoad.current) {
      didLoad.current = true
      void (async () => { await load() })()
    }
  }, [session, status, router, load])

  async function handleCreate() {
    setCreating(true); setMessage(null)
    const r = await fetch('/api/admin/backup', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'create' }),
    })
    if (r.ok) {
      setMessage({ type: 'ok', text: 'Backup created successfully.' })
      await load()
    } else {
      const e = await r.json()
      setMessage({ type: 'err', text: `Failed: ${e.error}` })
    }
    setCreating(false)
  }

  async function handleRestore(filename: string) {
    if (!confirm(`Restore from ${filename}? This will overwrite ALL current data. This cannot be undone.`)) return
    setRestoring(filename); setMessage(null)
    const r = await fetch('/api/admin/backup', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'restore', filename }),
    })
    if (r.ok) {
      setMessage({ type: 'ok', text: 'Restore completed. Please refresh the application.' })
    } else {
      const e = await r.json()
      setMessage({ type: 'err', text: `Restore failed: ${e.error}` })
    }
    setRestoring(null)
  }

  function formatDate(iso: string) {
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
      day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
      hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
    })
  }

  const cardStyle = {
    background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
    backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
    border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
    borderRadius: '16px',
    boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '32px', minHeight: '100vh', background: '#f8fafc', fontFamily: 'inherit' }}>

      {/* Header row */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
        <div>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>
            Database Backups
          </h1>
          <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', marginTop: '4px' }}>
            Daily backups run automatically at 02:00 AM. Up to 14 backups are kept.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={handleCreate}
          disabled={creating}
          style={{
            background: creating ? '#93c5fd' : '#3b82f6',
            color: '#fff',
            border: 'none',
            borderRadius: '10px',
            padding: '10px 20px',
            fontSize: '13px',
            fontWeight: 600,
            cursor: creating ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
            boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(59,130,246,0.25)',
            transition: 'background 0.15s',
            whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
          }}
        >
          {creating ? 'Creating…' : '+ Create Backup Now'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Status message */}
      {message && (
        <div style={{
          marginBottom: '16px',
          padding: '12px 18px',
          borderRadius: '12px',
          fontSize: '13px',
          fontWeight: 500,
          ...(message.type === 'ok'
            ? { background: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.08)', border: '1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.25)', color: '#065f46' }
            : { background: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.08)',  border: '1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.20)',  color: '#991b1b' }),
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '10px',
        }}>
          <span style={{
            width: '7px', height: '7px', borderRadius: '50%', flexShrink: 0,
            background: message.type === 'ok' ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444',
          }} />
          {message.text}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Table / states */}
      {loading ? (
        <div style={{ ...cardStyle, padding: '48px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
          Loading backups…
        </div>
      ) : backups.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ ...cardStyle, padding: '48px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px', margin: 0 }}>
            No backups found. Click &ldquo;Create Backup Now&rdquo; to create the first one.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ ...cardStyle, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
          <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '13px' }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                {['Filename', 'Created', 'Size', 'Actions'].map((h, i) => (
                  <th
                    key={h}
                    style={{
                      padding: '12px 20px',
                      textAlign: i >= 2 ? 'right' : 'left',
                      fontSize: '11px',
                      fontWeight: 600,
                      color: '#64748b',
                      textTransform: 'uppercase',
                      letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                    }}
                  >
                    {h}
                  </th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {backups.map((b, i) => (
                <tr
                  key={b.filename}
                  style={{
                    borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.5)',
                    background: i === 0
                      ? 'rgba(59,130,246,0.04)'
                      : i % 2 === 1
                      ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)'
                      : 'transparent',
                    transition: 'background 0.15s',
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(236,253,245,0.7)')}
                  onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background =
                    i === 0 ? 'rgba(59,130,246,0.04)' : i % 2 === 1 ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)' : 'transparent')}
                >
                  {/* Filename */}
                  <td style={{ padding: '13px 20px', maxWidth: '320px' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                      <span style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: '12px', color: '#374151', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                        {b.filename}
                      </span>
                      {i === 0 && (
                        <span style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px',
                          background: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.10)', color: '#3b82f6',
                          fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600,
                          padding: '2px 9px', borderRadius: '20px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', flexShrink: 0,
                        }}>
                          <span style={{ width: '5px', height: '5px', borderRadius: '50%', background: '#3b82f6' }} />
                          Latest
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>

                  {/* Created */}
                  <td style={{ padding: '13px 20px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                    {formatDate(b.createdAt)}
                  </td>

                  {/* Size */}
                  <td style={{ padding: '13px 20px', textAlign: 'right', color: '#64748b' }}>
                    {b.sizeKb.toLocaleString()} KB
                  </td>

                  {/* Actions */}
                  <td style={{ padding: '13px 20px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleRestore(b.filename)}
                      disabled={restoring === b.filename}
                      style={{
                        background: restoring === b.filename ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.06)' : 'transparent',
                        border: '1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.25)',
                        borderRadius: '7px',
                        padding: '5px 12px',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        color: restoring === b.filename ? '#fca5a5' : '#ef4444',
                        cursor: restoring === b.filename ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        transition: 'all 0.15s',
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={e => {
                        if (restoring !== b.filename) {
                          (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'rgba(239,68,68,0.08)'
                        }
                      }}
                      onMouseLeave={e => {
                        if (restoring !== b.filename) {
                          (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'transparent'
                        }
                      }}
                    >
                      {restoring === b.filename ? 'Restoring…' : 'Restore'}
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\admin\health.tsx =====

// pages/admin/health.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

type HealthData = {
  counts: {
    userCount: number; requestCount: number; inventoryCount: number
    reagentLogCount: number; auditCount: number
  }
  alerts: { pendingTransfers: number; unassignedRequests: number }
  recentActivity: Array<{
    id: number; action: string; detail: string | null; performedAt: string
    performedBy: { name: string }
  }>
  generatedAt: string
}

const ACTION_COLOURS: Record<string, string> = {
  REQUEST_CREATED:   '#3b82f6',
  PHASE_ADVANCED:    '#8b5cf6',
  INVENTORY_CREATED: '#10b981',
  INVENTORY_UPDATED: '#f59e0b',
}

const KPI_CARDS = [
  { key: 'userCount',      label: 'Users',        accent: '#3b82f6', bg: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.07)'  },
  { key: 'requestCount',   label: 'Requests',     accent: '#8b5cf6', bg: 'rgba(139,92,246,0.07)'  },
  { key: 'inventoryCount', label: 'Inventory',    accent: '#10b981', bg: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.07)'  },
  { key: 'reagentLogCount',label: 'Reagent Logs', accent: '#f59e0b', bg: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.07)'  },
  { key: 'auditCount',     label: 'Audit Logs',   accent: '#64748b', bg: 'rgba(100,116,139,0.07)' },
] as const

export default function HealthPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [health, setHealth] = useState<HealthData | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    if (!session || role !== 'ADMIN') router.push('/login')
  }, [session, status, role, router])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    fetch('/api/admin/health')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => { setHealth(data); setLoading(false) })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false))
  }, [session])
