  if (loading) return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: '64px', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
      Loading system health…
    </div>
  )
  if (!health) return null

  const hasAlerts = health.alerts.pendingTransfers > 0 || health.alerts.unassignedRequests > 0

  const cardStyle = {
    background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
    backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
    border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
    borderRadius: '16px',
    boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '32px', minHeight: '100vh', background: '#f8fafc', fontFamily: 'inherit' }}>

      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: '28px' }}>
        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>
          System Health
        </h1>
        <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8', marginTop: '4px', fontWeight: 500, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em' }}>
          Generated {new Date(health.generatedAt).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
            day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
          })}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Alert banners */}
      {hasAlerts && (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '10px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
          {health.alerts.unassignedRequests > 0 && (
            <div style={{
              background: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.08)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.25)',
              borderRadius: '12px',
              padding: '12px 18px',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: '10px',
              fontSize: '13px',
              color: '#92400e',
            }}>
              <span style={{
                width: '8px', height: '8px', borderRadius: '50%',
                background: '#f59e0b', flexShrink: 0,
              }} />
              <span>
                <strong>{health.alerts.unassignedRequests}</strong> active request(s) have no lab assigned.
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          {health.alerts.pendingTransfers > 0 && (
            <div style={{
              background: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.08)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(59,130,246,0.20)',
              borderRadius: '12px',
              padding: '12px 18px',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: '10px',
              fontSize: '13px',
              color: '#1e3a5f',
            }}>
              <span style={{
                width: '8px', height: '8px', borderRadius: '50%',
                background: '#3b82f6', flexShrink: 0,
              }} />
              <span>
                <strong>{health.alerts.pendingTransfers}</strong> transfer(s) awaiting approval.
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* KPI cards */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(5, 1fr)', gap: '14px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
        {KPI_CARDS.map(card => (
          <div key={card.key} style={{
            ...cardStyle,
            padding: '20px 16px',
            textAlign: 'center',
            background: card.bg,
            border: `1px solid ${card.accent}22`,
          }}>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '28px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1 }}>
              {health.counts[card.key].toLocaleString()}
            </p>
            <p style={{
              fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, color: card.accent,
              marginTop: '8px', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
            }}>
              {card.label}
            </p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Recent activity */}
      <div style={{ ...cardStyle, padding: '24px' }}>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 16px', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em' }}>
          Recent Activity
        </h2>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '0' }}>
          {health.recentActivity.map((entry, i) => {
            const dot = ACTION_COLOURS[entry.action] ?? '#94a3b8'
            return (
              <div
                key={entry.id}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'flex-start',
                  gap: '14px',
                  padding: '11px 0',
                  borderBottom: i < health.recentActivity.length - 1 ? '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.5)' : 'none',
                }}
              >
                {/* Dot */}
                <span style={{
                  width: '7px', height: '7px', borderRadius: '50%',
                  background: dot, flexShrink: 0, marginTop: '3px',
                }} />

                {/* Timestamp */}
                <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', flexShrink: 0, marginTop: '1px', minWidth: '110px' }}>
                  {new Date(entry.performedAt).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
                    day: '2-digit', month: 'short',
                    hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
                  })}
                </span>

                {/* Action tag */}
                <span style={{
                  fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, color: dot,
                  background: `${dot}14`, borderRadius: '6px',
                  padding: '2px 8px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', flexShrink: 0,
                }}>
                  {entry.action.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                </span>

                {/* Detail */}
                <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#374151', flex: 1 }}>
                  {entry.detail}
                </span>

                {/* Performer */}
                <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                  {entry.performedBy.name}
                </span>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\admin\import.tsx =====

import { useState, useRef } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx'

type PreviewRow = Record<string, unknown>

type ImportResult = {
  imported: number
  total: number
  skipped: number
  skippedDetails: string[]
  errors: string[]
  message: string
}

export default function ImportPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)

  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const canImportRequests = role === 'ADMIN'

  const [sheetType, setSheetType] = useState<'requests' | 'inventory'>(
    canImportRequests ? 'requests' : 'inventory'
  )
  const [labId, setLabId] = useState('1')
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<PreviewRow[]>([])
  const [columns, setColumns] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState('')
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false)
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ImportResult | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  if (status === 'loading') {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-64">
        <p className="text-gray-400">Loading...</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (status === 'unauthenticated') {
    router.push('/login')
    return null
  }

  if (role !== 'ADMIN' && role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER') {
    return (
      <div className="p-8 max-w-lg">
        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
          <p className="text-red-700 font-medium">Access Denied</p>
          <p className="text-red-600 text-sm mt-1">This page requires the ADMIN or INVENTORY_MANAGER role.</p>
          <button onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}
            className="mt-3 text-sm text-blue-600 hover:underline">
            ← Back to dashboard
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  function handleFileChange(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
    if (!file) return
    setFileName(file.name)
    setResult(null)
    setError('')
    const reader = new FileReader()
    reader.onload = (evt) => {
      const data = evt.target?.result
      const workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: 'binary' })
      const sheet = workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]]
      const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { defval: '' }) as PreviewRow[]
      if (rows.length > 0) {
        setColumns(Object.keys(rows[0]))
        setPreview(rows.slice(0, 5))
      }
    }
    reader.readAsBinaryString(file)
  }

  async function handleImport() {
    const file = fileRef.current?.files?.[0]
    if (!file) return setError('Please select a file first')
    setImporting(true)
    setResult(null)
    setError('')
    const formData = new FormData()
    formData.append('excel', file)
    formData.append('sheetType', sheetType)
    formData.append('labId', labId)
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/import/excel', { method: 'POST', body: formData })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) setError(data.error || 'Import failed')
      else setResult(data)
    } catch {
      setError('Network error during import')
    } finally {
      setImporting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto p-8 space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">Import Excel Data</h1>

      {/* Step 1: Configure */}
      <div className="bg-white border rounded-lg p-6 space-y-4">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">1. Configure Import</h2>
        <div className="flex gap-6 flex-wrap">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Data Type</label>
            {canImportRequests ? (
              /* ADMIN sees both options */
              <select
                value={sheetType}
                onChange={e => setSheetType(e.target.value as 'requests' | 'inventory')}
                className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
              >
                <option value="requests">Requests</option>
                <option value="inventory">Inventory Items</option>
              </select>
            ) : (
              /* INVENTORY_MANAGER sees only inventory, no dropdown */
              <div className="border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
                Inventory Items
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          {sheetType === 'inventory' && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Lab</label>
              <select value={labId} onChange={e => setLabId(e.target.value)}
                className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500">
                <option value="1">Lab 1</option>
                <option value="2">Lab 2</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Step 2: File */}
      <div className="bg-white border rounded-lg p-6 space-y-4">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">2. Select Excel File</h2>
        <input
          ref={fileRef}
          type="file"
          accept=".xlsx,.xls"
          onChange={handleFileChange}
          className="block text-sm text-gray-600 file:mr-4 file:py-2 file:px-4 file:rounded file:border-0 file:text-sm file:font-medium file:bg-blue-50 file:text-blue-700 hover:file:bg-blue-100"
        />
        {fileName && <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Selected: <span className="font-medium">{fileName}</span></p>}
        {columns.length > 0 && (
          <div className="text-sm text-gray-600 bg-gray-50 rounded p-3">
            <p className="font-medium mb-1">Columns detected in your file:</p>
            <p className="font-mono text-xs text-gray-500 break-all">{columns.join(', ')}</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Step 3: Preview */}
      {preview.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white border rounded-lg p-6 space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">3. Preview (first {preview.length} rows)</h2>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-xs border border-gray-200 rounded">
              <thead className="bg-gray-100">
                <tr>
                  {columns.map(col => (
                    <th key={col} className="px-2 py-2 text-left border-r font-medium text-gray-600 whitespace-nowrap">{col}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {preview.map((row, i) => (
                  <tr key={i} className="border-t hover:bg-gray-50">
                    {columns.map(col => (
                      <td key={col} className="px-2 py-2 border-r text-gray-700 max-w-32 truncate">{String(row[col] ?? '')}</td>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Step 4: Confirm */}
      {preview.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white border rounded-lg p-6 space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">4. Confirm Import</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
            Rows with missing values will use sensible defaults.
          </p>
          <button onClick={handleImport} disabled={importing}
            className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-6 py-2 rounded font-medium hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
            {importing ? 'Importing...' : 'Import Now'}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Result */}
      {result && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-lg p-4">
            <p className="font-semibold text-green-800 text-lg">✓ {result.imported} of {result.total} rows imported</p>
            <p className="text-green-700 text-sm mt-1">{result.message}</p>
          </div>
          {result.skipped > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200 rounded-lg p-4">
              <p className="font-medium text-yellow-800">{result.skipped} rows skipped (duplicates)</p>
              {result.skippedDetails?.length > 0 && (
                <ul className="text-xs text-yellow-700 mt-2 space-y-1 list-disc list-inside">
                  {result.skippedDetails.map((s, i) => <li key={i}>{s}</li>)}
                </ul>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
          {result.errors.length > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
              <p className="font-medium text-red-800">{result.errors.length} rows had errors:</p>
              <ul className="text-xs text-red-600 mt-2 space-y-1 list-disc list-inside">
                {result.errors.map((e, i) => <li key={i}>{e}</li>)}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
          <p className="text-red-700 text-sm">{error}</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\admin\users.tsx =====

// pages/admin/users.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

type User = {
  id: number
  name: string
  email: string
  role: string
}

const ROLES = ['BREEDER', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER', 'MANAGEMENT', 'ADMIN']

const ROLE_STYLES: Record<string, { bg: string; color: string; dot: string }> = {
  ADMIN:             { bg: '#fef2f2', color: '#991b1b', dot: '#f87171' },
  MANAGEMENT:        { bg: '#faf5ff', color: '#7e22ce', dot: '#c084fc' },
  BIOTECHNOLOGIST:   { bg: '#eff6ff', color: '#1d4ed8', dot: '#60a5fa' },
  INVENTORY_MANAGER: { bg: '#f0fdf4', color: '#15803d', dot: '#22c55e' },
  BREEDER:           { bg: '#fefce8', color: '#854d0e', dot: '#fbbf24' },
}

// ── Style helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const labelStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  display: 'block',
  fontSize: 11,
  fontWeight: 700,
  color: '#64748b',
  textTransform: 'uppercase',
  letterSpacing: '0.06em',
  marginBottom: 4,
}

const inputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: '100%',
  border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
  borderRadius: 8,
  padding: '8px 12px',
  fontSize: 13,
  color: '#374151',
  outline: 'none',
  background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
  boxSizing: 'border-box',
}

// ── KPI Card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function KpiCard({ label, value, sub, accent }: { label: string; value: number | string; sub?: string; accent: string }) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      flex: 1,
      minWidth: 140,
      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      border: '1px solid rgba(229,231,235,0.8)',
      borderRadius: 16,
      padding: '18px 22px',
      boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4,
      borderTop: `3px solid ${accent}`,
    }}>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.07em', margin: 0 }}>
        {label}
      </p>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>{value}</p>
      {sub && <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>{sub}</p>}
    </div>
  )
}

// ── Role Badge ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function RoleBadge({ role }: { role: string }) {
  const s = ROLE_STYLES[role] ?? { bg: '#f1f5f9', color: '#475569', dot: '#94a3b8' }
  return (
    <span style={{
      display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5,
      padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
      background: s.bg, color: s.color, letterSpacing: '0.03em',
      whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
    }}>
      <span style={{ width: 5, height: 5, borderRadius: '50%', background: s.dot, display: 'inline-block' }} />
      {role}
    </span>
  )
}

// ── Shared table components ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
const TH = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => (
  <th style={{
    padding: '11px 16px',
    textAlign: 'left',
    fontWeight: 700,
    fontSize: 11,
    color: '#64748b',
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
    letterSpacing: '0.06em',
    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
  }}>
    {children}
  </th>
)

const TD = ({ children, right }: { children: React.ReactNode; right?: boolean }) => (
  <td style={{
    padding: '12px 16px',
    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
    fontSize: 13,
    color: '#374151',
    textAlign: right ? 'right' : 'left',
  }}>
    {children}
  </td>
)

// ── Main Page ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export default function UsersPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [users, setUsers]       = useState<User[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading]   = useState(true)
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)

  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: '', email: '', password: '', userRole: 'BREEDER' })
  const [saving, setSaving]       = useState(false)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('')

  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    if (!session || role !== 'ADMIN') router.push('/login')
  }, [session, status, role, router])

  function load() {
    fetch('/api/admin/users')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => { setUsers(data); setLoading(false) })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false))
  }

  useEffect(() => { if (session) load() }, [session])

  async function handleCreate(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSaving(true)
    setFormError('')
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/users', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(form),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error)
      setShowForm(false)
      setForm({ name: '', email: '', password: '', userRole: 'BREEDER' })
      load()
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      setFormError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to create user')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleRoleChange(userId: number, newRole: string) {
    await fetch(`/api/admin/users/${userId}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ userRole: newRole }),
    })
    load()
  }

  async function handleDelete(userId: number, name: string) {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete user "${name}"? This cannot be undone.`)) return
    await fetch(`/api/admin/users/${userId}`, { method: 'DELETE' })
    load()
  }

  // ── Role counts for KPIs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const adminCount  = users.filter(u => u.role === 'ADMIN').length
  const breederCount = users.filter(u => u.role === 'BREEDER').length

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div style={{ padding: 32, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>
        <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
          style={{ animation: 'spin 1s linear infinite' }}>
          <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.3} />
          <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 00-9-9" strokeLinecap="round" />
        </svg>
        Loading users…
        <style>{`@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }`}</style>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '24px 28px', maxWidth: '100%' }}>

      {/* ── KPI Strip ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <KpiCard label="Total Users"  value={users.length} sub="All registered accounts" accent="#10b981" />
        <KpiCard label="Admins"       value={adminCount}   sub="Full system access"      accent="#ef4444" />
        <KpiCard label="Breeders"     value={breederCount} sub="Request submitters"      accent="#f59e0b" />
        <KpiCard
          label="Other Roles"
          value={users.length - adminCount - breederCount}
          sub="Lab & management staff"
          accent="#8b5cf6"
        />
      </div>

      {/* ── Page header ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start',
        justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 20,
        flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12,
      }}>
        <div>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>User Management</p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '4px 0 0' }}>
            {users.length} registered account{users.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowForm(!showForm)}
          style={{
            padding: '8px 18px',
            background: showForm ? 'rgba(241,245,249,0.9)' : '#10b981',
            color: showForm ? '#374151' : '#fff',
            border: showForm ? '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)' : 'none',
            borderRadius: 10,
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 600,
            cursor: 'pointer',
            transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
          }}
          onMouseEnter={e => {
            if (!showForm) (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = '#059669'
          }}
          onMouseLeave={e => {
            if (!showForm) (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = '#10b981'
          }}
        >
          {showForm ? 'Cancel' : '+ Add User'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ── Create User Form ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      {showForm && (
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(191,219,254,0.8)',
          borderRadius: 20,
          boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
          marginBottom: 20,
        }}>
          <div style={{
            padding: '14px 20px',
            background: 'rgba(239,246,255,0.7)',
            borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(191,219,254,0.6)',
          }}>
            <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e40af' }}>Add New User</p>
          </div>
          <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            {formError && (
              <div style={{
                background: '#fef2f2',
                border: '1px solid rgba(254,202,202,0.8)',
                borderRadius: 10,
                padding: '10px 14px',
                marginBottom: 16,
                fontSize: 13,
                color: '#991b1b',
              }}>
                {formError}
              </div>
            )}
            <form onSubmit={handleCreate}>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 14, marginBottom: 16 }}>
                <div>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Full Name</label>
                  <input
                    type="text" required value={form.name}
                    onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, name: e.target.value }))}
                    placeholder="e.g. Priya Sharma"
                    style={inputStyle}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Email</label>
                  <input
                    type="email" required value={form.email}
                    onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, email: e.target.value }))}
                    placeholder="user@example.com"
                    style={inputStyle}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Password</label>
                  <input
                    type="password" required value={form.password}
                    onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, password: e.target.value }))}
                    placeholder="Min. 8 characters"
                    style={inputStyle}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Role</label>
                  <select
                    value={form.userRole}
                    onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, userRole: e.target.value }))}
                    style={inputStyle}
                  >
                    {ROLES.map(r => <option key={r} value={r}>{r}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10 }}>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowForm(false)} style={{
                  padding: '7px 18px', fontSize: 13,
                  border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                  background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151',
                  cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 500,
                }}>
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button type="submit" disabled={saving} style={{
                  padding: '7px 20px', fontSize: 13,
                  background: saving ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.5)' : '#10b981',
                  color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 10,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  cursor: saving ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                  opacity: saving ? 0.7 : 1,
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                }}>
                  {saving && (
                    <svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
                      style={{ animation: 'spin 1s linear infinite' }}>
                      <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.3} />
                      <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 00-9-9" strokeLinecap="round" />
                    </svg>
                  )}
                  {saving ? 'Creating…' : 'Create User'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Users Table ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div style={{
        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
        WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
        border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
        borderRadius: 20,
        boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}>

        {/* Table header bar */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '14px 20px',
          borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.7)',
        }}>
          <p style={{
            margin: 0, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b',
            textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
          }}>
            All Users
          </p>
        </div>

        <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
          <table style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: 13, borderCollapse: 'collapse' }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                <TH>Name</TH>
                <TH>Email</TH>
                <TH>Current Role</TH>
                <TH>Change Role</TH>
                <TH>Actions</TH>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {users.map((u, i) => (
                <tr
                  key={u.id}
                  style={{
                    borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
                    background: i % 2 === 0 ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)',
                    transition: 'background 0.15s ease',
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={e => {
                    (e.currentTarget as HTMLTableRowElement).style.background = 'rgba(236,253,245,0.7)'
                  }}
                  onMouseLeave={e => {
                    (e.currentTarget as HTMLTableRowElement).style.background =
                      i % 2 === 0 ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)'
                  }}
                >
                  <TD>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                      {/* Avatar initials */}
                      <div style={{
                        width: 30, height: 30, borderRadius: '50%',
                        background: ROLE_STYLES[u.role]?.bg ?? '#f1f5f9',
                        border: `1px solid ${ROLE_STYLES[u.role]?.dot ?? '#94a3b8'}30`,
                        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                        fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
                        color: ROLE_STYLES[u.role]?.color ?? '#475569',
                        flexShrink: 0,
                      }}>
                        {u.name.split(' ').map(n => n[0]).join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
                      </div>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f172a' }}>{u.name}</span>
                    </div>
                  </TD>
                  <TD>
                    <span style={{ color: '#64748b' }}>{u.email}</span>
                  </TD>
                  <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                    <RoleBadge role={u.role} />
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                    <select
                      value={u.role}
                      onChange={e => handleRoleChange(u.id, e.target.value)}
                      style={{
                        border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
                        borderRadius: 7,
                        padding: '5px 9px',
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: '#374151',
                        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
                        outline: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                      }}
                    >
                      {ROLES.map(r => <option key={r} value={r}>{r}</option>)}
                    </select>
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleDelete(u.id, u.name)}
                        style={{
                          fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                          padding: '4px 12px',
                          border: '1px solid rgba(254,202,202,0.8)',
                          borderRadius: 8,
                          color: '#dc2626',
                          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={e => {
                          (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)'
                        }}
                        onMouseLeave={e => {
                          (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)'
                        }}
                      >
                        Delete
                      </button>
                      <a
                        href={`/admin/user-activity/${u.id}`}
                        style={{
                          fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                          padding: '4px 12px',
                          border: '1px solid rgba(191,219,254,0.8)',
                          borderRadius: 8,
                          color: '#1d4ed8',
                          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)',
                          textDecoration: 'none',
                          transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                          display: 'inline-block',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={e => {
                          (e.currentTarget as HTMLAnchorElement).style.background = 'rgba(239,246,255,0.9)'
                        }}
                        onMouseLeave={e => {
                          (e.currentTarget as HTMLAnchorElement).style.background = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)'
                        }}
                      >
                        Activity
                      </a>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
              {users.length === 0 && (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={5} style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>
                    No users found.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '12px 20px',
          borderTop: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.5)',
        }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>
            {users.length} user{users.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} registered
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <style>{`@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }`}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\admin\user-activity\[userId].tsx =====

// pages/admin/user-activity/[userId].tsx
// FIX: removed manual <Layout> import and wrapper — pages/_app.tsx already
// wraps every page in <Layout>, so this page was rendering a double navbar,
// double ToastNotification, and double ambient background blobs.
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

type AuditEntry = {
  id: number; action: string; entity: string; entityId: string | null
  detail: string | null; createdAt: string
}
type UserInfo = { id: number; name: string; email: string; role: string }

const PAGE_SIZE = 25

export default function UserActivityPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const userId = router.query.userId as string

  const [user,    setUser]    = useState<UserInfo | null>(null)
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<AuditEntry[]>([])
  const [total,   setTotal]   = useState(0)
  const [page,    setPage]    = useState(1)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'unauthenticated') {
      router.push('/')
      return
    }
    const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role
    if (status === 'authenticated' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
      router.push('/')
    }
  }, [session, status, router])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!userId) return

    const controller = new AbortController()

    async function fetchActivity() {
      setLoading(true)
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `/api/admin/user-activity?userId=${userId}&page=${page}&limit=${PAGE_SIZE}`,
          { signal: controller.signal }
        )
        const json = await res.json()
        setUser(json.user)
        setEntries(json.entries)
        setTotal(json.total)
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name !== 'AbortError') console.error(err)
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    fetchActivity()
    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [userId, page])

  function formatDate(iso: string) {
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
      day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
      hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
    })
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      <button onClick={() => router.back()} className="text-sm text-blue-600 hover:underline mb-4 block">
        ← Back to Users
      </button>

      {user && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700
                       rounded-lg p-5 mb-6">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">{user.name}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{user.email} · {user.role}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mt-1">
            {total} total audit entries
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Loading activity...</p>
      ) : entries.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">No audit entries found for this user.</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-lg">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Timestamp</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Action</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Entity</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Detail</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-700">
                {entries.map(e => (
                  <tr key={e.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-750">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap text-xs">
                      {formatDate(e.createdAt)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <span className="inline-block bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900 text-blue-700
                                      dark:text-blue-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                        {e.action}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 text-xs">
                      {e.entity}{e.entityId ? ` #${e.entityId}` : ''}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 text-xs max-w-xs truncate">
                      {e.detail ?? '—'}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-4">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              {Math.min((page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1, total)}–{Math.min(page * PAGE_SIZE, total)} of {total}
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                disabled={page <= 1}
                onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-sm border rounded-lg disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                Prev
              </button>
              <button
                disabled={page * PAGE_SIZE >= total}
                onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-sm border rounded-lg disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                Next
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\api\socket.ts =====

// pages/api/socket.ts

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { Server } from 'socket.io'
import type { Server as HTTPServer } from 'http'
import type { Socket as NetSocket } from 'net'

interface SocketServer extends HTTPServer {
  io?: InstanceType<typeof Server>
}
interface SocketWithIO extends NetSocket {
  server: SocketServer
}
interface ResponseWithSocket extends NextApiResponse {
  socket: SocketWithIO
}

declare global {
  var io: InstanceType<typeof Server> | undefined
}

export const config = { api: { bodyParser: false } }

export default function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: ResponseWithSocket) {
  if (!res.socket.server.io) {
    const io = new Server(res.socket.server, {
      path:             '/api/socket',
      addTrailingSlash: false,
    })
    res.socket.server.io = io
    global.io = io

    io.on('connection', socket => {
      // Join role-based room for broadcast notifications (e.g. 'ADMIN', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST')
      socket.on('join-role', (role: string) => {
        socket.join(role)
      })


      socket.on('join-user', (userId: string) => {
        socket.join(`user_${userId}`)
      })
    })
  }
  res.end()
}


===== pages\api\account\password.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs'
 
export default async function handler(
  req: NextApiRequest,
  res: NextApiResponse
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
 
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return res.status(405).end()
 
  const user = session.user as { id: string }
  const { currentPassword, newPassword } = req.body
 
  if (!currentPassword || !newPassword) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Both fields are required' })
  }
 
  if (newPassword.length < 8) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: 'New password must be at least 8 characters'
    })
  }
 
  const dbUser = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: parseInt(user.id) },
    select: { password: true }
  })
 
  if (!dbUser) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' })
 
  const valid = await bcrypt.compare(currentPassword, dbUser.password)
  if (!valid) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Current password is incorrect' })
  }
 
  const hashed = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, 10)
  await prisma.user.update({
    where: { id: parseInt(user.id) },
    data: { password: hashed }
  })
 
  return res.status(200).json({ message: 'Password updated successfully' })
}



===== pages\api\admin\audit.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(
  req: NextApiRequest,
  res: NextApiResponse
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Admin only' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).end()

  const { action, from, to } = req.query
  const where: Record<string, unknown> = {}

  if (action && action !== 'ALL') where.action = action
  if (from || to) {
    where.performedAt = {
      ...(from ? { gte: new Date(from as string) } : {}),
      ...(to   ? { lte: new Date(to   as string) } : {}),
    }
  }

  const logs = await prisma.auditLog.findMany({
    where,
    include: {
      performedBy: { select: { name: true, role: true } }
    },
    orderBy: { performedAt: 'desc' },
    take: 300,
  })

  return res.status(200).json(logs)
}


===== pages\api\admin\backup.ts =====

// pages/api/admin/backup.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { createBackup, listBackups, restoreBackup } from '../../../lib/backup'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'ADMIN') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })

  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    // List all backups
    const backups = listBackups().map(b => ({
      filename: b.filename,
      sizeKb: parseFloat((b.sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(1)),
      createdAt: b.createdAt.toISOString(),
    }))
    return res.status(200).json(backups)
  }

  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    const { action, filename } = req.body

    if (action === 'create') {
      try {
        const result = await createBackup()
        return res.status(200).json({
          filename: result.filename,
          sizeKb: parseFloat((result.sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(1)),
          createdAt: result.createdAt.toISOString(),
        })
      } catch (err) {
        return res.status(500).json({ error: String(err) })
      }
    }

    if (action === 'restore') {
      if (!filename || typeof filename !== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'filename required' })
      }
      // Sanitise: only allow simple filenames with no path traversal
      if (!/^genomiclab-backup-[\w\-]+\.sql\.gz$/.test(filename)) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid filename' })
      }
      try {
        await restoreBackup(filename)
        return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
      } catch (err) {
        return res.status(500).json({ error: String(err) })
      }
    }

    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unknown action' })
  }

  return res.status(405).end()
}



===== pages\api\admin\health.ts =====

// pages/api/admin/health.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== "ADMIN") return res.status(403).json({ error: "Admin only" })
  if (req.method !== "GET") return res.status(405).end()

  const [
    userCount,
    requestCount,
    inventoryCount,
    reagentLogCount,
    auditCount,
    pendingTransfers,
    unassignedRequests,
    recentActivity
  ] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.user.count(),
    prisma.request.count(),
    prisma.inventoryItem.count(),
    prisma.reagentLog.count(),
    prisma.auditLog.count(),
    prisma.inventoryTransfer.count({ where: { status: "PENDING" } }),
    prisma.request.count({
      where: { assignedLabId: null, status: { not: "COMPLETED" } }
    }),
    prisma.auditLog.findMany({
      orderBy: { performedAt: "desc" },
      take: 5,
      include: { performedBy: { select: { name: true } } }
    })
  ])

  return res.status(200).json({
    counts: { userCount, requestCount, inventoryCount, reagentLogCount, auditCount },
    alerts: { pendingTransfers, unassignedRequests },
    recentActivity,
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  })
}



===== pages\api\admin\user-activity.ts =====

// pages/api/admin/user-activity.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'ADMIN') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).end()

  const userId = parseInt(req.query.userId as string)
  const page   = Math.max(1, parseInt(req.query.page  as string) || 1)
  const limit  = Math.min(100, parseInt(req.query.limit as string) || 25)
  const skip   = (page - 1) * limit

  if (isNaN(userId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'userId required' })

  const [user, entries, total] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.user.findUnique({
      where:  { id: userId },
      select: { id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true},
    }),
    prisma.auditLog.findMany({
      where:   { performedById: userId },
      // Use the field name that Prisma actually generated.
      // If your schema column is `performedAt` change both here and below.
      orderBy: { performedAt: 'desc' },
      skip,
      take: limit,
    }),
    prisma.auditLog.count({ where: { performedById: userId } }),
  ])

  if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' })

  return res.status(200).json({ user, entries, total, page, limit })
}


===== pages\api\admin\users\index.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs'

export default async function handler(
  req: NextApiRequest,
  res: NextApiResponse
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'ADMIN') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Admin only' })

  // GET — list all users
  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    const users = await prisma.user.findMany({
      select: {
        id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true
      },
      orderBy: { name: 'asc' }
    })
    return res.status(200).json(users)
  }

  // POST — create a new user
  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    const { name, email, password, userRole } = req.body

    if (!name || !email || !password || !userRole) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'All fields are required' })
    }

    const existing = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } })
    if (existing) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A user with that email already exists' })
    }

    const hashed = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10)

    const user = await prisma.user.create({
      data: { name, email, password: hashed, role: userRole },
      select: { id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true }
    })

    return res.status(201).json(user)
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}


===== pages\api\admin\users\[id].ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'
 
export default async function handler(
  req: NextApiRequest,
  res: NextApiResponse
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
 
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'ADMIN') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Admin only' })
 
  const id = parseInt(req.query.id as string)
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid ID' })
 
  if (req.method === 'PATCH') {
    const { userRole } = req.body
    if (!userRole) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'userRole is required' })
 
    const updated = await prisma.user.update({
      where: { id },
      data: { role: userRole },
      select: { id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true }
    })
    return res.status(200).json(updated)
  }
 
  if (req.method === 'DELETE') {
    const me = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id
    if (parseInt(String(me)) === id) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'You cannot delete your own account' })
    }
    await prisma.user.delete({ where: { id } })
    return res.status(204).end()
  }
 
  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}



===== pages\api\approvals\capacity.ts =====

// pages/api/approvals/capacity.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../lib/audit'

const SERVICE_TYPES = ['DNA_ISOLATION', 'GENOTYPING'] as const
type ServiceType = typeof SERVICE_TYPES[number]

const DEFAULT_CAPACITIES: Record<ServiceType, number> = {
  DNA_ISOLATION: 15000,
  GENOTYPING:    10000,
}

// Both capacities are measured in SAMPLES — the number of physical samples
// the lab can process in a week. DNA_ISOLATION takes one sample through
// extraction; GENOTYPING takes one extracted sample through marker analysis.
// FIX: the API previously labelled genotyping as "data points" (samples × markers)
// while the modal label said "samples". Both are now "samples" throughout.

function getWeekStart(date: Date): Date {
  const d = new Date(date)
  const day = d.getDay()
  const diff = d.getDate() - day + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1)
  d.setDate(diff)
  d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
  return d
}

async function ensureCurrentWeekCapacity(weekStart: Date) {
  const ws = weekStart.toISOString()
  for (const serviceType of SERVICE_TYPES) {
    const existing = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ id: number }>>`
      SELECT id FROM "CapacityTracking"
      WHERE "weekStart" = ${ws}::timestamp AND "serviceType" = ${serviceType}
      LIMIT 1
    `
    if (!existing || existing.length === 0) {
      const prev = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ capacity: number }>>`
        SELECT capacity FROM "CapacityTracking"
        WHERE "serviceType" = ${serviceType}
        ORDER BY "weekStart" DESC
        LIMIT 1
      `
      const capacity = prev?.[0]?.capacity ?? DEFAULT_CAPACITIES[serviceType as ServiceType]
      await prisma.$executeRaw`
        INSERT INTO "CapacityTracking" ("weekStart", "serviceType", "capacity", "createdAt", "updatedAt")
        VALUES (${ws}::timestamp, ${serviceType}, ${capacity}, NOW(), NOW())
        ON CONFLICT ("weekStart", "serviceType") DO NOTHING
      `
    }
  }
}

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role   = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const userId = parseInt((session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string)

  const allowed = ['ADMIN', 'MANAGEMENT', 'BREEDER', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST']
  if (!allowed.includes(role)) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })

  const now       = new Date()
  const weekStart = getWeekStart(now)
  const weekEnd   = new Date(weekStart)
  weekEnd.setDate(weekEnd.getDate() + 7)

  // ── GET ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    await ensureCurrentWeekCapacity(weekStart)

    const capacityRows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{
      serviceType: string; capacity: number
    }>>`
      SELECT "serviceType", "capacity"
      FROM "CapacityTracking"
      WHERE "weekStart" = ${weekStart.toISOString()}::timestamp
    `

    // DNA_ISOLATION: count approved samples this week
    const dnaRows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ total: bigint }>>`
      SELECT COALESCE(SUM("approvedSamples"), 0)::bigint AS total
      FROM "Request"
      WHERE "approvalStatus" IN ('APPROVED', 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED')
        AND "approvalDate" >= ${weekStart.toISOString()}::timestamp
        AND "approvalDate" <  ${weekEnd.toISOString()}::timestamp
    `
    const dnaApprovedThisWeek = Number(dnaRows[0]?.total ?? 0)

    // GENOTYPING: count approved samples this week (same unit as DNA_ISOLATION).
    // FIX: was computing samples × markersPerSample ("data points") which is a
    // different magnitude. Capacity is now set and consumed in samples for both
    // service types, making the admin's capacity entry unambiguous.
    const genoRows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ total: bigint }>>`
      SELECT COALESCE(SUM("approvedSamples"), 0)::bigint AS total
      FROM "Request"
      WHERE "approvalStatus" IN ('APPROVED', 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED')
        AND "approvalDate" >= ${weekStart.toISOString()}::timestamp
        AND "approvalDate" <  ${weekEnd.toISOString()}::timestamp
    `
    const genoApprovedThisWeek = Number(genoRows[0]?.total ?? 0)

    // Pending queue stats
    const pendingRows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{
      cnt: bigint; samplesSum: bigint; dataPointsSum: bigint
    }>>`
      SELECT
        COUNT(*)::bigint AS cnt,
        COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE("totalSamples", "predictedSamples", 0)), 0)::bigint AS "samplesSum",
        COALESCE(SUM(
          COALESCE("totalSamples", "predictedSamples", 0)
          * COALESCE("markersPerSample", 1)
        ), 0)::bigint AS "dataPointsSum"
      FROM "Request"
      WHERE "approvalStatus" IN ('PENDING_APPROVAL', 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED', 'PARTIALLY_REJECTED')
    `
    const pendingRequests   = Number(pendingRows[0]?.cnt ?? 0)
    const pendingSamples    = Number(pendingRows[0]?.samplesSum ?? 0)
    const pendingDataPoints = Number(pendingRows[0]?.dataPointsSum ?? 0)

    const capacityMap: Record<string, number> = {}
    capacityRows.forEach(row => { capacityMap[row.serviceType] = row.capacity })

    const dnaCap  = capacityMap['DNA_ISOLATION'] ?? DEFAULT_CAPACITIES.DNA_ISOLATION
    const genoCap = capacityMap['GENOTYPING']    ?? DEFAULT_CAPACITIES.GENOTYPING

    return res.status(200).json({
      weekStart: weekStart.toISOString(),
      weekEnd:   weekEnd.toISOString(),
      dnaIsolation: {
        serviceType:       'DNA_ISOLATION',
        unit:              'samples',
        weeklyCapacity:    dnaCap,
        approvedThisWeek:  dnaApprovedThisWeek,
        remainingCapacity: Math.max(0, dnaCap - dnaApprovedThisWeek),
      },
      genotyping: {
        serviceType:       'GENOTYPING',
        unit:              'samples',          // FIX: was 'data points'
        weeklyCapacity:    genoCap,
        approvedThisWeek:  genoApprovedThisWeek,
        remainingCapacity: Math.max(0, genoCap - genoApprovedThisWeek),
      },
      queue: { pendingRequests, pendingSamples, pendingDataPoints },
    })
  }

  // ── PATCH: update capacity ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (req.method === 'PATCH') {
    if (role !== 'ADMIN') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Admin only' })

    const { serviceType, capacity } = req.body as {
      serviceType: string; capacity: number
    }

    if (!SERVICE_TYPES.includes(serviceType as ServiceType)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'serviceType must be DNA_ISOLATION or GENOTYPING' })
    }
    if (!capacity || capacity <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'capacity must be a positive number' })
    }

    await ensureCurrentWeekCapacity(weekStart)
    const ws = weekStart.toISOString()

    await prisma.$executeRaw`
      UPDATE "CapacityTracking"
      SET "capacity" = ${capacity}, "updatedAt" = NOW()
      WHERE "weekStart" = ${ws}::timestamp AND "serviceType" = ${serviceType}
    `

    await writeAudit({
      action: 'CAPACITY_CHANGED', entityType: 'CapacityTracking', entityId: 0,
      detail: `${serviceType} capacity set to ${capacity} samples/week`,
      performedById: userId,
    })

    return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}


===== pages\api\approvals\index.ts =====

// pages/api/approvals/index.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../lib/audit'

declare const global: { io?: { to: (r: string) => { emit: (e: string, d: unknown) => void } } }

const PRIORITY_ORDER: Record<string, number> = { HIGH: 0, NORMAL: 1, LOW: 2 }

async function recalculateQueuePositions() {
  const pending = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{
    id: number; approvalPriority: string; submittedAt: Date
  }>>`
    SELECT id, "approvalPriority", "submittedAt"
    FROM "Request"
    WHERE "approvalStatus" IN ('PENDING_APPROVAL', 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED', 'PARTIALLY_REJECTED')
  `

  const sorted = [...pending].sort((a, b) => {
    const pa = PRIORITY_ORDER[a.approvalPriority] ?? 1
    const pb = PRIORITY_ORDER[b.approvalPriority] ?? 1
    if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb
    return new Date(a.submittedAt).getTime() - new Date(b.submittedAt).getTime()
  })

  for (let idx = 0; idx < sorted.length; idx++) {
    const pos = idx + 1
    await prisma.$executeRaw`
      UPDATE "Request" SET "queuePosition" = ${pos} WHERE id = ${sorted[idx].id}
    `
  }
}

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role   = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const userId = parseInt((session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string)

  const allowed = ['ADMIN', 'MANAGEMENT', 'BREEDER', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST']
  if (!allowed.includes(role)) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })

  // ── GET: list queue ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    const { status: statusF, priority: priorityF, search, page, limit } = req.query

    const pageNum  = Math.max(1, parseInt(page as string) || 1)
    const pageSize = Math.min(100, parseInt(limit as string) || 20)
    const offset   = (pageNum - 1) * pageSize

    const conditions: string[] = []

    // FIX 1: Exclude terminal request statuses — COMPLETED and CANCELLED requests
    // have no business appearing in the approval queue regardless of approvalStatus.
    // CANCELLED is kept visible for ADMIN/MANAGEMENT so they can see rejected history;
    // COMPLETED is excluded for everyone since it means the request has been fully processed.
    conditions.push(`r."status" != 'COMPLETED'`)

    if (role === 'BREEDER') {
      conditions.push(`r."submittedById" = ${userId}`)
    }

    if (role === 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST') {
      conditions.push(`r."approvalStatus" IN ('APPROVED', 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED')`)
    } else if (statusF && statusF !== 'ALL') {
      conditions.push(`r."approvalStatus" = '${(statusF as string).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`)
    }

    if (priorityF && priorityF !== 'ALL') {
      // FIX 2: Filter on "approvalPriority" (the queue-specific priority column that
      // change_priority writes to). This is consistent with what we display and sort by.
      conditions.push(`r."approvalPriority" = '${(priorityF as string).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`)
    }

    if (search) {
      const s = (search as string).replace(/'/g, "''")
      conditions.push(`(r."requestCode" ILIKE '%${s}%' OR r."projectCode" ILIKE '%${s}%' OR r."sampleName" ILIKE '%${s}%')`)
    }

    const whereClause = conditions.length > 0 ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}` : ''

    const requests = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<Record<string, unknown>>>(`
      SELECT
        r.id, r."requestCode", r."projectCode", r."sampleName", r.crop,
        r."testType", r."analysisToBeDone",
        r."approvalStatus", r."approvalPriority",
        r."approvalDate", r."queuePosition",
        r."predictedSamples",
        r."actualSamples",
        r."approvedSamples", r."rejectedSamples", r."rejectionReason",
        r."submittedAt",
        sb.name AS "submittedByName", sb.email AS "submittedByEmail",
        ab.name AS "approvedByName"
      FROM "Request" r
      LEFT JOIN "User" sb ON sb.id = r."submittedById"
      LEFT JOIN "User" ab ON ab.id = r."approvedById"
      ${whereClause}
      ORDER BY
        CASE WHEN r."queuePosition" IS NULL THEN 999999 ELSE r."queuePosition" END ASC,
        r."submittedAt" ASC
      LIMIT ${pageSize} OFFSET ${offset}
    `)
    // NOTE: The original ORDER BY used `WHEN NULL` which is always false in SQL —
    // NULL must be tested with IS NULL. Fixed above.

    const countResult = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<{ count: bigint }>>(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM "Request" r ${whereClause}`
    )
    const total = Number(countResult[0]?.count ?? 0)

    const shaped = requests.map(r => {
      // FIX 3: Prefer actualSamples as the canonical sample count. Only fall back
      // to predictedSamples if actualSamples is genuinely null (draft/pre-submission).
      const canonicalSamples = (r.actualSamples as number | null) ?? (r.predictedSamples as number | null) ?? null

      return {
        id:               r.id,
        requestCode:      r.requestCode,
        projectCode:      r.projectCode,
        sampleName:       r.sampleName,
        crop:             r.crop,
        testType:         r.testType,
        analysisToBeDone: r.analysisToBeDone,
        // FIX 3 (continued): actualSamples on the response always reflects the live
        // value from the DB row, never a stale cached figure.
        actualSamples:    canonicalSamples,
        predictedSamples: r.predictedSamples,
        approvedSamples:  (r.approvedSamples as number) ?? 0,
        rejectedSamples:  (r.rejectedSamples as number) ?? 0,
        rejectionReason:  r.rejectionReason,
        submittedAt:      r.submittedAt,
        submittedBy: { name: r.submittedByName, email: r.submittedByEmail },
        assignedLab:  null,  // not stored on Request directly; extend if needed
        // Shape into the nested approvalQueue object the frontend expects
        approvalQueue: {
          approvalStatus:   r.approvalStatus,
          // FIX 2: approvalPriority is the source of truth for queue ordering and
          // display. It is kept in sync with the main priority field by the
          // change_priority action in [id].ts (see that file). Do NOT merge
          // r.priority here — keep a single authoritative column.
          approvalPriority: r.approvalPriority,
          approvalDate:     r.approvalDate,
          queuePosition:    r.queuePosition,
          approvedSamples:  (r.approvedSamples as number) ?? 0,
          rejectedSamples:  (r.rejectedSamples as number) ?? 0,
          rejectionReason:  r.rejectionReason,
          approvedBy:       r.approvedByName ? { name: r.approvedByName } : null,
        },
      }
    })

    return res.status(200).json({ requests: shaped, total, page: pageNum, pageSize })
  }

  // ── POST: bulk approve / reject ──────────────────────────────────────────
  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    if (role !== 'ADMIN') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Admin only' })

    const { action, requestIds } = req.body as {
      action: 'bulk_approve' | 'bulk_reject'
      requestIds: number[]
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(requestIds) || requestIds.length === 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'requestIds must be a non-empty array' })
    }
    if (requestIds.length > 50) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Maximum 50 requests per bulk action' })
    }

    const idList = requestIds.join(',')

    // FIX 3: Select actualSamples in the bulk fetch so approval math uses the
    // correct figure, not a stale totalSamples or predictedSamples fallback.
    const requests = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<{
      id: number; requestCode: string; projectCode: string | null
      submittedById: number
      actualSamples: number | null; totalSamples: number | null; predictedSamples: number | null
      approvedSamples: number; rejectedSamples: number
    }>>(`
      SELECT id, "requestCode", "projectCode", "submittedById",
             "actualSamples", "totalSamples", "predictedSamples",
             "approvedSamples", "rejectedSamples"
      FROM "Request"
      WHERE id IN (${idList})
        AND "approvalStatus" IN ('PENDING_APPROVAL', 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED', 'PARTIALLY_REJECTED')
        AND "status" != 'COMPLETED'
    `)

    const now = new Date().toISOString()

    if (action === 'bulk_approve') {
      for (const r of requests) {
        // FIX 3: actualSamples is now available and takes precedence
        const total     = r.actualSamples ?? r.totalSamples ?? r.predictedSamples ?? 0
        const remaining = total - r.approvedSamples - r.rejectedSamples
        const newApproved = r.approvedSamples + Math.max(0, remaining)
        await prisma.$executeRaw`
          UPDATE "Request" SET
            "approvalStatus"  = 'APPROVED',
            "approvedSamples" = ${newApproved},
            "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
            "approvedById"    = ${userId},
            "queuePosition"   = NULL,
            "currentPhase"    = 'SUBMITTED',
            "status"          = 'PENDING'
          WHERE id = ${r.id}
        `
        if (global.io) {
          global.io.to(`user_${r.submittedById}`).emit('notification', {
            type: 'success',
            message: `Your request '${r.projectCode ?? r.requestCode}' has been approved.`,
          })
        }
      }
    } else if (action === 'bulk_reject') {
      for (const r of requests) {
        const total     = r.actualSamples ?? r.totalSamples ?? r.predictedSamples ?? 0
        const remaining = total - r.approvedSamples - r.rejectedSamples
        const newRejected = r.rejectedSamples + Math.max(0, remaining)
        await prisma.$executeRaw`
          UPDATE "Request" SET
            "approvalStatus"  = 'REJECTED',
            "rejectedSamples" = ${newRejected},
            "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
            "approvedById"    = ${userId},
            "queuePosition"   = NULL,
            "status"          = 'CANCELLED'
          WHERE id = ${r.id}
        `
        if (global.io) {
          global.io.to(`user_${r.submittedById}`).emit('notification', {
            type: 'warning',
            message: `Your request '${r.projectCode ?? r.requestCode}' was not approved.`,
          })
        }
      }
    } else {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unknown bulk action' })
    }

    await writeAudit({
      action:        action === 'bulk_approve' ? 'BULK_APPROVAL' : 'BULK_REJECTION',
      entityType:    'Request',
      entityId:      0,
      detail:        `Bulk ${action === 'bulk_approve' ? 'approved' : 'rejected'} ${requests.length} request(s)`,
      performedById: userId,
    })

    await recalculateQueuePositions()
    return res.status(200).json({ ok: true, affected: requests.length })
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}



===== pages\api\approvals\[id].ts =====

// pages/api/approvals/[id].ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../lib/audit'

declare const global: { io?: { to: (r: string) => { emit: (e: string, d: unknown) => void } } }

const PRIORITY_ORDER: Record<string, number> = { HIGH: 0, NORMAL: 1, LOW: 2 }

const INITIAL_PHASES = new Set(['SUBMITTED'])

async function recalculateQueuePositions() {
  const pending = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{
    id: number; approvalPriority: string; submittedAt: Date
  }>>`
    SELECT id, "approvalPriority", "submittedAt"
    FROM "Request"
    WHERE "approvalStatus" IN ('PENDING_APPROVAL', 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED', 'PARTIALLY_REJECTED')
  `
  const sorted = [...pending].sort((a, b) => {
    const pa = PRIORITY_ORDER[a.approvalPriority] ?? 1
    const pb = PRIORITY_ORDER[b.approvalPriority] ?? 1
    if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb
    return new Date(a.submittedAt).getTime() - new Date(b.submittedAt).getTime()
  })
  for (let idx = 0; idx < sorted.length; idx++) {
    const pos = idx + 1
    await prisma.$executeRaw`
      UPDATE "Request" SET "queuePosition" = ${pos} WHERE id = ${sorted[idx].id}
    `
  }
}

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role   = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const userId = parseInt((session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string)

  const id = parseInt(req.query.id as string)
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid ID' })

  if (req.method !== 'PATCH') return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
  if (role !== 'ADMIN') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Admin only' })

  const { action, samples, reason, priority } = req.body as {
    action:    'approve' | 'reject' | 'partial_approve' | 'partial_reject' | 'change_priority'
    samples?:  number
    reason?:   string
    priority?: string
  }

  // FIX 3: "actualSamples" was missing from this SELECT entirely.
  // Without it, totalSamples falls back to predictedSamples and all approval
  // math (remaining, newApproved, newRejected) operates on the wrong number.
  const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{
    id: number; requestCode: string; projectCode: string | null
    approvalStatus: string; approvalPriority: string; currentPhase: string
    actualSamples: number | null
    totalSamples: number | null; predictedSamples: number | null
    approvedSamples: number; rejectedSamples: number
    rejectionReason: string | null; submittedById: number
  }>>`
    SELECT id, "requestCode", "projectCode", "approvalStatus", "approvalPriority",
           "currentPhase",
           "actualSamples",
           "totalSamples", "predictedSamples",
           "approvedSamples", "rejectedSamples",
           "rejectionReason", "submittedById"
    FROM "Request" WHERE id = ${id} LIMIT 1
  `

  if (!rows || rows.length === 0) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Request not found' })
  const existing = rows[0]

  // FIX 3: Use actualSamples as the canonical count. totalSamples and
  // predictedSamples are only fallbacks for legacy/draft records that
  // pre-date the actualSamples field being populated.
  const totalSamples    = existing.actualSamples ?? existing.totalSamples ?? existing.predictedSamples ?? 0
  const alreadyApproved = existing.approvedSamples
  const alreadyRejected = existing.rejectedSamples
  const remaining       = totalSamples - alreadyApproved - alreadyRejected
  const now             = new Date().toISOString()
  const code            = existing.projectCode ?? existing.requestCode

  const shouldResetPhase = INITIAL_PHASES.has(existing.currentPhase)
  const phaseOnApprove   = shouldResetPhase ? 'SUBMITTED' : existing.currentPhase

  // ── Change Priority ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (action === 'change_priority') {
    if (!priority || !['HIGH', 'NORMAL', 'LOW'].includes(priority)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid priority required: HIGH, NORMAL, LOW' })
    }
    // FIX 2: Write to BOTH priority columns so the Requests page and the
    // Approvals page always show the same value. Previously only
    // "approvalPriority" was updated, leaving "priority" stale.
    await prisma.$executeRaw`
      UPDATE "Request"
      SET "approvalPriority" = ${priority},
          "priority"         = ${priority}
      WHERE id = ${id}
    `
    await writeAudit({
      action: 'PRIORITY_CHANGED', entityType: 'Request', entityId: id,
      detail: `Priority changed to ${priority} for '${code}'`, performedById: userId,
    })
    await recalculateQueuePositions()
    return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
  }

  // ── Full Approve ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (action === 'approve') {
    if (existing.approvalStatus === 'APPROVED') {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Already fully approved' })
    }
    const newApproved = alreadyApproved + remaining
    await prisma.$executeRaw`
      UPDATE "Request" SET
        "approvalStatus"  = 'APPROVED',
        "approvedSamples" = ${newApproved},
        "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
        "approvedById"    = ${userId},
        "queuePosition"   = NULL,
        "currentPhase"    = ${phaseOnApprove},
        "status"          = 'PENDING'
      WHERE id = ${id}
    `
    await writeAudit({
      action: 'REQUEST_APPROVED', entityType: 'Request', entityId: id,
      detail: `Approved all ${remaining} remaining samples for '${code}'`, performedById: userId,
    })
    if (global.io) {
      global.io.to(`user_${existing.submittedById}`).emit('notification', {
        type: 'success',
        message: `Your request '${code}' has been approved.`,
      })
    }
    await recalculateQueuePositions()
    return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
  }

  // ── Full Reject ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (action === 'reject') {
    if (existing.approvalStatus === 'REJECTED') {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Already fully rejected' })
    }
    const newRejected = alreadyRejected + remaining
    const safeReason  = reason ?? null
    await prisma.$executeRaw`
      UPDATE "Request" SET
        "approvalStatus"  = 'REJECTED',
        "rejectedSamples" = ${newRejected},
        "rejectionReason" = ${safeReason},
