        "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
        "approvedById"    = ${userId},
        "queuePosition"   = NULL,
        "status"          = 'CANCELLED'
      WHERE id = ${id}
    `
    await writeAudit({
      action: 'REQUEST_REJECTED', entityType: 'Request', entityId: id,
      detail: `Rejected '${code}'${reason ? ` — ${reason}` : ''}`, performedById: userId,
    })
    if (global.io) {
      global.io.to(`user_${existing.submittedById}`).emit('notification', {
        type: 'warning',
        message: `Your request '${code}' was not approved.`,
      })
    }
    await recalculateQueuePositions()
    return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
  }

  // ── Partial Approve ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (action === 'partial_approve') {
    if (!samples || samples <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'samples must be a positive number' })
    }
    if (samples > remaining) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: `Cannot approve ${samples} — only ${remaining} remaining`,
      })
    }
    const newApproved     = alreadyApproved + samples
    const isFullyApproved = newApproved + alreadyRejected >= totalSamples

    if (isFullyApproved) {
      await prisma.$executeRaw`
        UPDATE "Request" SET
          "approvalStatus"  = 'APPROVED',
          "approvedSamples" = ${newApproved},
          "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
          "approvedById"    = ${userId},
          "queuePosition"   = NULL,
          "currentPhase"    = ${phaseOnApprove},
          "status"          = 'PENDING'
        WHERE id = ${id}
      `
    } else {
      await prisma.$executeRaw`
        UPDATE "Request" SET
          "approvalStatus"  = 'PARTIALLY_APPROVED',
          "approvedSamples" = ${newApproved},
          "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
          "approvedById"    = ${userId}
        WHERE id = ${id}
      `
    }
    await writeAudit({
      action: 'PARTIAL_APPROVAL', entityType: 'Request', entityId: id,
      detail: `Partially approved ${samples} samples for '${code}' (${newApproved}/${totalSamples} total)`,
      performedById: userId,
    })
    if (global.io) {
      global.io.to(`user_${existing.submittedById}`).emit('notification', {
        type: 'success',
        message: `${samples} samples from request '${code}' have been approved.`,
      })
    }
    await recalculateQueuePositions()
    return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
  }

  // ── Partial Reject ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (action === 'partial_reject') {
    if (!samples || samples <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'samples must be a positive number' })
    }
    if (samples > remaining) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: `Cannot reject ${samples} — only ${remaining} remaining`,
      })
    }
    const newRejected     = alreadyRejected + samples
    const isFullyRejected = newRejected + alreadyApproved >= totalSamples
    const safeReason      = reason ?? existing.rejectionReason

    if (isFullyRejected) {
      await prisma.$executeRaw`
        UPDATE "Request" SET
          "approvalStatus"  = 'REJECTED',
          "rejectedSamples" = ${newRejected},
          "rejectionReason" = ${safeReason},
          "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
          "approvedById"    = ${userId},
          "queuePosition"   = NULL,
          "status"          = 'CANCELLED'
        WHERE id = ${id}
      `
    } else {
      await prisma.$executeRaw`
        UPDATE "Request" SET
          "approvalStatus"  = 'PARTIALLY_REJECTED',
          "rejectedSamples" = ${newRejected},
          "rejectionReason" = ${safeReason},
          "approvalDate"    = ${now}::timestamp,
          "approvedById"    = ${userId}
        WHERE id = ${id}
      `
    }
    await writeAudit({
      action: 'PARTIAL_REJECTION', entityType: 'Request', entityId: id,
      detail: `Partially rejected ${samples} samples for '${code}' (${newRejected}/${totalSamples} rejected)`,
      performedById: userId,
    })
    if (global.io) {
      global.io.to(`user_${existing.submittedById}`).emit('notification', {
        type: 'warning',
        message: `${samples} samples from request '${code}' were rejected.`,
      })
    }
    await recalculateQueuePositions()
    return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
  }

  return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unknown action' })
}



===== pages\api\auth\[...nextauth].ts =====

// pages/api/auth/[...nextauth].ts

import NextAuth, { NextAuthOptions } from 'next-auth'
import CredentialsProvider from 'next-auth/providers/credentials'
import type { JWT } from 'next-auth/jwt'
import type { Session } from 'next-auth'
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs'
import { withRateLimit } from '../../../lib/withRateLimit'

const prisma = new PrismaClient()

export const authOptions: NextAuthOptions = {
  providers: [
    CredentialsProvider({
      name: 'Credentials',
      credentials: {
        email: { label: 'Email', type: 'email' },
        password: { label: 'Password', type: 'password' },
      },
      async authorize(credentials) {
        if (!credentials?.email || !credentials?.password) return null

        const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
          where: { email: credentials.email },
        })

        if (!user) return null

        const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(credentials.password, user.password)
        if (!isValid) return null

                return {
          id: String(user.id),
          name: user.name,
          email: user.email,
          role: user.role,
        }
      },
    }),
  ],

  session: { strategy: 'jwt' },

  callbacks: {
    
    async jwt({ token, user }: { token: JWT; user?: { id?: string; role?: string } }) {
      if (user?.role) token.role = user.role
      if (user?.id) token.id = user.id
      return token
    },

    
    async session({ session, token }: { session: Session; token: JWT }) {
      if (session.user && token.role) {
        session.user.role = token.role as string
      }

      if (session.user && token.id) {
        session.user.id = token.id as string
      }

      return session
    },
  },

  pages: { signIn: '/login' },
}

export default withRateLimit(NextAuth(authOptions), 10)


===== pages\api\dashboard\crops.ts =====

// pages/api/dashboard/crops.ts
//
// Returns the distinct non-null values of the `crop` field from Request.
// Used to populate the Crop filter dropdown on the dashboard.

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'MANAGEMENT' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })

  try {
    // `crop` is String? on Request — fetch distinct non-null values
    const rows = await prisma.request.findMany({
      where:    { crop: { not: null }, archived: false },
      select:   { crop: true },
      distinct: ['crop'],
      orderBy:  { crop: 'asc' },
    })

    const crops = rows
      .map((r: { crop: string | null }) => r.crop)
      .filter((c): c is string => c !== null)

    return res.status(200).json({ crops })
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[dashboard/crops]', err)
    return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' })
  }
}


===== pages\api\dashboard\export.ts =====

// pages/api/dashboard/export.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== "MANAGEMENT" && role !== "ADMIN") {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden" })
  }
  if (req.method !== "GET") return res.status(405).end()

  const { type } = req.query
  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new()
  let filename = "dashboard-export.xlsx"

  if (type === "reagent-summary") {
    const now = new Date()
    const startOfMonth = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1)
    const grouped = await prisma.reagentLog.groupBy({
      by: ["itemId"],
      where: { usedAt: { gte: startOfMonth } },
      _sum: { quantityUsed: true },
      orderBy: { _sum: { quantityUsed: "desc" } },
    })
    const itemIds = grouped.map(g => g.itemId)
    const items = await prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
      where: { id: { in: itemIds } },
      select: { id: true, name: true, unit: true }
    })
    const rows = grouped.map(g => {
      const item = items.find(i => i.id === g.itemId)
      return {
        "Reagent":      item?.name ?? "Unknown",
        "Unit":         item?.unit ?? "",
        "Total Used":   g._sum.quantityUsed ?? 0,
        "Period":       `${now.toLocaleString("default", { month: "long" })} ${now.getFullYear()}`,
      }
    })
    const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows)
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Reagent Summary")
    filename = `reagent-summary-${now.toISOString().slice(0,10)}.xlsx`

  } else if (type === "requests") {
    const requests = await prisma.request.findMany({
      include: {
        submittedBy: { select: { name: true } },
        assignedLab: { select: { name: true } }
      },
      orderBy: { submittedAt: "desc" }
    })
    const rows = requests.map(r => ({
      "Request Code":    r.requestCode,
      "Sample Name":     r.sampleName,
      "Crop":            r.crop ?? "",
      "Test Type":       r.testType,
      "Status":          r.status,
      "Current Phase":   r.currentPhase,
      "Submitted By":    r.submittedBy.name,
      "Lab":             r.assignedLab?.name ?? "Unassigned",
      "Submitted At":    new Date(r.submittedAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB"),
      "Expected By":     new Date(r.expectedByDate).toLocaleDateString("en-GB"),
      "Completed At":    r.completedAt ? new Date(r.completedAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB") : "",
    }))
    const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows)
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "All Requests")
    filename = `requests-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.xlsx`

  } else {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid export type" })
  }

  const buffer = XLSX.write(wb, { bookType: "xlsx", type: "buffer" })
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")
  res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", `attachment; filename="${filename}"`)
  return res.status(200).send(buffer)
}



===== pages\api\dashboard\lab-comparison.ts =====

// pages/api/dashboard/lab-comparison.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== "MANAGEMENT" && role !== "ADMIN") {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden" })
  }
  if (req.method !== "GET") return res.status(405).end()

  const { months = "3" } = req.query
  const monthCount = parseInt(months as string) || 3
  const since = new Date(Date.now() - monthCount * 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)

  // Get labs
  const labs = await prisma.lab.findMany({ select: { id: true, name: true } })

  // For each lab, sum reagent usage in the period
  const results = await Promise.all(labs.map(async lab => {
    // Get inventory items belonging to this lab
    const items = await prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
      where: { labId: lab.id },
      select: { id: true, name: true }
    })
    const itemIds = items.map(i => i.id)

    // Get top 5 reagents by usage for this lab
    const usage = await prisma.reagentLog.groupBy({
      by: ["itemId"],
      where: {
        itemId: { in: itemIds },
        usedAt: { gte: since }
      },
      _sum: { quantityUsed: true },
      orderBy: { _sum: { quantityUsed: "desc" } },
      take: 5
    })

    const totalUsage = usage.reduce((sum, u) => sum + (u._sum.quantityUsed ?? 0), 0)

    const topReagents = usage.map(u => {
      const item = items.find(i => i.id === u.itemId)
      return {
        name:  item?.name ?? "Unknown",
        total: u._sum.quantityUsed ?? 0
      }
    })

    return { labId: lab.id, labName: lab.name, totalUsage, topReagents }
  }))

  return res.status(200).json(results)
}



===== pages\api\dashboard\stats.ts =====

// pages/api/dashboard/stats.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

const ANALYSIS_KEY_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  'Foreground':             'Foreground',
  'Purity Analysis':        'Purity_Analysis',
  'Purity_Analysis':        'Purity_Analysis',
  'Hybridity Confirmation': 'Hybridity_Confirmation',
  'Hybridity_Confirmation': 'Hybridity_Confirmation',
  'Background':             'Background',
  'Line Fingerprinting':    'Line_Fingerprinting',
  'Line_Fingerprinting':    'Line_Fingerprinting',
  'Gene Profiling':         'Gene_Profiling',
  'Gene_Profiling':         'Gene_Profiling',
}

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'BREEDER' && role !== 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST' && role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN' && role !== 'MANAGEMENT') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })

  const yearParam  = req.query.year  as string | undefined
  const monthParam = req.query.month as string | undefined
  const cropParam  = req.query.crop  as string | undefined

  const parsedYear  = yearParam  ? parseInt(yearParam,  10) : null
  const parsedMonth = monthParam ? parseInt(monthParam, 10) : null

  type WhereClause = {
    archived: boolean
    crop?: string
    year?: number
    submittedAt?: { gte: Date; lt: Date }
  }

  const where: WhereClause = { archived: false }

  if (cropParam) where.crop = cropParam

  if (parsedYear && !isNaN(parsedYear)) {
    if (parsedMonth && !isNaN(parsedMonth)) {
      where.submittedAt = {
        gte: new Date(parsedYear, parsedMonth - 1, 1),
        lt:  new Date(parsedYear, parsedMonth,     1),
      }
    } else {
      where.year = parsedYear
    }
  }

  const allRequests = await prisma.request.findMany({
    where,
    select: {
      id:               true,
      status:           true,
      currentPhase:     true,
      markerType:       true,
      snpCount:         true,
      ssrCount:         true,
      markersPerSample: true,
      completedAt:      true,
      expectedByDate:   true,
      submittedAt:      true,
      actualSamples:    true,
      actualDataPoints: true,
      crop:             true,
      analysisToBeDone: true,
    },
  })

  const requests =
    parsedMonth && !isNaN(parsedMonth) && (!parsedYear || isNaN(parsedYear))
      ? allRequests.filter(
          r => new Date(r.submittedAt).getMonth() + 1 === parsedMonth
        )
      : allRequests

  const now = new Date()
  const startOfMonth = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1)

  const total = requests.length

  const completedThisMonth = requests.filter(r =>
    r.status === 'COMPLETED' &&
    r.completedAt != null &&
    new Date(r.completedAt) >= startOfMonth
  ).length

  const pending    = requests.filter(r => r.status === 'PENDING').length
  const inProgress = requests.filter(r => r.status === 'IN_PROGRESS').length

  const completedAll = requests.filter(r => r.status === 'COMPLETED' && r.completedAt != null)
  const onTimeCount  = completedAll.filter(
    r => new Date(r.completedAt!) <= new Date(r.expectedByDate)
  ).length
  const delayedCount = completedAll.length - onTimeCount
  const onTimeRate   = completedAll.length > 0
    ? Math.round((onTimeCount / completedAll.length) * 100)
    : 0

  const statusBreakdown: Record<string, number> = {}
  for (const r of requests) {
    statusBreakdown[r.status] = (statusBreakdown[r.status] ?? 0) + 1
  }

  const phaseDistribution: Record<string, number> = {}
  for (const r of requests) {
    phaseDistribution[r.currentPhase] = (phaseDistribution[r.currentPhase] ?? 0) + 1
  }

  // ── SNP / SSR aggregation ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  //
  // Marker count resolution order per request:
  //
  //   SNP-only  → snpCount ?? markersPerSample ?? 0
  //               (markersPerSample IS the SNP marker count for a pure SNP request)
  //
  //   SSR-only  → ssrCount ?? markersPerSample ?? 0
  //               (same logic for pure SSR requests)
  //
  //   BOTH      → snpCount and ssrCount used as-is (explicit split required;
  //               markersPerSample cannot be safely attributed to either type alone)
  //
  // Data points formula (per spec):
  //   SNP data points = resolvedSnpMarkers × actualSamples
  //   SSR data points = resolvedSsrMarkers × actualSamples

  const testTypeSplit:      Record<string, number> = {}
  const testTypeSamples:    Record<string, number> = {}
  const testTypeDataPoints: Record<string, number> = {}

  for (const r of requests) {
    const mt      = (r.markerType ?? 'Unknown').toUpperCase().trim()
    const samples = r.actualSamples ?? 0

    if (mt === 'BOTH') {
      const snp = r.snpCount ?? 0
      const ssr = r.ssrCount ?? 0

      testTypeSplit['SNP'] = (testTypeSplit['SNP'] ?? 0) + 1
      testTypeSplit['SSR'] = (testTypeSplit['SSR'] ?? 0) + 1

      testTypeSamples['SNP'] = (testTypeSamples['SNP'] ?? 0) + samples
      testTypeSamples['SSR'] = (testTypeSamples['SSR'] ?? 0) + samples

      testTypeDataPoints['SNP'] = (testTypeDataPoints['SNP'] ?? 0) + (snp * samples)
      testTypeDataPoints['SSR'] = (testTypeDataPoints['SSR'] ?? 0) + (ssr * samples)

    } else if (mt === 'SNP') {
      // FIX: fall back to markersPerSample when snpCount is null.
      // For a pure SNP request markersPerSample equals the SNP marker count.
      const snp = r.snpCount ?? r.markersPerSample ?? 0

      testTypeSplit['SNP']      = (testTypeSplit['SNP']      ?? 0) + 1
      testTypeSamples['SNP']    = (testTypeSamples['SNP']    ?? 0) + samples
      testTypeDataPoints['SNP'] = (testTypeDataPoints['SNP'] ?? 0) + (snp * samples)

    } else if (mt === 'SSR') {
      // FIX: fall back to markersPerSample when ssrCount is null.
      const ssr = r.ssrCount ?? r.markersPerSample ?? 0

      testTypeSplit['SSR']      = (testTypeSplit['SSR']      ?? 0) + 1
      testTypeSamples['SSR']    = (testTypeSamples['SSR']    ?? 0) + samples
      testTypeDataPoints['SSR'] = (testTypeDataPoints['SSR'] ?? 0) + (ssr * samples)

    } else {
      // Unknown / other marker types
      testTypeSplit[mt]      = (testTypeSplit[mt]      ?? 0) + 1
      testTypeSamples[mt]    = (testTypeSamples[mt]    ?? 0) + samples
      testTypeDataPoints[mt] = (testTypeDataPoints[mt] ?? 0) + (r.actualDataPoints ?? 0)
    }
  }

  const totalActualSamples = requests.reduce(
    (sum, r) => sum + (r.actualSamples ?? 0), 0
  )
  const totalActualDataPoints = requests.reduce(
    (sum, r) => sum + (r.actualDataPoints ?? 0), 0
  )

  const cropSamples: Record<string, number> = {}
  const cropDataPoints: Record<string, number> = {}
  for (const r of requests) {
    const crop = r.crop ?? 'Unknown'
    cropSamples[crop]    = (cropSamples[crop]    ?? 0) + (r.actualSamples    ?? 0)
    cropDataPoints[crop] = (cropDataPoints[crop] ?? 0) + (r.actualDataPoints ?? 0)
  }

  const analysisSamples: Record<string, number> = {}
  for (const r of requests) {
    const raw = r.analysisToBeDone ?? 'Unknown'
    const key = ANALYSIS_KEY_MAP[raw] ?? raw
    analysisSamples[key] = (analysisSamples[key] ?? 0) + (r.actualSamples ?? 0)
  }

  return res.status(200).json({
    total,
    completedThisMonth,
    pending,
    inProgress,
    onTimeRate,
    delayed: delayedCount,
    statusBreakdown,
    phaseDistribution,
    testTypeSplit,
    testTypeSamples,
    testTypeDataPoints,
    onTimeVsDelayed:       { onTime: onTimeCount, delayed: delayedCount },
    totalActualSamples,
    totalActualDataPoints,
    cropSamples,
    cropDataPoints,
    analysisSamples,
  })
}


===== pages\api\dashboard\timeline.ts =====

// pages/api/dashboard/timeline.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'MANAGEMENT' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).end()

  const months: Array<{ label: string; submitted: number; completed: number }> = []

  for (let i = 11; i >= 0; i--) {
    const d = new Date()
    d.setDate(1)
    d.setMonth(d.getMonth() - i)
    const start = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1)
    const end   = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59)

    const [submitted, completed] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.request.count({ where: { submittedAt: { gte: start, lte: end } } }),
      prisma.request.count({ where: { status: 'COMPLETED', completedAt: { gte: start, lte: end } } }),
    ])

    const label = d.toLocaleString('default', { month: 'short', year: '2-digit' })
    months.push({ label, submitted, completed })
  }
  return res.status(200).json(months)
}



===== pages\api\dashboard\years.ts =====

// pages/api/dashboard/years.ts
//
// Returns the distinct non-null values of the `year` Int? field from Request,
// sorted descending (most recent first).
// Used to populate the Year filter dropdown on the dashboard.

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'MANAGEMENT' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })

  try {
    // `year` is Int? on Request — fetch distinct non-null values
    const rows = await prisma.request.findMany({
      where:    { year: { not: null }, archived: false },
      select:   { year: true },
      distinct: ['year'],
      orderBy:  { year: 'desc' },   // most recent first
    })

    const years = rows
      .map((r: { year: number | null }) => r.year)
      .filter((y): y is number => y !== null)

    return res.status(200).json({ years })
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[dashboard/years]', err)
    return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' })
  }
}

===== pages\api\experiments\[id]\items.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })

  const id = parseInt(req.query.id as string)
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid experiment ID' })

  const experimentItems = await prisma.experimentItem.findMany({
    where: { experimentId: id },
    include: {
      item: {
        include: { lab: { select: { id: true, name: true } } }
      }
    }
  })

  const result = experimentItems.map(ei => ({
    id: ei.item.id,
    name: ei.item.name,
    quantity: ei.item.quantity,
    unit: ei.item.unit,
    labs: [{ id: ei.item.lab.id, name: ei.item.lab.name }]
  }))

  return res.status(200).json(result)
}


===== pages\api\import\excel.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx'
import formidable from 'formidable'

export const config = { api: { bodyParser: false } }

// ── 30-day analyses ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const THIRTY_DAY_ANALYSES = ['background', 'line fingerprinting', 'line_fingerprinting']

export function getTestType(analysisToBeDone: string | null): '15-DAY' | '30-DAY' {
  const lower = (analysisToBeDone ?? '').toLowerCase()
  return THIRTY_DAY_ANALYSES.some(a => lower.includes(a)) ? '30-DAY' : '15-DAY'
}

export function getDodDays(testType: '15-DAY' | '30-DAY'): number {
  return testType === '30-DAY' ? 30 : 15
}

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function rawStr(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return ''
  if (value instanceof Date) {
    if (isNaN(value.getTime())) return ''
    const d = String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
    const m = String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
    const y = value.getFullYear()
    return `${d}.${m}.${y}`
  }
  return String(value).trim()
}

function safeDate(value: unknown): Date | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null
  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return isNaN(value.getTime()) ? null : value
  }
  const str = String(value).trim()
  if (str === '' || str.toUpperCase() === 'NA' || str === '-' || str === '0') return null

  // DD.MM.YYYY
  const dotMatch = str.match(/^(\d{1,2})\.(\d{1,2})\.(\d{4})$/)
  if (dotMatch) {
    const [, d, m, y] = dotMatch
    const date = new Date(parseInt(y), parseInt(m) - 1, parseInt(d))
    return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date
  }

  // DD/MM/YYYY
  const slashMatch = str.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/)
  if (slashMatch) {
    const [, d, m, y] = slashMatch
    const date = new Date(parseInt(y), parseInt(m) - 1, parseInt(d))
    return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date
  }

  // DD-MM-YYYY
  const dashMatch = str.match(/^(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{4})$/)
  if (dashMatch) {
    const [, d, m, y] = dashMatch
    const date = new Date(parseInt(y), parseInt(m) - 1, parseInt(d))
    return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date
  }

  // Excel numeric serial
  if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(str)) {
    const serial = parseFloat(str)
    if (serial >= 1 && serial <= 2958465) {
      const excelEpoch = new Date(1899, 11, 30)
      const date = new Date(excelEpoch.getTime() + serial * 86400000)
      return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date
    }
    return null
  }

  const parsed = new Date(str)
  return isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed
}

function safeInt(value: unknown): number | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null
  if (value instanceof Date) return null
  const str = String(value).replace(/,/g, '').trim()
  if (str === '' || str.toUpperCase() === 'NA') return null
  const num = parseInt(str)
  return isNaN(num) ? null : num
}

function safeStr(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null
  if (value instanceof Date) return null
  const str = String(value).replace(/,/g, '').trim()
  if (str === '' || str.toUpperCase() === 'NA') return null
  return str
}

function safeQty(value: unknown): number {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return 0
  const str = String(value).trim().toLowerCase()
  if (str === '' || str === 'nil' || str === 'na' || str === 'n/a' || str === '-') return 0
  const match = str.match(/^(\d+\.?\d*)/)
  if (match) return parseFloat(match[1])
  return 0
}

function normaliseType(value: unknown): string {
  const s = rawStr(value).toLowerCase()
  if (s.includes('consumable')) return 'CONSUMABLE'
  return 'CHEMICAL'
}

function resolveLabId(value: unknown, targetLabId: number): number {
  const str = rawStr(value)
  const match = str.match(/lab\s*(\d+)/i)
  if (match) return parseInt(match[1])
  return targetLabId
}

// ── Handler ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'ADMIN' && role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'POST') return res.status(405).end()

  const form = formidable({})
  const [fields, files] = await form.parse(req)

  const sheetType = Array.isArray(fields.sheetType)
    ? fields.sheetType[0]
    : (fields.sheetType ?? 'requests')

  const labIdField = Array.isArray(fields.labId)
    ? fields.labId[0]
    : (fields.labId ?? '1')

  const targetLabId = parseInt(labIdField) || 1

  const uploadedFile = files.excel
  if (!uploadedFile?.[0]) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No file uploaded' })
  }

  const workbook = XLSX.readFile(uploadedFile[0].filepath, {
    cellDates: false,
    raw: false,
  })

  const sheetName =
    sheetType === 'inventory' && workbook.SheetNames.includes('Sheet2')
      ? 'Sheet2'
      : workbook.SheetNames[0]

  const sheet = workbook.Sheets[sheetName]

  const rawRows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {
    defval: '',
    header: 1,
  }) as unknown[][]

  if (rawRows.length < 2) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'The Excel sheet is empty' })
  }

  const headers = (rawRows[0] as unknown[]).map(h => rawStr(h))
  let remarksCount = 0
  const fixedHeaders = headers.map(h => {
    if (h === 'Remarks') {
      remarksCount++
      return remarksCount === 1 ? 'Remarks_Pre' : 'Remarks_Post'
    }
    return h
  })

  const rows: Record<string, unknown>[] = rawRows.slice(1).map(row => {
    const obj: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    fixedHeaders.forEach((key, i) => {
      obj[key] = (row as unknown[])[i] ?? ''
    })
    return obj
  })

  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'The Excel sheet has no data rows' })
  }

  console.log(`\n=== IMPORT STARTED (sheet: ${sheetName}, type: ${sheetType}) ===`)
  console.log('Row count:', rows.length)
  console.log('Columns:', fixedHeaders.join(', '))
  console.log('FIRST ROW RAW:', JSON.stringify(rows[0], null, 2))

  let imported = 0
  let skipped = 0
  const skippedDetails: string[] = []
  const errors: string[] = []

  // ── REQUESTS IMPORT ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  if (sheetType === 'requests') {
    const defaultSubmitter = await prisma.user.findFirst({ where: { role: 'BREEDER' } })
    if (!defaultSubmitter) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No BREEDER user found. Run: npx prisma db seed' })
    }

    // Check whether the approval columns exist so we can set approvalStatus correctly.
    const colCheck = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ column_name: string }>>`
      SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns
      WHERE table_name = 'Request' AND column_name = 'approvalStatus'
    `
    const hasApprovalColumns = colCheck.length > 0

    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      const row = rows[i]
      const rowNum = i + 2

      try {
        const importBatchId = `${Date.now().toString().slice(-8)}-${String(i).padStart(3, '0')}`
        const requestCode = `IMP-${importBatchId}-R${String(i + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`

        const year             = safeInt(row['Year'])
        const season           = rawStr(row['Season'])         || null
        const crop             = rawStr(row['Crop'])           || null
        const program          = rawStr(row['Program'])        || null
        const sampleName       = rawStr(row['Nursery Name'])   || `Row-${rowNum}`
        const purpose          = rawStr(row['Purpose'])        || null
        const materialStage    = rawStr(row['Material Stage']) || null
        const projectCode      = rawStr(row['Project Code'])   || null
        const project          = rawStr(row['Project'])        || null
        const analysisToBeDone = rawStr(row['Analysis to be done']) || null

        const traitGene = rawStr(
          row['Trait/ Gene'] !== '' ? row['Trait/ Gene']
          : row['Trait/Gene'] !== '' ? row['Trait/Gene']
          : row['Trait / Gene']
        ) || null

        const markersPerSample    = safeInt(row['Marker per sample'])
        const markerType          = safeStr(row['Marker type'])
        const predictedSamples    = safeInt(row['Predicted Samples'])
        const predictedDataPoints = safeInt(row['Predicted data points'])
        const actualSamples       = safeInt(row['Actual Samples'])
        const actualDataPoints    = safeInt(row['Actual Data points'])

        const remarksPre  = rawStr(row['Remarks_Pre'])  || null
        const remarksPost = rawStr(row['Remarks_Post']) || null
        const notes = [remarksPre, remarksPost].filter(Boolean).join(' | ') || null

        const priorityRaw = safeInt(row['PRIORITY']) ?? 1
        const priority = priorityRaw === 1 ? 3 : priorityRaw === 3 ? 1 : 2

        const tentativeSamplingDate    = safeDate(row['Tentative sampling date'])
        const dateOfSampling           = safeDate(row['Date of Sampling'])
        const dateOfDispatch           = safeDate(row['Date of dispatch'])
        const dnaExtractionDate        = safeDate(row['DNA Extraction completion'])
        const genotypingDate           = safeDate(row['Genotyping completion'])
        const resultsCommunicationDate = safeDate(row['Results communication date'])
        const dateRequiredByBreeder    = safeDate(row['Date on or before the data is required'])

        const testType = getTestType(analysisToBeDone)
        const dodDays  = getDodDays(testType)

        const samplingBase      = dateOfSampling ?? tentativeSamplingDate ?? new Date()
        const calculatedDod     = new Date(samplingBase.getTime() + dodDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
        const dodCommittedByLab = safeDate(row['DOD (Committed by lab)']) ?? calculatedDod
        const expectedByDate    = dateRequiredByBreeder ?? dodCommittedByLab
        const submittedAt       = dateOfSampling ?? tentativeSamplingDate ?? new Date()
        const completedAt       = resultsCommunicationDate

        const statusRaw = rawStr(
          row['STATUS'] !== '' ? row['STATUS'] : row['Status']
        ).toUpperCase()

        let status: string
        let currentPhase: string

        if (statusRaw === 'DONE' || statusRaw === 'COMPLETED') {
          status = 'COMPLETED'
          currentPhase = 'COMPLETED'
        } else if (
          statusRaw === 'IN PROGRESS' ||
          statusRaw === 'IN_PROGRESS' ||
          statusRaw === 'WIP'
        ) {
          status = 'PENDING'
          if (resultsCommunicationDate || genotypingDate) currentPhase = 'ANALYSIS'
          else if (dnaExtractionDate) currentPhase = 'GENOTYPING'
          else if (dateOfDispatch) currentPhase = 'DNA_ISOLATION'
          else currentPhase = 'SUBMITTED'
        } else {
          status = 'PENDING'
          currentPhase = 'SUBMITTED'
        }

        // FIX: imported requests represent historical data that has already been
        // processed — they should be immediately visible and workable without
        // going through the approval queue. Previously the schema default
        // 'PENDING_APPROVAL' was applied, hiding all imported records from
        // BIOTECHNOLOGIST and MANAGEMENT until a separate fix script was run.
        // We now explicitly set approvalStatus = 'APPROVED' and totalSamples so
        // that imported records behave like pre-approved requests from day one.
        const approvalData = hasApprovalColumns ? {
          approvalStatus:   'APPROVED',
          approvalPriority: 'NORMAL',
          approvedSamples:  actualSamples ?? predictedSamples ?? 0,
          totalSamples:     actualSamples ?? predictedSamples ?? 0,
          approvalDate:     submittedAt,
          queuePosition:    null,
        } : {}

        await prisma.request.create({
          data: {
            requestCode,
            submittedById:            defaultSubmitter.id,
            sampleName,
            crop,
            season,
            year,
            program,
            purpose,
            materialStage,
            project,
            projectCode,
            analysisToBeDone,
            testType,
            traitGene,
            markersPerSample,
            markerType,
            predictedSamples,
            predictedDataPoints,
            actualSamples,
            actualDataPoints,
            priority,
            notes,
            submittedAt,
            expectedByDate,
            tentativeSamplingDate,
            dateOfSampling,
            dateOfDispatch,
            dodCommittedByLab,
            dnaExtractionDate,
            genotypingDate,
            resultsCommunicationDate,
            completedAt,
            status,
            currentPhase,
            ...approvalData,
            phaseHistory: {
              create: { phase: currentPhase }
            }
          }
        })

        imported++
        console.log(
          `Row ${rowNum}: ✓ ${requestCode}` +
          ` [${testType}, DOD +${dodDays}d → ${dodCommittedByLab.toLocaleDateString()}]`
        )

      } catch (e) {
        const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        errors.push(`Row ${rowNum}: ${message}`)
        console.error(`Row ${rowNum}: ✗`, message)
      }
    }
  }

  // ── INVENTORY IMPORT ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (sheetType === 'inventory') {
    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      const row = rows[i]
      const rowNum = i + 2

      try {
        const name = rawStr(row['Material'])
        if (!name || name.toLowerCase() === 'material') {
          skipped++
          if (name) skippedDetails.push(`Row ${rowNum}: skipped header row`)
          else skippedDetails.push(`Row ${rowNum}: skipped — empty Material name`)
          continue
        }

        const catalogueNumber = rawStr(row['Catalogue Number'])
        const brand           = rawStr(row['Brand of Supply']) || null
        const type            = normaliseType(row['Type'])
        const labId           = resolveLabId(row['Lab'], targetLabId)
        const quantity        = safeQty(row['Available Quantity'])
        const unit            = rawStr(row['Unit of Qty']) || 'units'
        const mfgDate         = safeDate(row['Mfg Date'])
        const expiryDate      = safeDate(row['Expiry Date'])
        const category        = type === 'CONSUMABLE' ? 'Consumable' : 'Chemical'
        const notes           = catalogueNumber ? `Cat: ${catalogueNumber}` : null

        await prisma.inventoryItem.create({
          data: {
            name,
            brand,
            category,
            type,
            quantity,
            unit,
            minThreshold: 1,
            manufacturingDate: mfgDate,
            expiryDate,
            labId,
            notes,
          }
        })

        imported++
        console.log(`Row ${rowNum}: ✓ ${name} (${type}, qty: ${quantity} ${unit})`)

      } catch (e) {
        const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        errors.push(`Row ${rowNum}: ${message}`)
        console.error(`Row ${rowNum}: ✗`, message)
      }
    }
  }

  return res.status(200).json({
    imported,
    total: rows.length,
    skipped,
    skippedDetails,
    errors,
    message: `Imported ${imported} of ${rows.length} rows.${
      skipped > 0 ? ` ${skipped} skipped.` : ''
    }${errors.length > 0 ? ` ${errors.length} errors.` : ''}`
  })
}


===== pages\api\inventory\alerts.ts =====

// pages/api/inventory/alerts.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export type InventoryAlert = {
  id: number
  name: string
  quantity: number
  unit: string
  labName: string
  type: 'LOW_STOCK' | 'EXPIRY'
  detail: string
}

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })

  const items = await prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
    include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } }
  })

  const alerts: InventoryAlert[] = []
  const now = new Date()
  const thirtyDaysOut = new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)

  for (const item of items) {
    // Use minThreshold as the primary low-stock boundary throughout the system.
    // lowStockThreshold is an optional per-item override; falls back to minThreshold
    // when null so that both surfaces (alerts banner and inventory table) agree.
    // FIX: was using <= here while inventory/index.ts used <, causing the same item
    // to appear OK in the table and alarming in the banner simultaneously.
    // Both surfaces now use strictly-less-than (<).
    const effectiveThreshold = item.lowStockThreshold ?? item.minThreshold

    if (item.quantity < effectiveThreshold) {
      alerts.push({
        id:       item.id,
        name:     item.name,
        quantity: item.quantity,
        unit:     item.unit,
        labName:  item.lab.name,
        type:     'LOW_STOCK',
        detail:   `Only ${item.quantity} ${item.unit} remaining (threshold: ${effectiveThreshold})`,
      })
    }

    if (item.expiryDate && item.expiryDate <= thirtyDaysOut) {
      const daysLeft = Math.ceil((item.expiryDate.getTime() - now.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24))
      alerts.push({
        id:       item.id,
        name:     item.name,
        quantity: item.quantity,
        unit:     item.unit,
        labName:  item.lab.name,
        type:     'EXPIRY',
        detail:   daysLeft <= 0
          ? `Expired on ${item.expiryDate.toLocaleDateString()}`
          : `Expires in ${daysLeft} day${daysLeft === 1 ? '' : 's'}`,
      })
    }
  }

  return res.status(200).json(alerts)
}


===== pages\api\inventory\forecast.ts =====

// pages/api/inventory/forecast.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export type ForecastItem = {
  id: number; name: string; unit: string; currentStock: number
  avgDailyUse: number; daysUntilEmpty: number | null
  projectedStockAt30: number; projectedStockAt60: number; projectedStockAt90: number
  labName: string; threshold: number
}

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()
  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).end()

  const items = await prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
    include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } }
  })

  const since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
  const usage = await prisma.reagentLog.groupBy({
    by: ['itemId'],
    where: { usedAt: { gte: since } },
    _sum: { quantityUsed: true }
  })
  const usageMap = new Map(usage.map(u => [u.itemId, u._sum.quantityUsed ?? 0]))

  const forecast: ForecastItem[] = items.map(item => {
    const totalUsed30  = usageMap.get(item.id) ?? 0
    const avgDailyUse  = totalUsed30 / 30
    const daysUntilEmpty = avgDailyUse > 0 ? Math.floor(item.quantity / avgDailyUse) : null
    // FIX: was returning lowStockThreshold (nullable) while declared policy is that
    // minThreshold is the primary low-stock boundary. Return the same effective
    // threshold used by alerts.ts and inventory/index.ts: lowStockThreshold ?? minThreshold.
    const effectiveThreshold = item.lowStockThreshold ?? item.minThreshold
    return {
      id: item.id, name: item.name, unit: item.unit,
      currentStock: item.quantity, avgDailyUse: parseFloat(avgDailyUse.toFixed(3)),
      daysUntilEmpty,
      projectedStockAt30: Math.max(0, parseFloat((item.quantity - avgDailyUse * 30).toFixed(2))),
      projectedStockAt60: Math.max(0, parseFloat((item.quantity - avgDailyUse * 60).toFixed(2))),
      projectedStockAt90: Math.max(0, parseFloat((item.quantity - avgDailyUse * 90).toFixed(2))),
      labName: item.lab.name, threshold: effectiveThreshold,
    }
  })

  forecast.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.daysUntilEmpty === null && b.daysUntilEmpty === null) return 0
    if (a.daysUntilEmpty === null) return 1
    if (b.daysUntilEmpty === null) return -1
    return a.daysUntilEmpty - b.daysUntilEmpty
  })
  return res.status(200).json(forecast)
}


===== pages\api\inventory\import.ts =====

// pages/api/inventory/import.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../lib/audit'
import type { ImportRow } from '../../../lib/inventoryImportUtils'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'POST') {
    return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
  }

  const {
    rows,
    labId,
    minThreshold  = 10,
    clearExisting = false,
  } = req.body as {
    rows:          ImportRow[]
    labId:         number
    minThreshold:  number
    clearExisting: boolean
  }

  if (!rows || !Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No rows provided' })
  }

  const parsedLabId = Number(labId)
  if (!parsedLabId || isNaN(parsedLabId)) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Please select a valid lab' })
  }

  try {
    if (clearExisting) {
      await prisma.inventoryItem.deleteMany({ where: { labId: parsedLabId } })
    }

    const created = await prisma.$transaction(
      rows.map(row =>
        prisma.inventoryItem.create({
          data: {
            name:              row.name,
            brand:             row.brand      || null,
            category:          row.category,
            type:              row.type,
            quantity:          row.quantity,
            unit:              row.unit,
            minThreshold:      Number(minThreshold),
            manufacturingDate: row.manufacturingDate ? new Date(row.manufacturingDate) : null,
            expiryDate:        row.expiryDate        ? new Date(row.expiryDate)        : null,
            labId:             parsedLabId,
            notes:             row.catalogueNumber   ? `Cat: ${row.catalogueNumber}`   : null,
          },
        })
      )
    )

    await writeAudit({
      action:        'INVENTORY_IMPORTED',
      entityType:    'InventoryItem',
      entityId:      0,
      detail:        `Imported ${created.length} items into lab ${parsedLabId}`,
      performedById: parseInt((session.user as { id: string }).id),
    })

    return res.status(200).json({ imported: created.length })
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    console.error('Import error:', err)
    return res.status(500).json({ error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Import failed' })
  }
}


===== pages\api\inventory\index.ts =====

// pages/api/inventory/index.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../lib/audit'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const user = session.user as { id: string }

  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    try {
      const allowed = ['ADMIN', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER', 'MANAGEMENT']
      if (!allowed.includes(role)) {
        return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
      }

      const items = await prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
        include: { lab: { select: { id: true, name: true } } },
        orderBy: [{ labId: 'asc' }, { type: 'asc' }, { category: 'asc' }, { name: 'asc' }]
      })

      // Low-stock rule (unified with alerts.ts):
      //   effectiveThreshold = lowStockThreshold ?? minThreshold
      //   isLow              = quantity < effectiveThreshold  (strictly less than)
      // FIX: previously used only minThreshold with < here, while alerts.ts used
      // lowStockThreshold ?? minThreshold with <=. Both surfaces now use the same
      // field and the same operator so they agree on every item's status.
      const flagged = items.map(item => {
        const effectiveThreshold = item.lowStockThreshold ?? item.minThreshold
        return {
          ...item,
          isLow: item.quantity < effectiveThreshold,
          isExpiringSoon: item.expiryDate
            ? new Date(item.expiryDate) < new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
            : false,
        }
      })

      return res.status(200).json(flagged)
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
      console.error('GET /api/inventory error:', err)
      return res.status(500).json({ error: message })
    }
  }

  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
    }

    const {
      name, brand, category, type, quantity, unit, minThreshold,
      expiryDate, manufacturingDate, labId, notes
    } = req.body as {
      name: string
      brand?: string
      category: string
      type?: string
      quantity: number
      unit: string
      minThreshold: number
      expiryDate?: string
      manufacturingDate?: string
      labId: number
      notes?: string
    }

    const item = await prisma.inventoryItem.create({
      data: {
        name,
        brand: brand ?? null,
        category,
        type: type ?? 'CHEMICAL',
        quantity,
        unit,
        minThreshold,
        expiryDate: expiryDate ? new Date(expiryDate) : null,
        manufacturingDate: manufacturingDate ? new Date(manufacturingDate) : null,
        labId,
        notes: notes ?? null,
      },
      include: { lab: { select: { id: true, name: true } } }
    })

    await writeAudit({
      action:        'INVENTORY_CREATED',
      entityType:    'InventoryItem',
      entityId:      item.id,
      detail:        `${item.name} added to ${item.lab.name}`,
      performedById: parseInt(user.id),
    })

    return res.status(201).json(item)
  }

  if (req.method === 'PATCH') {
    if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
    }

    const { id, ...updates } = req.body as {
      id: number
      name?: string
      brand?: string
      category?: string
      type?: string
      quantity?: number
      unit?: string
      minThreshold?: number
      expiryDate?: string | null
      manufacturingDate?: string | null
      notes?: string | null
    }

    if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'id is required' })

    const item = await prisma.inventoryItem.update({
      where: { id: parseInt(String(id)) },
      data: {
        ...(updates.name !== undefined && { name: updates.name }),
        ...(updates.brand !== undefined && { brand: updates.brand }),
        ...(updates.category !== undefined && { category: updates.category }),
        ...(updates.type !== undefined && { type: updates.type }),
        ...(updates.quantity !== undefined && { quantity: updates.quantity }),
        ...(updates.unit !== undefined && { unit: updates.unit }),
        ...(updates.minThreshold !== undefined && { minThreshold: updates.minThreshold }),
        ...(updates.expiryDate !== undefined && {
          expiryDate: updates.expiryDate ? new Date(updates.expiryDate) : null
        }),
        ...(updates.manufacturingDate !== undefined && {
          manufacturingDate: updates.manufacturingDate ? new Date(updates.manufacturingDate) : null
        }),
        ...(updates.notes !== undefined && { notes: updates.notes }),
      },
      include: { lab: { select: { id: true, name: true } } }
    })

    await writeAudit({
      action:        'INVENTORY_UPDATED',
      entityType:    'InventoryItem',
      entityId:      item.id,
      detail:        `${item.name} updated`,
      performedById: parseInt(user.id),
    })

    return res.status(200).json(item)
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}


===== pages\api\inventory\log.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    const { itemId, requestId, quantityUsed, purpose } = req.body as {
      itemId: number
      requestId?: number
      quantityUsed: number
      purpose: string
    }

    const userId = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string

    if (!itemId || !quantityUsed || !purpose) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'itemId, quantityUsed, and purpose are required' })
    }
    if (quantityUsed <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'quantityUsed must be greater than zero' })
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction([
      prisma.reagentLog.create({
        data: {
          itemId,
          requestId: requestId ?? null,
          usedById: parseInt(userId),
          quantityUsed,
          purpose,
        }
      }),
      prisma.inventoryItem.update({
        where: { id: itemId },
        data: { quantity: { decrement: quantityUsed } }
      })
    ])

    return res.status(201).json(result)
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}


===== pages\api\inventory\transfer.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const userId = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string

  // ── POST: create a new transfer request ──────────────────────────────────
  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    const { fromItemId, toLabId, quantity, reason } = req.body as {
      fromItemId: number
      toLabId: number
      quantity: number
      reason?: string
    }

    if (!fromItemId || !toLabId || !quantity) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'fromItemId, toLabId, and quantity are required' })
    }
    if (quantity <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'quantity must be greater than zero' })
    }

    // Load source item
    const sourceItem = await prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: { id: fromItemId },
      include: { lab: { select: { id: true, name: true } } },
    })
    if (!sourceItem) {
      return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Source inventory item not found' })
    }
    if (sourceItem.labId === toLabId) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Source and destination lab must be different' })
    }
    if (sourceItem.quantity < quantity) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: `Insufficient stock. Available: ${sourceItem.quantity} ${sourceItem.unit}`,
      })
    }

    // Verify destination lab exists
    const destLab = await prisma.lab.findUnique({ where: { id: toLabId } })
    if (!destLab) {
      return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Destination lab not found' })
    }

    // Find an existing item with the same name in the destination lab,
    // or create a placeholder with quantity 0 that will be incremented on approval.
    let destItem = await prisma.inventoryItem.findFirst({
      where: {
        name: sourceItem.name,
        labId: toLabId,
      },
    })

    if (!destItem) {
      destItem = await prisma.inventoryItem.create({
        data: {
          name: sourceItem.name,
          brand: sourceItem.brand,
          category: sourceItem.category,
          type: sourceItem.type,
          quantity: 0,
          unit: sourceItem.unit,
          minThreshold: sourceItem.minThreshold,
          manufacturingDate: sourceItem.manufacturingDate,
          expiryDate: sourceItem.expiryDate,
          notes: sourceItem.notes,
          labId: toLabId,
        },
      })
    }

    const transfer = await prisma.inventoryTransfer.create({
      data: {
        fromItemId,
        toItemId: destItem.id,
        quantity,
        reason: reason ?? null,
        status: 'PENDING',
        requestedById: parseInt(userId),
      },
      include: {
        fromItem: { include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } } },
        toItem: { include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } } },
      },
    })

    return res.status(201).json(transfer)
  }

  // ── PUT: approve a pending transfer ──────────────────────────────────────
  if (req.method === 'PUT') {
    if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
    }

    const { transferId } = req.body as { transferId: number }
    if (!transferId) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'transferId is required' })
    }

    const transfer = await prisma.inventoryTransfer.findUnique({
      where: { id: transferId },
    })
    if (!transfer) {
      return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Transfer not found' })
    }
    if (transfer.status !== 'PENDING') {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Only PENDING transfers can be completed' })
    }

    const sourceItem = await prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: { id: transfer.fromItemId },
    })
    if (!sourceItem || sourceItem.quantity < transfer.quantity) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Insufficient stock at time of approval' })
    }

    const result = await prisma.$transaction([
      prisma.inventoryItem.update({
        where: { id: transfer.fromItemId },
        data: { quantity: { decrement: transfer.quantity } },
      }),
      prisma.inventoryItem.update({
        where: { id: transfer.toItemId },
        data: { quantity: { increment: transfer.quantity } },
      }),
      prisma.inventoryTransfer.update({
        where: { id: transferId },
        data: {
          status: 'COMPLETED',
          approvedById: parseInt(userId),
          completedAt: new Date(),
        },
      }),
    ])

    return res.status(200).json(result)
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}


===== pages\api\inventory\clusters\deduct.ts =====

// pages/api/inventory/clusters/deduct.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../../lib/audit'

// FIX: Removed the ExtendedPrismaClient type alias and 'db' variable.
// Previously the file defined:
//   const db = prisma as unknown as ExtendedPrismaClient
// but then the actual Prisma calls used the original 'prisma' client directly,
// making the type alias provide no real type safety. The db alias was used
// only for findUnique which also used 'as Record<string, unknown>' args,
// defeating any benefit. All calls now use 'prisma' directly.

type ClusterItemWithItem = {
  itemId: number
  qtyPerUnit: number
  item: {
    id: number
    name: string
    unit: string
    quantity: number
  }
}

type ClusterWithItems = {
  id: number
  name: string
  unit: string
  items: ClusterItemWithItem[]
}

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  try {
    const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
    if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

    const role   = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
    const userId = parseInt((session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string)

    const allowed = ['ADMIN', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST']
    if (!allowed.includes(role)) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })

    if (req.method !== 'POST') return res.status(405).end()

    const { clusterId, units, notes, projectCode, requestId } = req.body as {
      clusterId:    number
      units:        number
      notes?:       string
      projectCode?: string
      requestId?:   number
    }

    if (!clusterId || !units || units <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'clusterId and positive units are required' })
    }

    // Load the cluster with all its items using raw prisma (no type alias needed)
    const cluster = await (prisma as unknown as {
      consumptionCluster: {
        findUnique: (args: object) => Promise<ClusterWithItems | null>
      }
    }).consumptionCluster.findUnique({
      where: { id: clusterId },
      include: {
        items: {
          include: { item: true }
        }
      }
    } as object)

    if (!cluster) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Cluster not found' })

    // Pre-check: verify sufficient stock for all items
    const stockErrors: string[] = []
    for (const ci of cluster.items) {
      const totalNeeded = ci.qtyPerUnit * units
      if (ci.item.quantity < totalNeeded) {
        stockErrors.push(
          `"${ci.item.name}": need ${totalNeeded.toFixed(2)} ${ci.item.unit}, only ${ci.item.quantity} available`
        )
      }
    }

    if (stockErrors.length > 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Insufficient stock for some items',
        details: stockErrors
      })
    }

    const inventoryUpdates = cluster.items.map((ci: ClusterItemWithItem) => {
      const totalUsed = parseFloat((ci.qtyPerUnit * units).toPrecision(10))
      return prisma.inventoryItem.update({
        where: { id: ci.itemId },
        data:  { quantity: { decrement: totalUsed } },
      })
    })

    const reagentLogCreates = cluster.items.map((ci: ClusterItemWithItem) => {
      const totalUsed = parseFloat((ci.qtyPerUnit * units).toPrecision(10))
      return prisma.reagentLog.create({
        data: {
          itemId:       ci.itemId,
          // requestId is intentionally optional — deductions may occur outside
          // of a formal approved request (maintenance, calibration, etc.)
          requestId:    requestId ?? null,
          usedById:     userId,
          quantityUsed: totalUsed,
          purpose: `Cluster deduction: ${cluster.name} × ${units} ${cluster.unit}(s)${notes ? ` — ${notes}` : ''}`,
        },
      })
    })

    // Build deduction data with typed approach (no as-any cast needed)
    const deductionData: {
      cluster:       { connect: { id: number } }
      performedBy:   { connect: { id: number } }
      request?:      { connect: { id: number } }
      units:         number
      notes:         string | null
      projectCode?:  string
    } = {
      cluster:     { connect: { id: clusterId } },
      performedBy: { connect: { id: userId } },
      units,
      notes:       notes ?? null,
    }

    if (requestId) {
      deductionData.request = { connect: { id: requestId } }
    }
    if (projectCode) {
      deductionData.projectCode = projectCode
    }

    // Execute stock decrements + reagent logs + deduction record atomically
    await prisma.$transaction([
      ...inventoryUpdates,
      ...reagentLogCreates,
      prisma.consumptionDeduction.create({ data: deductionData }),
    ])

    await writeAudit({
      action:      'CLUSTER_DEDUCTION',
      entityType:  'ConsumptionCluster',
      entityId:    clusterId,
      detail:      `Deducted ${units} ${cluster.unit}(s) from cluster "${cluster.name}"`,
      performedById: userId,
    })

    // Return updated stock levels
    const updatedItems = await prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
      where:  { id: { in: cluster.items.map((ci: ClusterItemWithItem) => ci.itemId) } },
      select: { id: true, name: true, quantity: true, unit: true },
    })

    return res.status(200).json({
      ok:      true,
      cluster: cluster.name,
      units,
      deducted: cluster.items.map((ci: ClusterItemWithItem) => ({
        name:      ci.item.name,
        deducted:  ci.qtyPerUnit * units,
        unit:      ci.item.unit,
        remaining: updatedItems.find(u => u.id === ci.itemId)?.quantity ?? 0,
      })),
    })
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('DEDUCT ERROR:', err)
    return res.status(500).json({ error: String(err) })
  }
}


===== pages\api\inventory\clusters\index.ts =====

// pages/api/inventory/clusters/index.ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../../lib/audit'
import type { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'

type ExtendedPrismaClient = PrismaClient & {
  consumptionCluster: {
    findMany: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>[]>
    create: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown> & { id: number; name: string }>
    update: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>
  }
}

const db = prisma as unknown as ExtendedPrismaClient

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const userId = parseInt((session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string)

  const allowed = ['ADMIN', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST']
  if (!allowed.includes(role)) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })

  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    try {
      const clusters = await db.consumptionCluster.findMany({
        include: {
          experiment: { select: { id: true, name: true } },
          items: {
            include: {
              item: {
                include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } }
              }
            }
          },
          _count: { select: { deductions: true } }
        },
        orderBy: [{ sortOrder: 'asc' }, { createdAt: 'asc' }]
      } as Record<string, unknown>)
      return res.status(200).json(clusters)
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('CLUSTER GET ERROR:', e)
      return res.status(500).json({ error: String(e) })
    }
  }

  if (req.method === 'POST') {
    if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Only INVENTORY_MANAGER or ADMIN can create clusters' })
    }

    const { name, description, unit, experimentId, items } = req.body as {
      name: string
      description?: string
      unit: string
      experimentId?: number
      items: Array<{ itemId: number; qtyPerUnit: number }>
    }

    if (!name || !items || items.length === 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'name and at least one item are required' })
    }

    try {
      const cluster = await db.consumptionCluster.create({
        data: {
          name,
          description: description ?? null,
          unit: unit ?? 'sample',
          experimentId: experimentId ?? null,
          items: {
            create: items.map((i: { itemId: number; qtyPerUnit: number }) => ({
              itemId: i.itemId,
              qtyPerUnit: i.qtyPerUnit,
            }))
          }
        },
        include: {
          experiment: { select: { id: true, name: true } },
          items: {
            include: {
              item: { include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } } }
            }
          }
        }
      } as Record<string, unknown>)

      await writeAudit({
        action: 'CLUSTER_CREATED',
        entityType: 'ConsumptionCluster',
        entityId: cluster.id,
        detail: `Cluster "${cluster.name}" created with ${items.length} items`,
        performedById: userId,
      })

      return res.status(201).json(cluster)
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('CLUSTER CREATE ERROR:', e)
      return res.status(500).json({ error: String(e) })
    }
  }

  if (req.method === 'PATCH') {
    if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Only INVENTORY_MANAGER or ADMIN can reorder clusters' })
    }

    const { updates } = req.body as { updates: Array<{ id: number; sortOrder: number }> }
    if (!Array.isArray(updates)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'updates array required' })
    }

    try {
      await Promise.all(
        updates.map(u =>
          db.consumptionCluster.update({
            where: { id: u.id },
            data: { sortOrder: u.sortOrder },
          } as Record<string, unknown>)
        )
      )
      return res.status(200).json({ ok: true })
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('CLUSTER REORDER ERROR:', e)
      return res.status(500).json({ error: String(e) })
    }
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}


===== pages\api\inventory\clusters\[id].ts =====

// pages/api/inventory/clusters/[id].ts
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'
import { writeAudit } from '../../../../lib/audit'
import type { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'

type ExtendedPrismaClient = PrismaClient & {
  consumptionCluster: {
    findUnique: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown> | null>
    update: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>
    delete: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>
  }
  consumptionClusterItem: {
    deleteMany: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>
  }
  consumptionDeduction: {
    deleteMany: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>
  }
}

const db = prisma as unknown as ExtendedPrismaClient

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  const userId = parseInt((session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id as string)

  const id = parseInt(req.query.id as string)
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid ID' })

  if (req.method === 'GET') {
    const cluster = await db.consumptionCluster.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      include: {
        experiment: { select: { id: true, name: true } },
        items: {
          include: {
            item: {
              include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } }
            }
          }
        },
        deductions: {
          // FIX #2: was `performedAt` in query but schema had `deductedAt` —
          // schema is now renamed to `performedAt` so this is now correct.
          orderBy: { performedAt: 'desc' },
          take: 20,
        }
      }
    } as Record<string, unknown>)
    if (!cluster) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' })
    return res.status(200).json(cluster)
  }

  if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method === 'PUT') {
    const { name, description, unit, experimentId, items } = req.body as {
      name: string
      description?: string
      unit: string
      experimentId?: number | null
      items: Array<{ itemId: number; qtyPerUnit: number }>
    }

    // Delete old items and recreate
    await db.consumptionClusterItem.deleteMany({ where: { clusterId: id } } as Record<string, unknown>)

    const cluster = await db.consumptionCluster.update({
      where: { id },
      data: {
        name,
        description: description ?? null,
        // FIX #1: field is `unit` in schema (was basisUnit) — already aligned
        unit,
        experimentId: experimentId ?? null,
        items: {
          create: items.map((i: { itemId: number; qtyPerUnit: number }) => ({
            itemId: i.itemId,
            qtyPerUnit: i.qtyPerUnit,
          }))
        }
      },
      include: {
        experiment: { select: { id: true, name: true } },
        items: {
          include: { item: { include: { lab: { select: { name: true } } } } }
        }
      }
    } as Record<string, unknown>)

    await writeAudit({
      action: 'CLUSTER_UPDATED',
      entityType: 'ConsumptionCluster',
      entityId: id,
      detail: `Cluster "${name}" updated`,
      performedById: userId,
    })

    return res.status(200).json(cluster)
  }

  if (req.method === 'DELETE') {
    await db.consumptionClusterItem.deleteMany({ where: { clusterId: id } } as Record<string, unknown>)
    await db.consumptionDeduction.deleteMany({ where: { clusterId: id } } as Record<string, unknown>)
    await db.consumptionCluster.delete({ where: { id } } as Record<string, unknown>)

    await writeAudit({
      action: 'CLUSTER_DELETED',
      entityType: 'ConsumptionCluster',
      entityId: id,
      detail: `Cluster #${id} deleted`,
      performedById: userId,
    })

    return res.status(204).end()
  }

  return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })
}


===== pages\api\inventory\logs\export.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'

function escapeCsv(value: unknown): string {
  const str = value === null || value === undefined ? '' : String(value)
  if (str.includes(',') || str.includes('"') || str.includes('\n')) {
    return '"' + str.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"'
  }
  return str
}

export default async function handler(
  req: NextApiRequest,
  res: NextApiResponse
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).end()

  const { from, to, itemId } = req.query
  const where: Record<string, unknown> = {}

  if (from || to) {
    where.usedAt = {
      ...(from ? { gte: new Date(from as string) } : {}),
      ...(to   ? { lte: new Date(to   as string) } : {}),
    }
  }
  if (itemId) {
    where.itemId = parseInt(itemId as string)
  }

  const logs = await prisma.reagentLog.findMany({
    where,
    include: {
      item:    { select: { name: true, unit: true } },
      usedBy:  { select: { name: true } },
      request: { select: { requestCode: true, sampleName: true } },
    },
    orderBy: { usedAt: 'desc' },
    take: 5000,
  })

  const headers = [
    'Date', 'Reagent', 'Quantity Used', 'Unit',
    'Purpose', 'Request Code', 'Sample Name', 'Used By'
  ]

  const csvRows = [
    headers.join(','),
    ...logs.map(log => [
      escapeCsv(new Date(log.usedAt).toLocaleDateString('en-GB')),
      escapeCsv(log.item.name),
      escapeCsv(log.quantityUsed),
      escapeCsv(log.item.unit),
      escapeCsv(log.purpose),
      escapeCsv(log.request?.requestCode ?? ''),
      escapeCsv(log.request?.sampleName ?? ''),
      escapeCsv(log.usedBy.name),
    ].join(','))
  ]

  const csv = csvRows.join('\n')
  const filename = `reagent-logs-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`

  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/csv')
  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`)
  return res.status(200).send(csv)
}


===== pages\api\inventory\logs\index.ts =====

import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next'
import { getServerSession } from 'next-auth/next'
import { authOptions } from '../../auth/[...nextauth]'
import { prisma } from '../../../../lib/prisma'

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  const session = await getServerSession(req, res, authOptions)
  if (!session) return res.status(401).end()

  const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
  if (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' })
  }

  if (req.method !== 'GET') return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' })

  const { from, to, itemId } = req.query

  const where: Record<string, unknown> = {}

  if (from || to) {
    where.usedAt = {
      ...(from ? { gte: new Date(from as string) } : {}),
