      setEditError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to update')
    } finally { setEditSaving(false) }
  }

  async function handleDelete(id: number, name: string) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete cluster "${name}"? This cannot be undone.`)) return
    await fetch(`/api/inventory/clusters/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' })
    loadClusters()
  }

  async function handleDeduct(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!deductCluster) return

    if (!deductProjectCode.trim()) {
      setDeductError('Project Code is required.')
      return
    }

    setDeducting(true); setDeductError(''); setDeductResult(null)

    let resolvedRequestId: number | null = null
    try {
      const lookup = await fetch(`/api/requests?projectCode=${encodeURIComponent(deductProjectCode.trim())}`)
      const lookupData = await lookup.json()
      const list = Array.isArray(lookupData) ? lookupData : (lookupData.requests ?? [])
      if (list.length > 0) {
        resolvedRequestId = list[0].id
      }
    } catch { /* silently ignore */ }

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/inventory/clusters/deduct', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          clusterId: deductCluster.id,
          units: parseFloat(deductUnits),
          notes: deductProjectCode.trim(),
          projectCode: deductProjectCode.trim(),
          requestId: resolvedRequestId ?? undefined,
        })
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (data.details) setDeductError(data.error + ':\n' + data.details.join('\n'))
        else throw new Error(data.error)
        return
      }
      setDeductResult(data)
      loadClusters()
      fetch('/api/inventory').then(r => r.json()).then(d => setItems(Array.isArray(d) ? d : [])).catch(() => {})
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      setDeductError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Deduction failed')
    } finally { setDeducting(false) }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div style={{ padding: 32, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>
        <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
          style={{ animation: 'spin 1s linear infinite' }}>
          <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.3}/>
          <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 00-9-9" strokeLinecap="round"/>
        </svg>
        Loading clusters…
        <style>{`@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }`}</style>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const totalDeductions = clusters.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (c._count?.deductions ?? 0), 0)

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '24px 28px', maxWidth: '100%' }}>

      {/* ── KPI Strip ── */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <KpiCard label="Total Clusters" value={clusters.length} sub="Consumption templates" accent="#10b981" />
        <KpiCard label="Total Deductions" value={totalDeductions} sub="Logged deductions" accent="#f59e0b" />
        <KpiCard
          label="Items Tracked"
          value={clusters.reduce((s, c) => s + c.items.length, 0)}
          sub="Across all clusters"
          accent="#3b82f6"
        />
        <KpiCard
          label="Low Stock Alerts"
          value={clusters.reduce((s, c) => s + c.items.filter(ci =>
            ci.qtyPerUnit > 0 && Math.floor(ci.item.quantity / ci.qtyPerUnit) < 10
          ).length, 0)}
          sub="Items running low"
          accent="#ef4444"
        />
      </div>

      {/* ── Page header ── */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 20, flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12 }}>
        <div>
          <button
            onClick={() => router.push('/inventory')}
            style={{
              fontSize: 12, color: '#10b981', background: 'none', border: 'none',
              cursor: 'pointer', padding: 0, fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 4, display: 'block',
            }}
          >
            ← Back to Inventory
          </button>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>Consumption Clusters</p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '4px 0 0' }}>
            Define how much each chemical / consumable is used per sample or datapoint, then deduct in bulk.
          </p>
        </div>
        {canManage && (
          <button
            onClick={() => { setShowCreate(!showCreate); setEditClusterId(null) }}
            style={{
              padding: '8px 18px',
              background: showCreate ? 'rgba(241,245,249,0.9)' : '#10b981',
              color: showCreate ? '#374151' : '#fff',
              border: showCreate ? '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)' : 'none',
              borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,
              cursor: 'pointer', transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
            }}
          >
            {showCreate ? 'Cancel' : '+ New Cluster'}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ── Create form ── */}
      {showCreate && canManage && (
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(191,219,254,0.8)',
          borderRadius: 20,
          boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
          overflow: 'hidden', marginBottom: 20,
        }}>
          <div style={{
            padding: '14px 20px',
            background: 'rgba(239,246,255,0.7)',
            borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(191,219,254,0.6)',
          }}>
            <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e40af' }}>Create New Consumption Cluster</p>
          </div>
          <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            <form onSubmit={handleCreate}>
              {createError && (
                <div style={{
                  background: '#fef2f2', border: '1px solid rgba(254,202,202,0.8)',
                  borderRadius: 10, padding: '10px 14px', marginBottom: 16,
                  fontSize: 13, color: '#991b1b', whiteSpace: 'pre-line',
                }}>
                  {createError}
                </div>
              )}
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr 1fr', gap: 14, marginBottom: 16 }}>
                <div>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Cluster Name *</label>
                  <input
                    required value={clusterForm.name}
                    onChange={e => setClusterForm({ ...clusterForm, name: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="e.g. DNA Extraction"
                    style={inputStyle}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Unit Basis</label>
                  <select
                    value={clusterForm.unit}
                    onChange={e => setClusterForm({ ...clusterForm, unit: e.target.value })}
                    style={inputStyle}
                  >
                    <option value="sample">Per Sample</option>
                    <option value="datapoint">Per Datapoint</option>
                    <option value="reaction">Per Reaction</option>
                    <option value="plate">Per Plate</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Linked Experiment</label>
                  <select
                    value={clusterForm.experimentId}
                    onChange={e => setClusterForm({ ...clusterForm, experimentId: e.target.value })}
                    style={inputStyle}
                  >
                    <option value="">None</option>
                    {experiments.map(ex => <option key={ex.id} value={ex.id}>{ex.name}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1' }}>
                  <label style={labelStyle}>Description</label>
                  <input
                    value={clusterForm.description}
                    onChange={e => setClusterForm({ ...clusterForm, description: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Optional description…"
                    style={inputStyle}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <ClusterRowEditor
                rows={clusterRows} items={items} unitLabel={clusterForm.unit}
                onAddRow={makeAddRow(clusterRows, setClusterRows)}
                onRemoveRow={makeRemoveRow(clusterRows, setClusterRows)}
                onUpdateRow={makeUpdateRow(clusterRows, setClusterRows)}
              />
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10, marginTop: 16 }}>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowCreate(false)} style={{
                  padding: '7px 18px', fontSize: 13,
                  border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                  background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151',
                  cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 500,
                }}>
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button type="submit" disabled={createSaving} style={{
                  padding: '7px 20px', fontSize: 13, background: '#10b981',
                  color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 10,
                  fontWeight: 600, cursor: createSaving ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                  opacity: createSaving ? 0.6 : 1,
                }}>
                  {createSaving ? 'Creating…' : 'Create Cluster'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Clusters list ── */}
      {clusters.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
          borderRadius: 20,
          padding: 64, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8',
          boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
        }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 40, marginBottom: 12 }}>⚗</div>
          <p style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#374151', margin: 0 }}>No consumption clusters yet</p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, marginTop: 6 }}>
            Create a cluster to define how much each material is consumed per sample or datapoint.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
          {clusters.map((cluster, clusterIndex) => (
            <div key={cluster.id} style={{
              background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
              backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
              WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
              borderRadius: 20,
              boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
              overflow: 'hidden',
            }}>

              {/* Cluster header */}
              <div style={{
                padding: '16px 20px',
                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between',
                background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.7)',
                borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
              }}>
                <div>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                    <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                      {cluster.name}
                    </p>
                    <span style={{
                      fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
                      background: 'rgba(239,246,255,0.9)', color: '#1d4ed8',
                      padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 20, letterSpacing: '0.03em',
                    }}>
                      per {cluster.unit}
                    </span>
                    {cluster.experiment && (
                      <span style={{
                        fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
                        background: 'rgba(245,243,255,0.9)', color: '#7e22ce',
                        padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 20,
                      }}>
                        {cluster.experiment.name}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  {cluster.description && (
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0', fontSize: 13, color: '#64748b' }}>{cluster.description}</p>
                  )}
                  <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0', fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                    {cluster.items.length} item{cluster.items.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} · {cluster._count?.deductions ?? 0} deduction{(cluster._count?.deductions ?? 0) !== 1 ? 's' : ''} logged
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexShrink: 0, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      setDeductCluster(cluster)
                      setDeductUnits('')
                      setDeductProjectCode('')
                      setDeductResolvedRequestId(null)
                      setDeductResult(null)
                      setDeductError('')
                    }}
                    style={{
                      padding: '6px 14px', background: '#10b981', color: '#fff',
                      border: 'none', borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                      cursor: 'pointer', transition: 'background 0.15s ease',
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = '#059669')}
                    onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = '#10b981')}
                  >
                    ↓ Deduct Usage
                  </button>
                  {canManage && (
                    <>
                      {/* ── Sort buttons ── */}
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleReorder(cluster.id, 'up')}
                        disabled={clusterIndex === 0}
                        title="Move up"
                        style={{
                          padding: '6px 10px', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700,
                          border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151',
                          cursor: clusterIndex === 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                          opacity: clusterIndex === 0 ? 0.35 : 1,
                          transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                      >
                        ↑
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleReorder(cluster.id, 'down')}
                        disabled={clusterIndex === clusters.length - 1}
                        title="Move down"
                        style={{
                          padding: '6px 10px', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700,
                          border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151',
                          cursor: clusterIndex === clusters.length - 1 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                          opacity: clusterIndex === clusters.length - 1 ? 0.35 : 1,
                          transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                      >
                        ↓
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => editClusterId === cluster.id ? setEditClusterId(null) : startEdit(cluster)}
                        style={{
                          padding: '6px 14px', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                          border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151', cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                      >
                        {editClusterId === cluster.id ? 'Cancel Edit' : 'Edit'}
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleDelete(cluster.id, cluster.name)}
                        style={{
                          padding: '6px 14px', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                          border: '1px solid rgba(254,202,202,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#dc2626', cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                      >
                        Delete
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* ── Items table ── */}
              <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
                <table style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: 13, borderCollapse: 'collapse' }}>
                  <thead>
                    <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                      {[
                        'Material', 'Type', 'Lab',
                        `Qty / ${cluster.unit}`, 'Unit',
                        'Current Stock', `Max ${cluster.unit}s`,
                      ].map((h, i) => (
                        <th key={i} style={{
                          padding: '10px 16px',
                          textAlign: (i === 3 || i === 5 || i === 6) ? 'right' : 'left',
                          fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
                          color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                          whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                        }}>
                          {h}
                        </th>
                      ))}
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {cluster.items.map((ci, i) => {
                      const maxUnits = ci.qtyPerUnit > 0 ? Math.floor(ci.item.quantity / ci.qtyPerUnit) : null
                      const isLimiting = maxUnits !== null && maxUnits < 10
                      return (
                        <tr key={ci.id} style={{
                          borderBottom: i < cluster.items.length - 1 ? '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)' : 'none',
                          background: isLimiting
                            ? 'rgba(254,242,242,0.5)'
                            : i % 2 === 0 ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)',
                          transition: 'background 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                          onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(236,253,245,0.7)')}
                          onMouseLeave={e => {
                            e.currentTarget.style.background = isLimiting
                              ? 'rgba(254,242,242,0.5)'
                              : i % 2 === 0 ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)'
                          }}
                        >
                          <td style={{ padding: '11px 16px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f172a' }}>{ci.item.name}</td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '11px 16px' }}>
                            <span style={{
                              fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
                              padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 20,
                              background: ci.item.type === 'CHEMICAL' ? 'rgba(245,243,255,0.9)' : 'rgba(240,253,250,0.9)',
                              color: ci.item.type === 'CHEMICAL' ? '#7e22ce' : '#0f766e',
                            }}>
                              {ci.item.type === 'CHEMICAL' ? 'Chemical' : 'Consumable'}
                            </span>
                          </td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '11px 16px', fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b' }}>{ci.item.lab.name}</td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '11px 16px', textAlign: 'right', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums', color: '#374151' }}>
                            {ci.qtyPerUnit}
                          </td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '11px 16px', fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                            {ci.item.unit}
                          </td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '11px 16px', textAlign: 'right', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
                            <span style={{
                              fontWeight: ci.item.quantity < ci.qtyPerUnit * 5 ? 700 : 400,
                              color: ci.item.quantity < ci.qtyPerUnit * 5 ? '#f59e0b' : '#374151',
                            }}>
                              {ci.item.quantity}
                            </span>
                          </td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '11px 16px', textAlign: 'right', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
                            {maxUnits === null ? (
                              <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>—</span>
                            ) : (
                              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: isLimiting ? '#dc2626' : '#10b981' }}>
                                {maxUnits}{isLimiting && ' ⚠'}
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      )
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>

              {/* Edit form (inline) */}
              {editClusterId === cluster.id && canManage && (
                <div style={{
                  borderTop: '1px solid rgba(191,219,254,0.6)',
                  background: 'rgba(239,246,255,0.4)',
                  padding: 20,
                }}>
                  <p style={{ margin: '0 0 14px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e40af' }}>Edit Cluster</p>
                  <form onSubmit={handleEdit}>
                    {editError && (
                      <div style={{
                        background: '#fef2f2', border: '1px solid rgba(254,202,202,0.8)',
                        borderRadius: 10, padding: '10px 14px', marginBottom: 14,
                        fontSize: 13, color: '#991b1b',
                      }}>
                        {editError}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr 1fr', gap: 14, marginBottom: 16 }}>
                      <div>
                        <label style={labelStyle}>Cluster Name *</label>
                        <input required value={editForm.name}
                          onChange={e => setEditForm({ ...editForm, name: e.target.value })}
                          style={inputStyle} />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label style={labelStyle}>Unit Basis</label>
                        <select value={editForm.unit}
                          onChange={e => setEditForm({ ...editForm, unit: e.target.value })}
                          style={inputStyle}>
                          <option value="sample">Per Sample</option>
                          <option value="datapoint">Per Datapoint</option>
                          <option value="reaction">Per Reaction</option>
                          <option value="plate">Per Plate</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label style={labelStyle}>Linked Experiment</label>
                        <select value={editForm.experimentId}
                          onChange={e => setEditForm({ ...editForm, experimentId: e.target.value })}
                          style={inputStyle}>
                          <option value="">None</option>
                          {experiments.map(ex => <option key={ex.id} value={ex.id}>{ex.name}</option>)}
                        </select>
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1' }}>
                        <label style={labelStyle}>Description</label>
                        <input value={editForm.description}
                          onChange={e => setEditForm({ ...editForm, description: e.target.value })}
                          style={inputStyle} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <ClusterRowEditor
                      rows={editRows} items={items} unitLabel={editForm.unit}
                      onAddRow={makeAddRow(editRows, setEditRows)}
                      onRemoveRow={makeRemoveRow(editRows, setEditRows)}
                      onUpdateRow={makeUpdateRow(editRows, setEditRows)}
                    />
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10, marginTop: 16 }}>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => setEditClusterId(null)} style={{
                        padding: '7px 18px', fontSize: 13,
                        border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151', cursor: 'pointer',
                      }}>
                        Cancel
                      </button>
                      <button type="submit" disabled={editSaving} style={{
                        padding: '7px 20px', fontSize: 13, background: '#3b82f6',
                        color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 10,
                        fontWeight: 600, cursor: editSaving ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        opacity: editSaving ? 0.6 : 1,
                      }}>
                        {editSaving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save Changes'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </form>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Deduct Modal ── */}
      {deductCluster && (
        <div style={{
          position: 'fixed', inset: 0, zIndex: 50,
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
          padding: '72px 16px 24px',
          background: 'rgba(15,23,42,0.45)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
        }}>
          <div style={{
            background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.96)',
            backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
            WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
            borderRadius: 20,
            boxShadow: '0 25px 80px rgba(15,23,42,0.25)',
            width: '100%', maxWidth: 760,
            margin: '0 16px',
            maxHeight: '90vh',
            display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column',
            border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
            overflow: 'hidden',
          }}>
            {/* Modal header */}
            <div style={{
              padding: '14px 20px',
              borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
              background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.8)',
              flexShrink: 0,
            }}>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a' }}>Deduct Usage</p>
              <p style={{ margin: '2px 0 0', fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b' }}>
                Cluster: <strong style={{ color: '#374151' }}>{deductCluster.name}</strong>
              </p>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
              {!deductResult ? (
                <form onSubmit={handleDeduct} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
                  {deductError && (
                    <div style={{
                      margin: '12px 20px 0',
                      background: '#fef2f2', border: '1px solid rgba(254,202,202,0.8)',
                      borderRadius: 10, padding: '8px 12px',
                      fontSize: 13, color: '#991b1b', whiteSpace: 'pre-line',
                      flexShrink: 0,
                    }}>
                      {deductError}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* Scrollable preview table */}
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: '12px 20px 0' }}>
                    <p style={{ ...labelStyle, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                      What will be deducted per {deductCluster.unit}:
                    </p>
                    <div style={{ border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)', borderRadius: 10, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                      <table style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: 13, borderCollapse: 'collapse' }}>
                        <thead style={{ position: 'sticky', top: 0, zIndex: 1 }}>
                          <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                            {['Material', `Per ${deductCluster.unit}`, `Total (${deductUnits || '?'} ×)`, 'In Stock'].map((h, i) => (
                              <th key={h} style={{
                                padding: '8px 12px',
                                textAlign: (i === 1 || i === 2 || i === 3) ? 'right' : 'left',
                                fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
                                color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                                whiteSpace: 'nowrap', background: '#f8fafc',
                              }}>{h}</th>
                            ))}
                          </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                          {deductCluster.items.map((ci, i) => {
                            const total = deductUnits ? parseFloat(deductUnits) * ci.qtyPerUnit : null
                            const insufficient = total !== null && total > ci.item.quantity
                            return (
                              <tr key={ci.id} style={{
                                borderBottom: i < deductCluster.items.length - 1 ? '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)' : 'none',
                                background: insufficient ? 'rgba(254,242,242,0.5)' : i % 2 === 0 ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)',
                              }}>
                                <td style={{ padding: '7px 12px', color: '#374151', fontWeight: 500 }}>{ci.item.name}</td>
                                <td style={{ padding: '7px 12px', textAlign: 'right', color: '#64748b', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
                                  {ci.qtyPerUnit}
                                </td>
                                <td style={{
                                  padding: '7px 12px', textAlign: 'right',
                                  fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums',
                                  color: insufficient ? '#dc2626' : '#374151', whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                                }}>
                                  {total !== null ? <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{total.toFixed(4)}</span> : '—'}
                                </td>
                                <td style={{
                                  padding: '7px 12px', textAlign: 'right', fontSize: 12,
                                  fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums', whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                                  color: insufficient ? '#dc2626' : '#64748b',
                                }}>
                                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{ci.item.quantity}</span>
                                  <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', margin: '0 3px' }}>×</span>
                                  <span style={{ color: insufficient ? '#dc2626' : '#94a3b8' }}>{ci.item.unit}</span>
                                  {insufficient && <span style={{ marginLeft: 3, color: '#dc2626' }}>⚠</span>}
                                </td>
                              </tr>
                            )
                          })}
                        </tbody>
                      </table>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Fixed footer — inputs + buttons */}
                  <div style={{
                    padding: '12px 20px',
                    borderTop: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
                    background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.6)',
                    flexShrink: 0,
                  }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                      <div>
                        <label style={labelStyle}>
                          Number of {deductCluster.unit}s processed *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          required type="number" step="0.1" min="0.1"
                          value={deductUnits}
                          onChange={e => setDeductUnits(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="e.g. 96"
                          style={inputStyle}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label style={labelStyle}>
                          Project Code *
                          <span style={{
                            marginLeft: 6, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 600,
                            color: '#dc2626', letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                          }}>
                            REQUIRED
                          </span>
                        </label>
                        <input
                          required
                          value={deductProjectCode}
                          onChange={e => setDeductProjectCode(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="e.g. ICTP002"
                          style={{
                            ...inputStyle,
                            borderColor: deductProjectCode.trim() === '' && deductError
                              ? '#dc2626'
                              : 'rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
                          }}
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10 }}>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => setDeductCluster(null)} style={{
                        padding: '7px 18px', fontSize: 13,
                        border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151', cursor: 'pointer',
                      }}>
                        Cancel
                      </button>
                      <button type="submit" disabled={deducting} style={{
                        padding: '7px 20px', fontSize: 13, background: '#10b981',
                        color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 10,
                        fontWeight: 600, cursor: deducting ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        opacity: deducting ? 0.6 : 1,
                      }}>
                        {deducting ? 'Deducting…' : 'Confirm Deduction'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </form>
              ) : (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: '12px 20px' }}>
                    <div style={{
                      background: 'rgba(240,253,244,0.8)',
                      border: '1px solid rgba(167,243,208,0.8)',
                      borderRadius: 10, padding: '12px 14px', marginBottom: 12,
                    }}>
                      <p style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#15803d', margin: 0 }}>✓ Deduction successful</p>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#166534', margin: '2px 0 0' }}>
                        {deductResult.units} {deductCluster.unit}(s) of &ldquo;{deductResult.cluster}&rdquo; processed.
                      </p>
                      <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#166534', margin: '2px 0 0' }}>
                        Project Code: <strong>{deductProjectCode}</strong>
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)', borderRadius: 10, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                      <table style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: 13, borderCollapse: 'collapse' }}>
                        <thead>
                          <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                            {['Material', 'Deducted', 'Remaining'].map((h, i) => (
                              <th key={h} style={{
                                padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: i === 0 ? 'left' : 'right',
                                fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b',
                                textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                              }}>{h}</th>
                            ))}
                          </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                          {deductResult.deducted.map((d, i) => (
                            <tr key={i} style={{ borderBottom: i < deductResult.deducted.length - 1 ? '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)' : 'none' }}>
                              <td style={{ padding: '7px 12px', color: '#374151', fontWeight: 500 }}>{d.name}</td>
                              <td style={{ padding: '7px 12px', textAlign: 'right', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums', color: '#dc2626', fontWeight: 600, whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                                −{d.deducted} {d.unit}
                              </td>
                              <td style={{ padding: '7px 12px', textAlign: 'right', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums', color: '#374151', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                                {d.remaining} {d.unit}
                              </td>
                            </tr>
                          ))}
                        </tbody>
                      </table>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{
                    padding: '12px 20px',
                    borderTop: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
                    background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.6)',
                    display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', flexShrink: 0,
                  }}>
                    <button onClick={() => setDeductCluster(null)} style={{
                      padding: '8px 24px', fontSize: 13, background: '#0f172a',
                      color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 10, fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer',
                    }}>
                      Done
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\inventory\forecast.tsx =====

// pages/inventory/forecast.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

// ─── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

type LabRequest = {
  id: number
  requestCode: string
  sampleName: string
  projectCode: string | null
  crop: string | null
  season: string | null
  year: number | null
  predictedSamples: number | null
  predictedDataPoints: number | null
}

type ClusterItem = {
  id: number
  itemId: number
  qtyPerUnit: number
  item: {
    id: number
    name: string
    lab?: { name: string } | null
  }
}

type Cluster = {
  id: number
  name: string
  description: string | null
  unit: string
  items: ClusterItem[]
}

interface ForecastRow {
  clusterId: number
  clusterName: string
  itemId: number
  itemName: string
  qtyPerUnit: number
  clusterPredictedSamples: number
  forecastedRequirement: number
}

// ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function Spinner() {
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 64, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13 }}>
      Loading…
    </div>
  )
}

const TEAL = '#14b8a6'
const TEAL_DARK = '#0d9488'
const TEAL_LIGHT = 'rgba(20,184,166,0.08)'
const TEAL_BORDER = 'rgba(20,184,166,0.22)'

// FIX: The API now supports up to 500 records per page. We fetch with
// limit=500 and show a warning banner if the total exceeds what was returned,
// so users know the forecast is incomplete rather than silently wrong.
const REQUEST_FETCH_LIMIT = 500

// ─── Main Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function ForecastPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string

  const [requests, setRequests]             = useState<LabRequest[]>([])
  const [requestTotal, setRequestTotal]     = useState(0)
  const [clusters, setClusters]             = useState<Cluster[]>([])
  const [pageLoading, setPageLoading]       = useState(true)
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen]           = useState(false)
  const [selectedReqIds, setSelectedReqIds] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
  const [clusterInputs, setClusterInputs]   = useState<Record<number, string>>({})
  const [forecastRows, setForecastRows]     = useState<ForecastRow[] | null>(null)
  const [expandedClusters, setExpandedClusters] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    if (!session || (role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' && role !== 'ADMIN' && role !== 'MANAGEMENT')) {
      router.push('/inventory')
    }
  }, [session, status, role, router])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    Promise.all([
      fetch(`/api/requests?page=1&limit=${REQUEST_FETCH_LIMIT}&archived=false`).then(r => r.json()),
      fetch('/api/inventory/clusters').then(r => r.json()),
    ])
      .then(([reqData, clusterData]) => {
        setRequests(reqData?.requests ?? [])
        setRequestTotal(reqData?.total ?? 0)
        setClusters(Array.isArray(clusterData) ? clusterData : [])
      })
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => setPageLoading(false))
  }, [session])

  const totalPredictedSamples    = requests.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.predictedSamples    ?? 0), 0)
  const totalPredictedDataPoints = requests.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.predictedDataPoints ?? 0), 0)
  // FIX: Detect truncation so we can warn the user
  const isTruncated = requestTotal > requests.length

  const handleStartForecasting = () => {
    const init: Record<number, string> = {}
    clusters.forEach(c => { init[c.id] = '' })
    setClusterInputs(init)
    setSelectedReqIds(new Set())
    setExpandedClusters(new Set())
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  const toggleRequest = (id: number) => {
    setSelectedReqIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev)
      if (next.has(id)) { next.delete(id) } else { next.add(id) }
      return next
    })
  }

  const toggleAllRequests = () => {
    if (selectedReqIds.size === requests.length) {
      setSelectedReqIds(new Set())
    } else {
      setSelectedReqIds(new Set(requests.map(r => r.id)))
    }
  }

  const toggleClusterExpand = (id: number) => {
    setExpandedClusters(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev)
      if (next.has(id)) { next.delete(id) } else { next.add(id) }
      return next
    })
  }

  const handleGenerateForecast = () => {
    const rows: ForecastRow[] = []
    clusters.forEach(cluster => {
      const samples = parseInt(clusterInputs[cluster.id] || '0', 10) || 0
      const items = cluster.items ?? []
      items.forEach(ci => {
        rows.push({
          clusterId:               cluster.id,
          clusterName:             cluster.name,
          itemId:                  ci.item.id,
          itemName:                ci.item.name,
          qtyPerUnit:              ci.qtyPerUnit,
          clusterPredictedSamples: samples,
          forecastedRequirement:   samples * ci.qtyPerUnit,
        })
      })
    })
    setForecastRows(rows)
    setModalOpen(false)
  }

  if (pageLoading) return <Spinner />

  const card: React.CSSProperties = {
    background: '#ffffff',
    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
    borderRadius: 16,
    boxShadow: '0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.05), 0 4px 24px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
  }

  const sectionLabel: React.CSSProperties = {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: 700,
    color: '#475569',
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
    letterSpacing: '0.08em',
    margin: 0,
  }

  const headerTh: React.CSSProperties = {
    padding: '11px 16px',
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: 700,
    color: '#475569',
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
    letterSpacing: '0.07em',
    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
    background: '#f8fafc',
    borderBottom: '1.5px solid #e2e8f0',
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '28px 32px', maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto' }}>

      {/* ── Page header ── */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 32 }}>
        <h1 style={{ fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a', letterSpacing: '-0.03em', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
          Forecast
        </h1>
        <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#64748b', margin: '5px 0 0', fontWeight: 500 }}>
          Predict required chemical quantities for the next season.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* FIX: Show truncation warning when request list was capped */}
      {isTruncated && (
        <div style={{
          marginBottom: 20,
          padding: '12px 16px',
          background: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.08)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.3)',
          borderRadius: 12,
          fontSize: 13,
          color: '#92400e',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: 10,
        }}>
          <span style={{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: '50%', background: '#f59e0b', flexShrink: 0 }} />
          <span>
            <strong>Partial data:</strong> showing {requests.length} of {requestTotal.toLocaleString()} requests.
            The forecast below is based on this subset only. Contact your system administrator
            to increase the API page limit if your lab has a large number of active requests.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {forecastRows === null && (
        <div style={{
          ...card,
          width: '100%',
          boxSizing: 'border-box',
          display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center', padding: '80px 32px', gap: 16,
        }}>
          <div style={{
            width: 56, height: 56, borderRadius: '50%',
            background: 'rgba(20,184,166,0.10)',
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
            fontSize: 26,
          }}>🧪</div>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>
            No forecast generated yet
          </p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#64748b', margin: 0, textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: 360, lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            Select requests and allocate predicted samples per cluster to generate the chemical requirement forecast.
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={handleStartForecasting}
            style={{
              marginTop: 8, padding: '11px 32px', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700,
              color: '#fff', background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${TEAL}, ${TEAL_DARK})`,
              border: 'none', borderRadius: 12, cursor: 'pointer',
              boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(20,184,166,0.35)',
              transition: 'transform 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={e => {
              e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)'
              e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 6px 20px rgba(20,184,166,0.45)'
            }}
            onMouseLeave={e => {
              e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(0)'
              e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 4px 16px rgba(20,184,166,0.35)'
            }}
          >
            Start Forecasting
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Forecast table ── */}
      {forecastRows !== null && (
        <>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <button
              onClick={handleStartForecasting}
              style={{
                padding: '9px 22px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                color: TEAL, background: TEAL_LIGHT,
                border: `1.5px solid ${TEAL_BORDER}`, borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer',
                transition: 'background 0.15s',
              }}
              onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(20,184,166,0.14)' }}
              onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = TEAL_LIGHT }}
            >
              ↺ Re-run Forecast
            </button>
          </div>

          <div style={{ ...card, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
            <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
              <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: 13 }}>
                <thead>
                  <tr style={{ borderTop: `3px solid ${TEAL}` }}>
                    {[
                      { label: 'Cluster',                align: 'left'  },
                      { label: 'Item / Chemical',        align: 'left'  },
                      { label: 'Qty Per Unit',           align: 'right' },
                      { label: 'Predicted Samples',      align: 'right' },
                      { label: 'Forecasted Requirement', align: 'right' },
                    ].map(({ label, align }) => (
                      <th key={label} style={{ ...headerTh, textAlign: align as 'left' | 'right' }}>
                        {label}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {forecastRows.length === 0 ? (
                    <tr>
                      <td colSpan={5} style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>
                        No items found in the selected clusters.
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ) : forecastRows.map((row, i) => (
                    <tr
                      key={`${row.clusterId}-${row.itemId}`}
                      style={{
                        borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                        background: i % 2 === 1 ? '#fafafa' : '#ffffff',
                        transition: 'background 0.12s',
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(204,251,241,0.35)' }}
                      onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = i % 2 === 1 ? '#fafafa' : '#ffffff' }}
                    >
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>{row.clusterName}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', color: '#334155' }}>{row.itemName}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', textAlign: 'right', color: '#334155', fontWeight: 500 }}>{row.qtyPerUnit}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', textAlign: 'right', color: '#334155', fontWeight: 500 }}>{row.clusterPredictedSamples.toLocaleString()}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                        <span style={{
                          fontWeight: 800,
                          color: row.forecastedRequirement > 0 ? '#0f172a' : '#94a3b8',
                          fontSize: 14,
                        }}>
                          {row.forecastedRequirement.toLocaleString()}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {/* ── Modal ── */}
      {modalOpen && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'fixed', inset: 0,
            background: 'rgba(15,23,42,0.55)',
            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
            zIndex: 1000, padding: '24px', overflowY: 'auto',
          }}
          onClick={e => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) setModalOpen(false) }}
        >
          <div style={{
            ...card,
            width: '100%', maxWidth: 920, maxHeight: '82vh',
            display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column',
            borderRadius: 20, overflow: 'hidden',
          }}>

            {/* ── Modal header ── */}
            <div style={{
              padding: '26px 28px 22px',
              borderBottom: '1.5px solid #e2e8f0',
              background: '#fff',
            }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 18 }}>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, letterSpacing: '-0.02em' }}>
                  Forecasting on basis of samples
                </h2>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setModalOpen(false)}
                  style={{
                    width: 30, height: 30, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                    background: '#f1f5f9', border: 'none', borderRadius: 8,
                    cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 16, color: '#64748b',
                    transition: 'background 0.12s',
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = '#e2e8f0' }}
                  onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = '#f1f5f9' }}
                >
                  ×
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* KPI cards */}
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 12 }}>
                {[
                  { label: 'Total Predicted Samples',     value: totalPredictedSamples.toLocaleString() },
                  { label: 'Total Predicted Data Points', value: totalPredictedDataPoints.toLocaleString() },
                ].map(({ label, value }) => (
                  <div key={label} style={{
                    background: 'rgba(20,184,166,0.07)',
                    border: `1.5px solid ${TEAL_BORDER}`,
                    borderRadius: 12, padding: '14px 18px',
                  }}>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 4px', lineHeight: 1, letterSpacing: '-0.03em' }}>{value}</p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: TEAL, margin: 0, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.08em' }}>
                      {label}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* FIX: Show truncation warning inside modal as well */}
              {isTruncated && (
                <div style={{
                  marginTop: 12, padding: '8px 12px',
                  background: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.08)',
                  border: '1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.25)',
                  borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 12, color: '#92400e',
                }}>
                  ⚠ Showing {requests.length} of {requestTotal.toLocaleString()} requests. Forecast may be incomplete.
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* ── Modal body ── */}
            <div style={{ padding: '22px 28px', background: '#fff', flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', minHeight: 0 }}>

              {/* Section 1: Requests */}
              <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <p style={sectionLabel}>Requests</p>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                    {selectedReqIds.size} of {requests.length} selected
                    {isTruncated && ` (${requestTotal.toLocaleString()} total)`}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div style={{
                  border: '1.5px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 12,
                  overflowX: 'auto', overflowY: 'auto', maxHeight: 220,
                }}>
                  <table style={{ minWidth: 900, borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: 13 }}>
                    <colgroup>
                      <col style={{ width: 36 }} />
                      <col style={{ width: 110 }} />
                      <col style={{ width: 150 }} />
                      <col style={{ width: 80 }} />
                      <col style={{ width: 110 }} />
                      <col style={{ width: 90 }} />
                      <col style={{ width: 100 }} />
                    </colgroup>
                    <thead>
                      <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1.5px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                        <th style={{ padding: '10px 14px', position: 'sticky', top: 0, background: '#f8fafc', zIndex: 1 }}>
                          <input
                            type="checkbox"
                            checked={selectedReqIds.size === requests.length && requests.length > 0}
                            onChange={toggleAllRequests}
                            style={{ accentColor: TEAL, width: 15, height: 15, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                          />
                        </th>
                        {['Project Code', 'Nursery Name', 'Crop', 'Season / Year', 'Pred. Samples', 'Pred. Data Points'].map(h => (
                          <th key={h} style={{
                            padding: '10px 12px', textAlign: 'left',
                            fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#475569',
                            textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.07em', whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                            overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                            position: 'sticky', top: 0, background: '#f8fafc', zIndex: 1,
                          }}>
                            {h}
                          </th>
                        ))}
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {requests.length === 0 ? (
                        <tr>
                          <td colSpan={7} style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13 }}>
                            No requests found.
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ) : requests.map((req, i) => {
                        const isChecked = selectedReqIds.has(req.id)
                        return (
                          <tr
                            key={req.id}
                            style={{
                              background: isChecked ? 'rgba(204,251,241,0.40)' : i % 2 === 1 ? '#fafafa' : '#fff',
                              borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                              cursor: 'pointer',
                              transition: 'background 0.1s',
                            }}
                            onClick={() => toggleRequest(req.id)}
                            onMouseEnter={e => {
                              if (!isChecked) e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(204,251,241,0.22)'
                            }}
                            onMouseLeave={e => {
                              if (!isChecked) e.currentTarget.style.background = i % 2 === 1 ? '#fafafa' : '#fff'
                            }}
                          >
                            <td style={{ padding: '10px 14px', width: 36 }}>
                              <input
                                type="checkbox"
                                checked={isChecked}
                                onChange={() => toggleRequest(req.id)}
                                onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
                                style={{ accentColor: TEAL, width: 15, height: 15, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                              />
                            </td>
                            <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f766e', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>
                              {req.projectCode ?? '—'}
                            </td>
                            <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', color: '#334155', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                              {req.sampleName}
                            </td>
                            <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', color: '#334155', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{req.crop ?? '—'}</td>
                            <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', color: '#334155', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                              {[req.season, req.year].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || '—'}
                            </td>
                            <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                              {req.predictedSamples?.toLocaleString() ?? '—'}
                            </td>
                            <td style={{ padding: '10px 12px', textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                              {req.predictedDataPoints?.toLocaleString() ?? '—'}
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        )
                      })}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>

                {selectedReqIds.size > 0 && (() => {
                  const sel   = requests.filter(r => selectedReqIds.has(r.id))
                  const selS  = sel.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.predictedSamples    ?? 0), 0)
                  const selDp = sel.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.predictedDataPoints ?? 0), 0)
                  return (
                    <div style={{
                      display: 'flex', gap: 20, marginTop: 10,
                      padding: '10px 16px',
                      background: 'rgba(20,184,166,0.06)',
                      border: `1.5px solid ${TEAL_BORDER}`,
                      borderRadius: 10,
                    }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#475569', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                        {selectedReqIds.size} request{selectedReqIds.size !== 1 ? 's' : ''} selected
                      </span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8' }}>·</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#475569', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                        Samples: <strong style={{ color: '#0f172a', fontWeight: 800 }}>{selS.toLocaleString()}</strong>
                      </span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8' }}>·</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#475569', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                        Data Points: <strong style={{ color: '#0f172a', fontWeight: 800 }}>{selDp.toLocaleString()}</strong>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )
                })()}
              </div>

              <div style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #e2e8f0', margin: '0 0 22px' }} />

              {/* Section 2: Cluster allocation */}
              <div>
                <p style={{ ...sectionLabel, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                  Allocate predicted samples per cluster
                </p>

                {clusters.length === 0 ? (
                  <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13 }}>No clusters found.</p>
                ) : (
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
                    {clusters.map(cluster => {
                      const isExpanded = expandedClusters.has(cluster.id)
                      const itemCount = cluster.items?.length ?? 0
                      return (
                        <div
                          key={cluster.id}
                          style={{
                            background: '#f8fafc',
                            border: '1.5px solid #e2e8f0',
                            borderRadius: 12, overflow: 'hidden',
                            transition: 'border-color 0.15s',
                          }}
                        >
                          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: '12px 16px' }}>
                            <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                              <span style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>{cluster.name}</span>
                              {itemCount > 0 && (
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => toggleClusterExpand(cluster.id)}
                                  style={{
                                    marginLeft: 10,
                                    fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
                                    color: isExpanded ? TEAL : '#64748b',
                                    background: isExpanded ? TEAL_LIGHT : 'transparent',
                                    border: `1px solid ${isExpanded ? TEAL_BORDER : '#e2e8f0'}`,
                                    borderRadius: 6, padding: '2px 8px',
                                    cursor: 'pointer', transition: 'all 0.12s',
                                  }}
                                >
                                  {itemCount} item{itemCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} {isExpanded ? '▲' : '▼'}
                                </button>
                              )}
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                              <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: 500 }}>samples</span>
                              <input
                                type="number" min={0} placeholder="0"
                                value={clusterInputs[cluster.id] ?? ''}
                                onChange={e => setClusterInputs(prev => ({ ...prev, [cluster.id]: e.target.value }))}
                                style={{
                                  width: 100, padding: '8px 12px', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700,
                                  color: '#0f172a', background: '#ffffff',
                                  border: '1.5px solid #d1d5db',
                                  borderRadius: 8, outline: 'none', textAlign: 'right',
                                  transition: 'border-color 0.12s',
                                }}
                                onFocus={e => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = TEAL }}
                                onBlur={e => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#d1d5db' }}
                              />
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          {isExpanded && itemCount > 0 && (
                            <div style={{
                              borderTop: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                              padding: '10px 16px 12px',
                              background: '#fff',
                              display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6,
                            }}>
                              {cluster.items.map(ci => (
                                <span
                                  key={ci.id}
                                  title={`${ci.item.name} × ${ci.qtyPerUnit} /${cluster.unit}`}
                                  style={{
                                    display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5,
                                    fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 500, color: '#334155',
                                    background: '#f1f5f9', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    borderRadius: 6, padding: '4px 10px',
                                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap', maxWidth: 220, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                                  }}
                                >
                                  <span style={{ display: 'inline-block', maxWidth: 130, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                                    {ci.item.name}
                                  </span>
                                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: TEAL, background: 'rgba(20,184,166,0.10)', borderRadius: 4, padding: '1px 5px', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                                    ×{ci.qtyPerUnit}
                                  </span>
                                </span>
                              ))}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* ── Modal footer ── */}
            <div style={{
              padding: '16px 28px',
              borderTop: '1.5px solid #e2e8f0',
              display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10,
              background: '#f8fafc',
            }}>
              <button
                onClick={() => setModalOpen(false)}
                style={{
                  padding: '10px 24px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,
                  color: '#475569', background: '#fff',
                  border: '1.5px solid #d1d5db', borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer',
                  transition: 'background 0.12s',
                }}
                onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = '#f1f5f9' }}
                onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.background = '#fff' }}
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleGenerateForecast}
                style={{
                  padding: '10px 28px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 800,
                  color: '#fff', background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${TEAL}, ${TEAL_DARK})`,
                  border: 'none', borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer',
                  boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(20,184,166,0.3)',
                  transition: 'transform 0.12s, box-shadow 0.12s',
                }}
                onMouseEnter={e => {
                  e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)'
                  e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 6px 18px rgba(20,184,166,0.4)'
                }}
                onMouseLeave={e => {
                  e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(0)'
                  e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 4px 14px rgba(20,184,166,0.3)'
                }}
              >
                Generate Forecast
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\inventory\import.tsx =====

// pages/inventory/import.tsx
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx'
import { parseExcelRows } from '../../lib/inventoryImportUtils'
import type { ImportPreview } from '../../lib/inventoryImportUtils'

type Lab = { id: number; name: string }

function fmtDate(s: string | null) {
  if (!s) return '—'
  return new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
}

export default function ImportPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const role      = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string ?? 'GUEST'
  const canImport = role === 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' || role === 'ADMIN'

  const [labs, setLabs]                   = useState<Lab[]>([])
  const [labId, setLabId]                 = useState('')
  const [minThreshold, setMinThreshold]   = useState('10')
  const [clearExisting, setClearExisting] = useState(false)

  const [fileName, setFileName]           = useState('')
  const [preview, setPreview]             = useState<ImportPreview | null>(null)
  const [importing, setImporting]         = useState(false)
  const [result, setResult]               = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [error, setError]                 = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/labs')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then((d: Lab[]) => setLabs(Array.isArray(d) ? d : []))
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [])

  function handleFile(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
    if (!file) return

    setFileName(file.name)
    setPreview(null)
    setResult(null)
    setError('')

    const reader = new FileReader()
    reader.onload = ev => {
      try {
        const data = new Uint8Array(ev.target!.result as ArrayBuffer)
        const wb   = XLSX.read(data, { type: 'array', cellDates: false, raw: true })
        const ws   = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]]
        // Get raw values as strings — we parse dates ourselves
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json<unknown[]>(ws, {
          header: 1,
          defval: null,
          raw:    true,
        })
        setPreview(parseExcelRows(rows))
      } catch (err) {
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to read file')
      }
    }
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file)
  }

  async function handleImport() {
    if (!preview?.rows.length) return
    if (!labId) { setError('Please select a lab first'); return }

    setImporting(true)
    setError('')

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/inventory/import', {
        method:  'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          rows:         preview.rows,
          labId:        parseInt(labId),
          minThreshold: parseFloat(minThreshold) || 10,
          clearExisting,
        }),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error)
      setResult(data.imported)
      setPreview(null)
      setFileName('')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Import failed')
    } finally {
      setImporting(false)
    }
  }

  if (status === 'loading') return <div className="p-6 text-gray-400">Loading...</div>
  if (!canImport) return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-red-700">
        You do not have permission to import inventory.
      </div>
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">

      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Import Inventory</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">Upload your Excel file to bulk-add items to inventory.</p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => router.push('/inventory')}
          className="text-sm text-blue-600 hover:underline">
          ← Back to Inventory
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Expected format info */}
      <div className="bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-700 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300">
        <p className="font-semibold mb-1">Expected column order (row 1 is header and will be skipped):</p>
        <p className="font-mono text-xs tracking-tight">
          Material · Catalogue Number · Brand of Supply · Type · Lab · Available Quantity · Unit of Qty · Mfg Date · Expiry Date
        </p>
        <p className="text-xs mt-2 opacity-70">
          Type should be <strong>Chemicals</strong> or <strong>Consumables</strong>.
          Quantities like &quot;nil&quot;, &quot;1kg&quot;, &quot;4pks&quot; are handled automatically.
          Dates can be blank, &quot;No.Mfg Date&quot;, a year like 2022, or a full date.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Step 1 — File */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl p-6">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 mb-4">Step 1 — Choose Excel file</h2>
        <label className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-xl py-10 cursor-pointer hover:border-blue-400 transition-colors">
          <span className="text-4xl mb-2">📂</span>
          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">
            {fileName || 'Click to upload .xlsx'}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Excel .xlsx only</span>
          <input type="file" accept=".xlsx" className="hidden" onChange={handleFile} />
        </label>
      </div>

      {/* Step 2 — Options (only after file chosen) */}
      {preview && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl p-6 space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">Step 2 — Import options</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 mb-1">Target Lab *</label>
              <select value={labId} onChange={e => setLabId(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:border-gray-600 dark:text-gray-100">
                <option value="">Select lab...</option>
                {labs.map(l => <option key={l.id} value={l.id}>{l.name}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 mb-1">Min Stock Threshold</label>
              <input type="number" min="0" value={minThreshold}
                onChange={e => setMinThreshold(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:border-gray-600 dark:text-gray-100" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 cursor-pointer select-none">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={clearExisting} onChange={e => setClearExisting(e.target.checked)}
              className="rounded border-gray-300 text-blue-600" />
            <span><span className="text-red-600 font-medium">Delete existing items</span> for this lab before importing</span>
          </label>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Warnings */}
      {preview && preview.warnings.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-yellow-50 dark:bg-yellow-900/20 border border-yellow-200 rounded-xl p-4">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300 mb-2">
            ⚠ {preview.warnings.length} warning{preview.warnings.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
          </p>
          <ul className="space-y-0.5">
            {preview.warnings.map((w, i) => (
              <li key={i} className="text-xs text-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-400">{w}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Error */}
      {error && (
