        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl p-4 text-red-700 text-sm">{error}</div>
      )}

      {/* Preview table */}
      {preview && preview.rows.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-3 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">
              Preview — {preview.rows.length} rows ready to import
            </p>
            <button onClick={handleImport} disabled={importing || !labId}
              className="px-5 py-2 text-sm bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50">
              {importing ? 'Importing...' : `Import ${preview.rows.length} items`}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600">
                  {['#', 'Material', 'Cat No.', 'Brand', 'Type', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'Mfg Date', 'Expiry'].map(h => (
                    <th key={h} className="px-3 py-2 text-left font-semibold text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 whitespace-nowrap">{h}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-700">
                {preview.rows.map((row, i) => (
                  <tr key={i} className="hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-750">
                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-gray-400">{i + 1}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2 font-medium text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 max-w-52 truncate">{row.name}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-gray-400 max-w-28 truncate">{row.catalogueNumber || '—'}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">{row.brand || '—'}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
                      <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium ${
                        row.type === 'CHEMICAL'
                          ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                          : 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-700'
                      }`}>
                        {row.type === 'CHEMICAL' ? '🧪' : '🧴'} {row.type}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className={`px-3 py-2 tabular-nums font-semibold ${row.quantity === 0 ? 'text-gray-300' : 'text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100'}`}>
                      {row.quantity}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-gray-500">{row.unit}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-gray-500 whitespace-nowrap">{fmtDate(row.manufacturingDate)}</td>
                    <td className={`px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap ${row.expiryDate ? 'text-orange-600 font-medium' : 'text-gray-300'}`}>
                      {fmtDate(row.expiryDate)}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Success */}
      {result !== null && (
        <div className="bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 border border-green-200 dark:border-green-700 rounded-xl p-8 text-center">
          <p className="text-3xl mb-3">✅</p>
          <p className="text-green-800 dark:text-green-300 font-semibold text-lg">
            {result} items imported successfully
          </p>
          <button onClick={() => router.push('/inventory')}
            className="mt-4 px-5 py-2 text-sm bg-green-600 text-white rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-green-700">
            Go to Inventory
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\inventory\index.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
import Link from 'next/link'

// ─── Brand teal tokens ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const TEAL_ACCENT = '#14b8a6'                    // top bar
const TEAL_BORDER = '#99f6e4'                    // header bottom border
const TEAL_SEP    = 'rgba(153,246,228,0.55)'     // column separator pipes

// ─── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

type InventoryItem = {
  id: number
  name: string
  brand: string | null
  category: string
  type: string
  quantity: number
  unit: string
  minThreshold: number
  expiryDate: string | null
  manufacturingDate: string | null
  notes: string | null
  isLow: boolean
  isExpiringSoon: boolean
  lab: { id: number; name: string }
}

type InventoryAlert = {
  id: number
  name: string
  quantity: number
  unit: string
  labName: string
  type: 'LOW_STOCK' | 'EXPIRY'
  detail: string
}

type Lab = { id: number; name: string }

type AddItemForm = {
  name: string; brand: string; category: string; type: string
  quantity: string; unit: string; minThreshold: string
  expiryDate: string; manufacturingDate: string; labId: string; notes: string
}

const EMPTY_FORM: AddItemForm = {
  name: '', brand: '', category: '', type: 'CHEMICAL',
  quantity: '', unit: '', minThreshold: '10',
  expiryDate: '', manufacturingDate: '', labId: '', notes: '',
}

// ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function fmtDate(d: string | null) {
  if (!d) return '—'
  return new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
}

// ─── Icons ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const IconPlus = () => (
  <svg width="15" height="15" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2.5}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 4.5v15m7.5-7.5h-15" />
  </svg>
)

const IconSearch = () => (
  <svg width="14" height="14" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M21 21l-5.197-5.197m0 0A7.5 7.5 0 105.196 5.196a7.5 7.5 0 0010.607 10.607z" />
  </svg>
)

const IconFilter = () => (
  <svg width="14" height="14" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 3c2.755 0 5.455.232 8.083.678.533.09.917.556.917 1.096v1.044a2.25 2.25 0 01-.659 1.591l-5.432 5.432a2.25 2.25 0 00-.659 1.591v2.927a2.25 2.25 0 01-1.244 2.013L9.75 21v-6.568a2.25 2.25 0 00-.659-1.591L3.659 7.409A2.25 2.25 0 013 5.818V4.774c0-.54.384-1.006.917-1.096A48.32 48.32 0 0112 3z" />
  </svg>
)

const IconAlert = () => (
  <svg width="14" height="14" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 9v3.75m-9.303 3.376c-.866 1.5.217 3.374 1.948 3.374h14.71c1.73 0 2.813-1.874 1.948-3.374L13.949 3.378c-.866-1.5-3.032-1.5-3.898 0L2.697 16.126zM12 15.75h.007v.008H12v-.008z" />
  </svg>
)

const IconSpinner = () => (
  <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2.5}
    style={{ animation: 'spin 0.8s linear infinite', display: 'inline-block' }}>
    <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.25} />
    <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 00-9-9" strokeLinecap="round" />
  </svg>
)

const IconTransfer = () => (
  <svg width="13" height="13" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M7.5 21L3 16.5m0 0L7.5 12M3 16.5h13.5m0-13.5L21 7.5m0 0L16.5 12M21 7.5H7.5" />
  </svg>
)

// ─── Shared form field style ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

const iS: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box',
  border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 10,
  padding: '9px 12px', fontSize: 13, color: '#1e293b',
  background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)',
  outline: 'none', transition: 'border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s',
}

const lS: React.CSSProperties = {
  display: 'block', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
  color: '#98a2b3', textTransform: 'uppercase',
  letterSpacing: '0.12em', marginBottom: 6,
}

// ─── Item form (shared add / edit) ────────────────────────────────────────────

function ItemForm({
  form, setForm, onSubmit, onCancel, saving, error, submitLabel, labs,
}: {
  form: AddItemForm
  setForm: (f: AddItemForm) => void
  onSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent) => void
  onCancel: () => void
  saving: boolean
  error: string
  submitLabel: string
  labs: Lab[]
}) {
  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit}>
      {error && (
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)', border: '1px solid rgba(252,165,165,0.5)',
          borderRadius: 10, padding: '10px 14px',
          color: '#b91c1c', fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 18,
        }}>
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '14px 20px' }}>
        <div style={{ gridColumn: 'span 2' }}>
          <label style={lS}>Material Name <span style={{ color: '#ef4444' }}>*</span></label>
          <input required value={form.name} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="e.g. Taq DNA Polymerase" style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Brand / Supplier</label>
          <input value={form.brand} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, brand: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="e.g. Sigma, Thermo Fisher" style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Category <span style={{ color: '#ef4444' }}>*</span></label>
          <input required value={form.category} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, category: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="e.g. Buffer, Enzyme" style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Type</label>
          <select value={form.type} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, type: e.target.value })} style={iS}>
            <option value="CHEMICAL">🧪 Chemical</option>
            <option value="CONSUMABLE">🧴 Consumable</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Lab <span style={{ color: '#ef4444' }}>*</span></label>
          <select required value={form.labId} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, labId: e.target.value })} style={iS}>
            <option value="">Select lab…</option>
            {labs.map(l => <option key={l.id} value={l.id}>{l.name}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Available Qty <span style={{ color: '#ef4444' }}>*</span></label>
          <input required type="number" step="0.01" min="0" value={form.quantity}
            onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, quantity: e.target.value })} style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Unit <span style={{ color: '#ef4444' }}>*</span></label>
          <input required value={form.unit} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, unit: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="mL, g, units…" style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Min Threshold</label>
          <input type="number" step="0.01" min="0" value={form.minThreshold}
            onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, minThreshold: e.target.value })} style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Mfg Date</label>
          <input type="date" value={form.manufacturingDate}
            onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, manufacturingDate: e.target.value })} style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style={lS}>Expiry Date</label>
          <input type="date" value={form.expiryDate}
            onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, expiryDate: e.target.value })} style={iS} />
        </div>
        <div style={{ gridColumn: 'span 2' }}>
          <label style={lS}>Notes</label>
          <textarea rows={2} value={form.notes}
            onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, notes: e.target.value })}
            style={{ ...iS, resize: 'vertical' }} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10, marginTop: 20 }}>
        <button type="button" onClick={onCancel}
          style={{
            padding: '9px 20px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,
            border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
            cursor: 'pointer', background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151',
          }}>
          Cancel
        </button>
        <button type="submit" disabled={saving}
          style={{
            display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
            padding: '9px 24px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
            border: 'none', borderRadius: 10,
            cursor: saving ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
            background: saving
              ? 'rgba(20,184,166,0.4)'
              : 'linear-gradient(135deg, #0f9f8f 0%, #14b8a6 100%)',
            color: '#fff',
            boxShadow: saving ? 'none' : '0 4px 14px rgba(20,184,166,0.28)',
          }}>
          {saving && <IconSpinner />}
          {saving ? 'Saving…' : submitLabel}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}

// ─── Main Page ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function InventoryPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [items, setItems]           = useState<InventoryItem[]>([])
  const [alerts, setAlerts]         = useState<InventoryAlert[]>([])
  const [labs, setLabs]             = useState<Lab[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading]       = useState(true)
  const [loadError, setLoadError]   = useState('')
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  const [filterLabId,    setFilterLabId]    = useState('ALL')
  const [filterType,     setFilterType]     = useState('ALL')
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState('ALL')
  const [filterAlert,    setFilterAlert]    = useState('ALL')
  const [search,         setSearch]         = useState('')

  const [showAdd,   setShowAdd]   = useState(false)
  const [addForm,   setAddForm]   = useState<AddItemForm>(EMPTY_FORM)
  const [addSaving, setAddSaving] = useState(false)
  const [addError,  setAddError]  = useState('')

  const [editId,     setEditId]     = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [editForm,   setEditForm]   = useState<AddItemForm>(EMPTY_FORM)
  const [editSaving, setEditSaving] = useState(false)
  const [editError,  setEditError]  = useState('')

  const role         = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string || 'GUEST'
  const canManage    = role === 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' || role === 'ADMIN'
  const canSeeAlerts = role === 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' || role === 'ADMIN'
  const isReadOnly   = role === 'MANAGEMENT'

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'unauthenticated') router.push('/login')
  }, [status, router])

  const loadItems = useCallback(() => {
    setLoadError('')
    fetch('/api/inventory')
      .then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`Server error ${r.status}`); return r.json() })
      .then((data: InventoryItem[]) => { setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []); setLoading(false) })
      .catch((err: Error) => { setLoadError(err.message || 'Failed to load inventory'); setLoading(false) })
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    let cancelled = false
    fetch('/api/inventory')
      .then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`Server error ${r.status}`); return r.json() })
      .then((data: InventoryItem[]) => {
        if (cancelled) return
        setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
        setLoading(false)
      })
      .catch((err: Error) => {
        if (cancelled) return
        setLoadError(err.message || 'Failed to load inventory')
        setLoading(false)
      })
    return () => { cancelled = true }
  }, [session])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    fetch('/api/labs').then(r => r.json()).then((d: Lab[]) => setLabs(Array.isArray(d) ? d : [])).catch(() => {})
  }, [session])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session || !canSeeAlerts) return
    fetch('/api/inventory/alerts').then(r => r.json()).then((d: InventoryAlert[]) => setAlerts(Array.isArray(d) ? d : [])).catch(() => {})
  }, [session, canSeeAlerts])

  async function handleAdd(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); setAddSaving(true); setAddError('')
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/inventory', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: addForm.name, brand: addForm.brand || null,
          category: addForm.category, type: addForm.type,
          quantity: parseFloat(addForm.quantity), unit: addForm.unit,
          minThreshold: parseFloat(addForm.minThreshold),
          expiryDate: addForm.expiryDate || null,
          manufacturingDate: addForm.manufacturingDate || null,
          labId: parseInt(addForm.labId), notes: addForm.notes || null,
        }),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error)
      setShowAdd(false); setAddForm(EMPTY_FORM); loadItems()
    } catch (e: unknown) { setAddError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to add item') }
    finally { setAddSaving(false) }
  }

  function startEdit(item: InventoryItem) {
    setEditId(item.id)
    setEditForm({
      name: item.name, brand: item.brand ?? '', category: item.category,
      type: item.type, quantity: String(item.quantity), unit: item.unit,
      minThreshold: String(item.minThreshold),
      expiryDate: item.expiryDate ? item.expiryDate.split('T')[0] : '',
      manufacturingDate: item.manufacturingDate ? item.manufacturingDate.split('T')[0] : '',
      labId: String(item.lab.id), notes: item.notes ?? '',
    })
    setEditError('')
  }

  async function handleEdit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!editId) return
    setEditSaving(true); setEditError('')
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/inventory', {
        method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          id: editId, name: editForm.name, brand: editForm.brand || null,
          category: editForm.category, type: editForm.type,
          quantity: parseFloat(editForm.quantity), unit: editForm.unit,
          minThreshold: parseFloat(editForm.minThreshold),
          expiryDate: editForm.expiryDate || null,
          manufacturingDate: editForm.manufacturingDate || null,
          notes: editForm.notes || null,
        }),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error)
      setEditId(null); loadItems()
    } catch (e: unknown) { setEditError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to update item') }
    finally { setEditSaving(false) }
  }

  if (status === 'loading' || loading) return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', height: 240 }}>
      <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
        <IconSpinner />
        <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14, marginTop: 12 }}>Loading inventory…</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )

  if (loadError) return (
    <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>
      <div style={{
        background: 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)', border: '1px solid rgba(252,165,165,0.5)',
        borderRadius: 14, padding: '16px 20px', color: '#b91c1c',
      }}>
        <p style={{ fontWeight: 600, margin: '0 0 4px' }}>Failed to load inventory</p>
        <p style={{ fontSize: 13, margin: 0 }}>{loadError}</p>
        <button onClick={() => { setLoading(true); loadItems() }}
          style={{ marginTop: 10, fontSize: 13, color: '#0f9f8f', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )

  const lowCount      = items.filter(i => i.isLow).length
  const expiringCount = items.filter(i => i.isExpiringSoon).length
  const hasFilters    = filterLabId !== 'ALL' || filterType !== 'ALL' || filterCategory !== 'ALL' || filterAlert !== 'ALL' || !!search

  const filtered = items.filter(item => {
    if (filterLabId !== 'ALL' && String(item.lab.id) !== filterLabId) return false
    if (filterType !== 'ALL' && item.type !== filterType) return false
    if (filterCategory !== 'ALL' && item.category !== filterCategory) return false
    if (filterAlert === 'LOW' && !item.isLow) return false
    if (filterAlert === 'EXPIRING' && !item.isExpiringSoon) return false
    if (search && !item.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) &&
        !(item.brand ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())) return false
    return true
  })

  // ── Table column headers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const headerCols = [
    'Material', 'Brand', 'Type', 'Lab', 'Available Qty', 'Unit',
    'Mfg Date', 'Expiry Date', 'Status',
    ...(canManage ? ['Actions'] : []),
  ]

  return (
    <>
      <style>{`
        @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }
        .inv-row { transition: background 0.15s; }
        .inv-row:hover { background: rgba(204,251,241,0.35) !important; }
        .inv-row-selected { background: #f3fcf8 !important; border-left: 3px solid ${TEAL_ACCENT} !important; }
        .inv-row-selected:hover { background: #edfdf5 !important; }
      `}</style>

      <div style={{
        width: '100%',
        maxWidth: '1600px',
        margin: '0 auto',
        padding: '32px 28px 48px',
      }}>

        {/* ── Page header ── */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 16, marginBottom: 28 }}>
          <div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
              <h1 style={{ fontSize: 30, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a', letterSpacing: '-0.03em', margin: 0 }}>
                Inventory
              </h1>
              {isReadOnly && (
                <span style={{
                  fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, padding: '3px 10px',
                  background: 'rgba(241,245,249,0.9)', color: '#64748b',
                  borderRadius: 20, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                }}>
                  Read Only
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14, marginTop: 6, marginBottom: 0 }}>
              {filtered.length} of {items.length} items
            </p>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <Link href="/inventory/clusters" style={{
              display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
              padding: '9px 16px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,
              border: '1px solid rgba(99,102,241,0.25)', borderRadius: 12,
              color: '#6366f1', background: 'rgba(238,242,255,0.6)',
              textDecoration: 'none', transition: 'all 0.15s',
            }}>
              ⚗ Consumption Clusters
            </Link>

            {canManage && (
              <Link href="/inventory/new" style={{
                display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
                padding: '9px 18px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                border: 'none', borderRadius: 12, cursor: 'pointer',
                background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #0f9f8f 0%, #14b8a6 100%)',
                color: '#fff', textDecoration: 'none',
                boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(20,184,166,0.28)',
              }}>
                <IconPlus /> Add Item
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── Alert banners ── */}
        {canSeeAlerts && alerts.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8, marginBottom: 20 }}>
            {alerts.map(alert => (
              <div key={`${alert.id}-${alert.type}`} style={{
                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 10,
                padding: '12px 16px', borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 13,
                background: alert.type === 'EXPIRY' ? 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)' : 'rgba(254,252,232,0.9)',
                border: `1px solid ${alert.type === 'EXPIRY' ? 'rgba(252,165,165,0.5)' : 'rgba(253,224,71,0.5)'}`,
                color: alert.type === 'EXPIRY' ? '#b91c1c' : '#713f12',
              }}>
                <IconAlert />
                <span>
                  <strong>{alert.type === 'EXPIRY' ? 'Expiry:' : 'Low Stock:'}</strong>{' '}
                  <strong>{alert.name}</strong> ({alert.labName}) — {alert.detail}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ── Stock summary cards ── */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: 20 }}>
          {[
            {
              show: true,
              bg: lowCount > 0 ? 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)' : expiringCount > 0 ? 'rgba(254,252,232,0.9)' : 'rgba(209,250,229,0.8)',
              border: lowCount > 0 ? 'rgba(252,165,165,0.4)' : expiringCount > 0 ? 'rgba(253,224,71,0.4)' : 'rgba(20,184,166,0.3)',
              color: lowCount > 0 ? '#b91c1c' : expiringCount > 0 ? '#713f12' : '#0f766e',
              label: lowCount > 0
                ? `${lowCount} item${lowCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} below minimum stock`
                : expiringCount > 0
                  ? `${expiringCount} item${expiringCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} expiring within 30 days`
                  : 'All stock levels are healthy ✓',
            },
          ].filter(c => c.show).map((c, i) => (
            <div key={i} style={{
              padding: '12px 18px', borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,
              background: c.bg, border: `1px solid ${c.border}`, color: c.color,
            }}>
              {c.label}
            </div>
          ))}

          <div style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', display: 'flex', gap: 10, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8' }}>
              {filtered.filter(i => i.type === 'CHEMICAL').length} chemicals
              · {filtered.filter(i => i.type === 'CONSUMABLE').length} consumables
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── Add item panel ── */}
        {showAdd && canManage && (
          <div style={{
            background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)',
            backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
            borderRadius: 20, boxShadow: '0 8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,0.07)',
            padding: '24px 28px', marginBottom: 20,
          }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 20 }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: 0 }}>Add New Item</h2>
              <button onClick={() => { setShowAdd(false); setAddForm(EMPTY_FORM) }}
                style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 20, lineHeight: 1 }}>
                ×
              </button>
            </div>
            <ItemForm
              form={addForm} setForm={setAddForm}
              onSubmit={handleAdd} onCancel={() => { setShowAdd(false); setAddForm(EMPTY_FORM) }}
              saving={addSaving} error={addError} submitLabel="Add Item" labs={labs}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ── Filters ── */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
          borderRadius: 16, boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(15,23,42,0.04)',
          padding: '16px 20px', marginBottom: 16,
          display: 'flex', gap: 14, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'flex-end',
        }}>
          <div>
            <label style={lS}>
              <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}><IconSearch /> Search</span>
            </label>
            <input value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Name or brand…"
              style={{ ...iS, width: 180 }} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={lS}><span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}><IconFilter /> Type</span></label>
            <select value={filterType} onChange={e => setFilterType(e.target.value)} style={{ ...iS, width: 140 }}>
              <option value="ALL">All Types</option>
              <option value="CHEMICAL">🧪 Chemical</option>
              <option value="CONSUMABLE">🧴 Consumable</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={lS}>Lab</label>
            <select value={filterLabId} onChange={e => setFilterLabId(e.target.value)} style={{ ...iS, width: 140 }}>
              <option value="ALL">All Labs</option>
              {labs.map(l => <option key={l.id} value={String(l.id)}>{l.name}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={lS}>Alert</label>
            <select value={filterAlert} onChange={e => setFilterAlert(e.target.value)} style={{ ...iS, width: 140 }}>
              <option value="ALL">All Items</option>
              <option value="LOW">Low Stock</option>
              <option value="EXPIRING">Expiring Soon</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          {hasFilters && (
            <button
              onClick={() => { setFilterLabId('ALL'); setFilterType('ALL'); setFilterCategory('ALL'); setFilterAlert('ALL'); setSearch('') }}
              style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#0f9f8f', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 600, paddingBottom: 2 }}>
              Clear filters
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ── Inventory table ── */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
          borderRadius: 20, boxShadow: '0 8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}>
          <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            <table style={{
              width: '100%',
              minWidth: '1450px',
              borderCollapse: 'collapse',
              fontSize: 13,
            }}>
              {/* ── Headers ── */}
              <thead>
                {/*
                  borderTop:    3px solid #14b8a6  → brand accent bar (matches requests table)
                  borderBottom: 1px solid #99f6e4  → soft teal rule under headers
                */}
                <tr style={{
                  background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.9)',
                  borderTop: `3px solid ${TEAL_ACCENT}`,
                  borderBottom: `1px solid ${TEAL_BORDER}`,
                }}>
                  {headerCols.map((h, i) => (
                    <th key={h} style={{
                      padding: '12px 16px',
                      textAlign: i === 4 ? 'right' : 'left',
                      fontSize: 10,
                      fontWeight: 600,
                      color: '#98a2b3',
                      textTransform: 'uppercase',
                      letterSpacing: '0.12em',
                      whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                      // ── separator pipe on every col except the last ──
                      borderRight: i < headerCols.length - 1 ? `1px solid ${TEAL_SEP}` : undefined,
                    }}>
                      {h}
                    </th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>

              {/* ── Rows ── */}
              <tbody>
                {filtered.map((item, rowIdx) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedId === item.id
                  const zebraBg    = rowIdx % 2 === 0 ? '#ffffff' : '#fbfdfc'
                  const rowClass   = ['inv-row', isSelected ? 'inv-row-selected' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')

                  return (
                    <>
                      <tr
                        key={item.id}
                        className={rowClass}
                        onClick={() => setSelectedId(isSelected ? null : item.id)}
                        style={{
                          borderBottom: '1px solid #eef2f1',
                          background: item.isLow && !isSelected ? 'rgba(254,242,242,0.4)' : zebraBg,
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                      >
                        {/* Material */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px' }}>
                          <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f766e', letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}>
                            {item.name}
                          </div>
                          {item.notes && (
                            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#667085', marginTop: 2, maxWidth: 200, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                              {item.notes}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </td>

                        {/* Brand */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px', color: item.brand ? '#667085' : '#cbd5e1', fontSize: 13 }}>
                          {item.brand || '—'}
                        </td>

                        {/* Type */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px' }}>
                          <span style={{
                            display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center',
                            padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
                            background: item.type === 'CHEMICAL' ? 'rgba(237,233,254,0.8)' : 'rgba(204,251,241,0.8)',
                            color: item.type === 'CHEMICAL' ? '#6d28d9' : '#0f766e',
                          }}>
                            {item.type === 'CHEMICAL' ? '🧪 Chemical' : '🧴 Consumable'}
                          </span>
                        </td>

                        {/* Lab */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px', color: '#667085', fontSize: 13 }}>
                          {item.lab.name}
                        </td>

                        {/* Qty */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                          <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: item.isLow ? '#dc2626' : '#1e293b' }}>
                            {item.quantity}
                          </span>
                          {item.isLow && (
                            <div style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#f87171', marginTop: 1 }}>min: {item.minThreshold}</div>
                          )}
                        </td>

                        {/* Unit */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px', color: '#667085', fontSize: 13 }}>
                          {item.unit}
                        </td>

                        {/* Mfg Date */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 12, whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                          {fmtDate(item.manufacturingDate)}
                        </td>

                        {/* Expiry Date */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px', fontSize: 12, whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                          {item.expiryDate ? (
                            <span style={{ color: item.isExpiringSoon ? '#ea580c' : '#667085', fontWeight: item.isExpiringSoon ? 600 : 400 }}>
                              {fmtDate(item.expiryDate)} {item.isExpiringSoon && '⚠'}
                            </span>
                          ) : <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>—</span>}
                        </td>

                        {/* Status */}
                        <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px' }}>
                          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                            {item.isLow && (
                              <span style={{ padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, background: 'rgba(254,226,226,0.8)', color: '#dc2626' }}>Low</span>
                            )}
                            {item.isExpiringSoon && (
                              <span style={{ padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, background: 'rgba(255,237,213,0.8)', color: '#c2410c' }}>Expiring</span>
                            )}
                            {!item.isLow && !item.isExpiringSoon && (
                              <span style={{ padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, background: 'rgba(204,251,241,0.6)', color: '#0f766e' }}>OK</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </td>

                        {/* Actions */}
                        {canManage && (
                          <td style={{ padding: '14px 16px' }}>
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                              <button
                                onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); startEdit(item) }}
                                style={{
                                  fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, padding: '5px 12px',
                                  border: '1px solid rgba(99,102,241,0.25)', borderRadius: 8,
                                  cursor: 'pointer', color: '#6366f1',
                                  background: 'rgba(238,242,255,0.6)', transition: 'all 0.12s',
                                }}
                                onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(238,242,255,0.95)')}
                                onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(238,242,255,0.6)')}>
                                Edit
                              </button>
                              <button
                                onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); router.push(`/inventory/transfer?fromItemId=${item.id}`) }}
                                style={{
                                  display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4,
                                  fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, padding: '5px 12px',
                                  border: '1px solid #eef2f1', borderRadius: 8,
                                  cursor: 'pointer', color: '#667085',
                                  background: '#fbfdfc', transition: 'all 0.12s',
                                }}
                                onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = '#f6fbf9')}
                                onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = '#fbfdfc')}>
                                <IconTransfer /> Transfer
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          </td>
                        )}
                      </tr>

                      {/* Inline edit row */}
                      {editId === item.id && (
                        <tr key={`edit-${item.id}`}>
                          <td colSpan={canManage ? 10 : 9} style={{ padding: '0 16px 16px' }}>
                            <div style={{
                              background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.9)',
                              border: '1px solid rgba(20,184,166,0.2)',
                              borderRadius: 14, padding: '20px 24px',
                            }}>
                              <h3 style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: '0 0 16px' }}>
                                Edit: {item.name}
                              </h3>
                              <ItemForm
                                form={editForm} setForm={setEditForm}
                                onSubmit={handleEdit} onCancel={() => setEditId(null)}
                                saving={editSaving} error={editError} submitLabel="Save Changes" labs={labs}
                              />
                            </div>
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      )}
                    </>
                  )
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>

            {filtered.length === 0 && (
              <div style={{ padding: '56px 24px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 32, marginBottom: 12 }}>📦</div>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 14, margin: 0 }}>
                  {items.length === 0 ? 'No inventory items yet. Click "Add Item" to get started.' : 'No items match your filters.'}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </>
  )
}


===== pages\inventory\logs.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

// ─── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

type LogEntry = {
  id: number
  quantityUsed: number
  purpose: string
  usedAt: string
  item:    { name: string; unit: string }
  usedBy:  { name: string }
  request: { requestCode: string; sampleName: string } | null
  cluster: { name: string } | null
}

type InventoryItem = { id: number; name: string }

// ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function fmt(d: string) {
  return new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
}

// ─── Icons ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const IconBack = () => (
  <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M10.5 19.5L3 12m0 0l7.5-7.5M3 12h18" />
  </svg>
)

const IconFilter = () => (
  <svg width="15" height="15" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
      d="M12 3c2.755 0 5.455.232 8.083.678.533.09.917.556.917 1.096v1.044a2.25 2.25 0 01-.659 1.591l-5.432 5.432a2.25 2.25 0 00-.659 1.591v2.927a2.25 2.25 0 01-1.244 2.013L9.75 21v-6.568a2.25 2.25 0 00-.659-1.591L3.659 7.409A2.25 2.25 0 013 5.818V4.774c0-.54.384-1.006.917-1.096A48.32 48.32 0 0112 3z" />
  </svg>
)

const IconDownload = () => (
  <svg width="15" height="15" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
      d="M3 16.5v2.25A2.25 2.25 0 005.25 21h13.5A2.25 2.25 0 0021 18.75V16.5M16.5 12L12 16.5m0 0L7.5 12m4.5 4.5V3" />
  </svg>
)

const IconReagent = () => (
  <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8}>
    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
      d="M20.25 6.375c0 2.278-3.694 4.125-8.25 4.125S3.75 8.653 3.75 6.375m16.5 0c0-2.278-3.694-4.125-8.25-4.125S3.75 4.097 3.75 6.375m16.5 0v11.25c0 2.278-3.694 4.125-8.25 4.125s-8.25-1.847-8.25-4.125V6.375m16.5 0v3.75m-16.5-3.75v3.75m16.5 0v3.75C20.25 16.153 16.556 18 12 18s-8.25-1.847-8.25-4.125v-3.75m16.5 0c0 2.278-3.694 4.125-8.25 4.125s-8.25-1.847-8.25-4.125" />
  </svg>
)

const IconSpinner = () => (
  <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2.5}
    style={{ animation: 'spin 0.8s linear infinite', display: 'inline-block' }}>
    <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.25} />
    <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 00-9-9" strokeLinecap="round" />
  </svg>
)

// ─── Shared styles ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const iS: React.CSSProperties = {
  border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 10,
  padding: '9px 12px', fontSize: 13, color: '#1e293b',
  background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', outline: 'none',
  transition: 'border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s',
}

const lS: React.CSSProperties = {
  display: 'block', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
  color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase',
  letterSpacing: '0.07em', marginBottom: 6,
}

// ─── Main Page ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function LogsPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [logs, setLogs]       = useState<LogEntry[]>([])
  const [items, setItems]     = useState<InventoryItem[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [from, setFrom]       = useState('')
  const [to, setTo]           = useState('')
  const [itemId, setItemId]   = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    if (!session) { router.push('/login'); return }
    const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
    if (!['INVENTORY_MANAGER', 'ADMIN', 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST'].includes(role)) {
      router.push('/inventory')
    }
  }, [session, status, router])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    fetch('/api/inventory').then(r => r.json()).then(data => setItems(data))
  }, [session])

const loadLogs = useCallback(() => {
  setLoading(true)
  const params = new URLSearchParams()
  if (from)   params.set('from', from)
  if (to)     params.set('to', to)
  if (itemId) params.set('itemId', itemId)
  fetch(`/api/inventory/logs?${params.toString()}`)
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => { setLogs(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []); setLoading(false) })
    .catch(() => { setLogs([]); setLoading(false) })
}, [from, to, itemId])

  useEffect(() => { if (session) { void Promise.resolve().then(() => loadLogs()) } }, [session, loadLogs])

  function handleExport() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    if (from)   params.set('from', from)
    if (to)     params.set('to', to)
    if (itemId) params.set('itemId', itemId)
    window.location.href = `/api/inventory/logs/export?${params.toString()}`
  }

  const hasFilters = !!(from || to || itemId)

  if (status === 'loading') return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: 240 }}>
      <IconSpinner />
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <>
      <style>{`
        @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }
        input:focus, select:focus { border-color: #10b981 !important; box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(16,185,129,0.12) !important; }
      `}</style>

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 1100, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px 48px' }}>

        {/* ── Page header ── */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 28 }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => router.push('/inventory')}
            style={{
              display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
              fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: '#64748b',
              background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
              padding: '6px 0', marginBottom: 16, transition: 'color 0.15s',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#0f172a')}
            onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#64748b')}
          >
            <IconBack /> Back to Inventory
          </button>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-end', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 16 }}>
            <div>
              <h1 style={{ fontSize: 30, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a', letterSpacing: '-0.03em', margin: 0 }}>
                Reagent Usage Logs
              </h1>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#94a3b8', marginTop: 6, marginBottom: 0 }}>
                {loading ? 'Loading…' : `${logs.length} log entr${logs.length === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies'} found`}
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={handleExport}
              style={{
                display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                padding: '10px 18px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                border: 'none', borderRadius: 12, cursor: 'pointer',
                background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #059669 0%, #10b981 50%, #06b6d4 100%)',
                color: '#fff', boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(16,185,129,0.3)',
                transition: 'all 0.15s',
              }}
              onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)'; e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 6px 20px rgba(16,185,129,0.4)' }}
              onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'none'; e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 4px 14px rgba(16,185,129,0.3)' }}
            >
              <IconDownload /> Download CSV
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── Filter card ── */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
          borderRadius: 20, boxShadow: '0 8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)',
          padding: '20px 24px', marginBottom: 20,
        }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginBottom: 18 }}>
            <span style={{
              width: 30, height: 30, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
              background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(16,185,129,0.12), rgba(6,182,212,0.08))',
              borderRadius: 9, color: '#059669',
            }}>
              <IconFilter />
            </span>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: 0 }}>Filter Logs</h2>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
            <div>
              <label style={lS}>From Date</label>
              <input type="date" value={from} onChange={e => setFrom(e.target.value)} style={iS} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={lS}>To Date</label>
              <input type="date" value={to} onChange={e => setTo(e.target.value)} style={iS} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={lS}>Reagent</label>
              <select value={itemId} onChange={e => setItemId(e.target.value)} style={{ ...iS, minWidth: 180 }}>
                <option value="">All reagents</option>
                {items.map(item => (
                  <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={loadLogs}
              style={{
                display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                padding: '9px 20px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                border: 'none', borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer',
                background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #059669, #10b981)',
                color: '#fff', boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(16,185,129,0.25)',
                transition: 'all 0.15s',
              }}
              onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)')}
              onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'none')}
            >
              Apply Filters
            </button>
            {hasFilters && (
              <button
                onClick={() => { setFrom(''); setTo(''); setItemId('') }}
                style={{
                  fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: '#64748b',
                  background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
                  textDecoration: 'underline', paddingBottom: 2,
                }}
              >
                Clear
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── Logs table ── */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
          borderRadius: 20, boxShadow: '0 8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}>
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
            padding: '16px 24px',
            borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
            background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.6)',
          }}>
            <span style={{
              width: 30, height: 30, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
              background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(16,185,129,0.12), rgba(6,182,212,0.08))',
              borderRadius: 9, color: '#059669',
            }}>
              <IconReagent />
            </span>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: 0 }}>
              Usage Records
            </h2>
            {!loading && (
              <span style={{
                marginLeft: 6, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
                padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 20,
                background: 'rgba(209,250,229,0.8)', color: '#059669',
              }}>
                {logs.length}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div style={{ padding: '56px 24px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
              <IconSpinner />
              <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13, marginTop: 12 }}>Loading logs…</p>
            </div>
          ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
            <div style={{ padding: '56px 24px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 32, marginBottom: 12 }}>📋</div>
              <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14, margin: 0 }}>
                No log entries match your filters.
              </p>
              {hasFilters && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setFrom(''); setTo(''); setItemId('') }}
                  style={{ marginTop: 12, fontSize: 13, color: '#059669', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                  Clear filters
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
              <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: 13 }}>
                <thead>
                  <tr style={{ background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.9)', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)' }}>
                    {['Reagent', 'Qty Used', 'Purpose', 'Request / Cluster', 'Used By', 'Date'].map(h => (
                      <th key={h} style={{
                        padding: '11px 16px', textAlign: 'left',
                        fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b',
                        textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                      }}>
                        {h}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {logs.map((log, i) => (
                    <tr
                      key={log.id}
                      style={{
                        borderBottom: i < logs.length - 1 ? '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.7)' : 'none',
                        background: i % 2 === 0 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.3)',
                        transition: 'background 0.1s',
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(240,253,244,0.6)')}
                      onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = i % 2 === 0 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.3)')}
                    >
                      {/* Reagent */}
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px' }}>
                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#1e293b' }}>{log.item.name}</div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8', marginTop: 2 }}>{log.item.unit}</div>
                      </td>

                      {/* Qty */}
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px' }}>
                        <span style={{
                          fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b',
                          background: 'rgba(209,250,229,0.6)',
                          padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 12,
                        }}>
                          {log.quantityUsed} {log.item.unit}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* Purpose */}
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', color: '#475569' }}>{log.purpose}</td>

                      {/* Request / Cluster */}
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px' }}>
                        {log.request ? (
                          <>
                            <div style={{
                              fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
                              color: '#6366f1', background: 'rgba(238,242,255,0.7)',
                              padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 6, display: 'inline-block',
                              marginBottom: 3,
                            }}>
                              {log.request.requestCode}
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8' }}>{log.request.sampleName}</div>
                          </>
                        ) : log.cluster ? (
                          <>
                            <div style={{
                              fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
                              color: '#f59e0b', background: 'rgba(254,243,199,0.7)',
                              padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 6, display: 'inline-block',
                              marginBottom: 3,
                            }}>
                              Cluster
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8' }}>{log.cluster.name}</div>
                          </>
                        ) : (
                          <div style={{
                            fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: '#94a3b8',
                            fontStyle: 'italic',
                          }}>
                            —
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      {/* Used By */}
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', color: '#475569' }}>{log.usedBy.name}</td>

                      {/* Date */}
                      <td style={{ padding: '13px 16px', color: '#94a3b8', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                        {fmt(log.usedAt)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>
    </>
  )
}


===== pages\inventory\new.tsx =====

// pages/inventory/new.tsx
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

const TYPE_OPTIONS = ['CHEMICAL', 'CONSUMABLE']
const UNIT_OPTIONS = ['mg', 'g', 'kg', 'mL', 'µL', 'L', 'packets', 'vials', 'tubes', 'boxes', 'units']

type Lab = { id: number; name: string }

type FormState = {
  name:              string
  catalogueNumber:   string
  brand:             string
  category:          string
  type:              string
  labId:             string
  quantity:          string
  unit:              string
  minThreshold:      string
  manufacturingDate: string
  expiryDate:        string
  notes:             string
}

// ── Icons ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const IconFlask = () => (

      d="M21 7.5l-9-5.25L3 7.5m18 0l-9 5.25m9-5.25v9l-9 5.25M3 7.5l9 5.25M3 7.5v9l9 5.25m0-9v9" />

      d="M19.5 14.25v-2.625a3.375 3.375 0 00-3.375-3.375h-1.5A1.125 1.125 0 0113.5 7.125v-1.5a3.375 3.375 0 00-3.375-3.375H8.25m0 12.75h7.5m-7.5 3H12M10.5 2.25H5.625c-.621 0-1.125.504-1.125 1.125v17.25c0 .621.504 1.125 1.125 1.125h12.75c.621 0 1.125-.504 1.125-1.125V11.25a9 9 0 00-9-9z" />

    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M10.5 19.5L3 12m0 0l7.5-7.5M3 12h18" />
  </svg>
)

const IconSpinner = () => (
  <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2.5}
    style={{ animation: 'spin 0.8s linear infinite' }}>
    <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.25} />
