// ── Shared styles ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const IC = [
  'w-full',
  'border border-gray-200',
  'rounded-xl',
  'px-3 py-2.5',
  'text-sm text-gray-800',
  'bg-white/70',
  'transition-all duration-200',
  'focus:outline-none',
  'focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-200/50',
  'focus:border-emerald-400',
  'placeholder:text-gray-400',
].join(' ')

const LC = 'block text-[11px] font-700 text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5'

// ── Section card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function SectionCard({ icon, title, children }: {
  icon: React.ReactNode; title: string; children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
      border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
      borderRadius: 20,
      boxShadow: '0 8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)',
      padding: '28px 28px',
    }}>
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
        marginBottom: 24, paddingBottom: 16,
        borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
      }}>
        <span style={{
          width: 34, height: 34,
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
          background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(16,185,129,0.12), rgba(6,182,212,0.08))',
          borderRadius: 10, color: '#059669',
        }}>
          {icon}
        </span>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: 0, letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}>
          {title}
        </h2>
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

// ── Field wrapper ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function Field({ label, required, hint, children }: {
  label: string; required?: boolean; hint?: string; children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className={LC} style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>
        {label}
        {required && <span style={{ color: '#ef4444', marginLeft: 3 }}>*</span>}
      </label>
      {children}
      {hint && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8', marginTop: 5, marginBottom: 0 }}>{hint}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ── Page ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export default function NewInventoryPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string

  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>({
    name: '', catalogueNumber: '', brand: '', category: '',
    type: 'CHEMICAL', labId: '', quantity: '', unit: '',
    minThreshold: '0', manufacturingDate: '', expiryDate: '', notes: '',
  })
  const [labs, setLabs]             = useState<Lab[]>([])
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError]           = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    fetch('/api/labs')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then((data: Lab[]) => setLabs(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []))
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [session])

  if (status === 'loading') return (
    <div style={{ padding: 32, color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>Loading...</div>
  )
  if (!session) { router.push('/login'); return null }
  if (role !== 'ADMIN' && role !== 'INVENTORY_MANAGER') {
    return <div style={{ padding: 32, color: '#dc2626' }}>Access denied.</div>
  }

  const today     = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
  const isExpired = form.expiryDate && form.expiryDate < today

  function handleChange(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) {
    const { name, value } = e.target
    setForm(f => ({ ...f, [name]: value }))
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!form.name.trim())     return setError('Material name is required')
    if (!form.labId)           return setError('Lab is required')
    if (!form.quantity.trim()) return setError('Available Quantity is required')
    if (!form.unit.trim())     return setError('Unit of Qty is required')
    setSubmitting(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/inventory', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name:              form.name,
          catalogueNumber:   form.catalogueNumber   || null,
          brand:             form.brand             || null,
          category:          form.category          || 'General',
          type:              form.type              || 'CHEMICAL',
          labId:             parseInt(form.labId),
          quantity:          parseFloat(form.quantity),
          unit:              form.unit,
          minThreshold:      parseFloat(form.minThreshold) || 0,
          manufacturingDate: form.manufacturingDate || null,
          expiryDate:        form.expiryDate        || null,
          notes:             form.notes             || null,
        }),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Submission failed')
      router.push('/inventory')
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Unknown error')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <style>{`@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }`}</style>

      {/* Page content — bottom padding clears sticky footer */}
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 680, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px 120px' }}>

        {/* Page header */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 32 }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => router.back()}
            style={{
              display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
              fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: '#64748b',
              background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
              padding: '6px 0', marginBottom: 16, transition: 'color 0.15s',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#0f172a')}
            onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#64748b')}
          >
            <IconArrowLeft /> Back
          </button>

          <h1 style={{
            fontSize: 30, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a',
            letterSpacing: '-0.03em', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1,
          }}>
            Add Inventory Item
          </h1>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#94a3b8', marginTop: 6, marginBottom: 0 }}>
            Register a new chemical or consumable item to the lab inventory.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Error banner */}
        {error && (
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
            background: 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)', border: '1px solid rgba(252,165,165,0.6)',
            borderRadius: 12, padding: '12px 16px',
            color: '#b91c1c', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, marginBottom: 20,
          }}>
            <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
              <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
                d="M12 9v3.75m-9.303 3.376c-.866 1.5.217 3.374 1.948 3.374h14.71c1.73 0 2.813-1.874 1.948-3.374L13.949 3.378c-.866-1.5-3.032-1.5-3.898 0L2.697 16.126zM12 15.75h.007v.008H12v-.008z" />
            </svg>
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>

            {/* ── Section 1: Item Details ── */}
            <SectionCard icon={<IconFlask />} title="Item Details">
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>

                {/* Material — full width */}
                <Field label="Material" required>
                  <input type="text" name="name" value={form.name}
                    onChange={handleChange} required
                    placeholder="e.g. Taq DNA Polymerase"
                    className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                </Field>

                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '16px 20px' }}>

                  <Field label="Catalogue Number">
                    <input type="text" name="catalogueNumber" value={form.catalogueNumber}
                      onChange={handleChange} placeholder="e.g. CAT-00123"
                      className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                  </Field>

                  <Field label="Brand of Supply">
                    <input type="text" name="brand" value={form.brand}
                      onChange={handleChange} placeholder="e.g. Sigma, Thermo Fisher"
                      className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                  </Field>

                  <Field label="Category">
                    <input type="text" name="category" value={form.category}
                      onChange={handleChange} placeholder="e.g. Buffer, Enzyme, Plasticware"
                      className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                  </Field>

                  <Field label="Type">
                    <select name="type" value={form.type}
                      onChange={handleChange} className={`${IC} field-input`}>
                      {TYPE_OPTIONS.map(t => (
                        <option key={t} value={t}>
                          {t === 'CHEMICAL' ? '🧪 Chemical' : '🧴 Consumable'}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </Field>

                  {/* Lab — full width */}
                  <div style={{ gridColumn: 'span 2' }}>
                    <Field label="Lab" required>
                      <select name="labId" value={form.labId}
                        onChange={handleChange} required className={`${IC} field-input`}>
                        <option value="">Select lab…</option>
                        {labs.map(l => (
                          <option key={l.id} value={l.id}>{l.name}</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </Field>
                  </div>

                </div>
              </div>
            </SectionCard>

            {/* ── Section 2: Quantity ── */}
            <SectionCard icon={<IconCube />} title="Quantity">
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '16px 20px' }}>

                <Field label="Available Qty" required>
                  <input type="number" name="quantity" value={form.quantity}
                    onChange={handleChange} min={0} step="0.01" required
                    className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                </Field>

                <Field label="Unit of Qty" required>
                  <select name="unit" value={form.unit}
                    onChange={handleChange} required className={`${IC} field-input`}>
                    <option value="">Select unit…</option>
                    {UNIT_OPTIONS.map(u => (
                      <option key={u} value={u}>{u}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </Field>

                <div style={{ gridColumn: 'span 2' }}>
                  <Field
                    label="Min Threshold"
                    hint="Triggers a Low Stock alert when quantity falls below this value."
                  >
                    <input type="number" name="minThreshold" value={form.minThreshold}
                      onChange={handleChange} min={0} step="0.01"
                      className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                  </Field>
                </div>

              </div>
            </SectionCard>

            {/* ── Section 3: Dates ── */}
            <SectionCard icon={<IconCalendar />} title="Dates">
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '16px 20px' }}>

                <Field label="Mfg Date">
                  <input type="date" name="manufacturingDate" value={form.manufacturingDate}
                    onChange={handleChange} className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                </Field>

                <Field label="Expiry Date">
                  <input type="date" name="expiryDate" value={form.expiryDate}
                    onChange={handleChange} className={`${IC} field-input`} />
                </Field>

              </div>

              {isExpired && (
                <div style={{
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                  marginTop: 14,
                  background: 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)',
                  border: '1px solid rgba(252,165,165,0.5)',
                  borderRadius: 12, padding: '10px 14px',
                  color: '#b91c1c', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500,
                }}>
                  <svg width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
                      d="M12 9v3.75m-9.303 3.376c-.866 1.5.217 3.374 1.948 3.374h14.71c1.73 0 2.813-1.874 1.948-3.374L13.949 3.378c-.866-1.5-3.032-1.5-3.898 0L2.697 16.126zM12 15.75h.007v.008H12v-.008z" />
                  </svg>
                  Expiry date is in the past — this item will be flagged as <strong style={{ marginLeft: 3 }}>Expiring</strong>.
                </div>
              )}
            </SectionCard>

            {/* ── Section 4: Notes ── */}
            <SectionCard icon={<IconNote />} title="Notes">
              <textarea
                name="notes" value={form.notes} onChange={handleChange}
                rows={3}
                placeholder="Any additional notes about this item…"
                className={`${IC} field-input`}
                style={{ resize: 'vertical' }}
              />
            </SectionCard>

          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {/* ── Sticky action footer ── */}
      <div style={{
        position: 'fixed', bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0, zIndex: 50,
        backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
        WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)',
        borderTop: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
        boxShadow: '0 -8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
        padding: '14px 24px',
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10,
      }}>
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', marginRight: 'auto', marginBottom: 0 }}>
          Fields marked <span style={{ color: '#ef4444' }}>*</span> are required
        </p>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => router.push('/inventory')}
          style={{
            padding: '9px 20px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,
            border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
            borderRadius: 12, cursor: 'pointer',
            background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151',
            transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
          }}
          onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = '#f8fafc')}
          onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)')}
        >
          Cancel
        </button>

        <button
          type="submit"
          form=""
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={handleSubmit}
          style={{
            padding: '9px 28px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
            border: 'none', borderRadius: 12,
            cursor: submitting ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
            background: submitting
              ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.5)'
              : 'linear-gradient(135deg, #059669 0%, #10b981 50%, #06b6d4 100%)',
            color: '#fff',
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
            boxShadow: submitting ? 'none' : '0 4px 14px rgba(16,185,129,0.35)',
            transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
            opacity: submitting ? 0.75 : 1,
          }}
          onMouseEnter={e => { if (!submitting) e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)' }}
          onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(0)' }}
        >
          {submitting && <IconSpinner />}
          {submitting ? 'Saving…' : 'Add Item'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}


===== pages\inventory\transfer.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

type Transfer = {
  id: number
  quantity: number
  reason: string | null
  status: string
  createdAt: string
  fromItem: { name: string; unit: string; lab: { name: string } }
  toItem:   { name: string; unit: string; lab: { name: string } }
  requestedBy: { name: string }
}

// ── KPI Card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function KpiCard({
  label, value, sub, accent,
}: { label: string; value: number | string; sub?: string; accent: string }) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      flex: 1,
      minWidth: 140,
      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      border: '1px solid rgba(229,231,235,0.8)',
      borderRadius: 16,
      padding: '18px 22px',
      boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4,
      borderTop: `3px solid ${accent}`,
    }}>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.07em', margin: 0 }}>
        {label}
      </p>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
        {value}
      </p>
      {sub && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>{sub}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ── Status Badge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const STATUS_STYLES: Record<string, { bg: string; color: string; dot: string }> = {
  PENDING:   { bg: '#fef9c3', color: '#854d0e', dot: '#fbbf24' },
  APPROVED:  { bg: '#f0fdf4', color: '#15803d', dot: '#22c55e' },
  REJECTED:  { bg: '#fef2f2', color: '#991b1b', dot: '#f87171' },
  CANCELLED: { bg: '#f1f5f9', color: '#475569', dot: '#94a3b8' },
}

function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: string }) {
  const s = STATUS_STYLES[status] ?? { bg: '#f1f5f9', color: '#475569', dot: '#94a3b8' }
  return (
    <span style={{
      display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5,
      padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
      background: s.bg, color: s.color, letterSpacing: '0.03em',
    }}>
      <span style={{ width: 5, height: 5, borderRadius: '50%', background: s.dot, display: 'inline-block' }} />
      {status.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
    </span>
  )
}

// ── Shared table cell components ──────────────────────────────────────────────
const TH = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => (
  <th style={{
    padding: '11px 16px',
    textAlign: 'left',
    fontWeight: 700,
    fontSize: 11,
    color: '#64748b',
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
    letterSpacing: '0.06em',
    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
  }}>
    {children}
  </th>
)

const TD = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => (
  <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', fontSize: 13, color: '#374151' }}>
    {children}
  </td>
)

export default function TransfersPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [transfers, setTransfers] = useState<Transfer[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading]     = useState(true)
  const [acting, setActing]       = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [error, setError]         = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return
    if (!session) { router.push('/login'); return }
    const role = (session.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string
    if (!['BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER', 'ADMIN', 'MANAGEMENT'].includes(role)) {
      router.push('/inventory')
    }
  }, [session, status, router])

function loadTransfers() {
  fetch('/api/inventory/transfers')
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => { setTransfers(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []); setLoading(false) })
    .catch(() => { setTransfers([]); setLoading(false) })
}

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    loadTransfers()
  }, [session])

  async function handleApprove(transferId: number) {
    setActing(transferId)
    setError('')
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/inventory/transfer', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ transferId })
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) { setError(data.error || 'Approval failed'); return }
      loadTransfers()
    } catch {
      setError('Network error')
    } finally {
      setActing(null)
    }
  }

  if (status === 'loading' || loading) {
    return (
      <div style={{ padding: 32, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>
        <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
          style={{ animation: 'spin 1s linear infinite' }}>
          <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.3}/>
          <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 00-9-9" strokeLinecap="round"/>
        </svg>
        Loading transfers…
        <style>{`@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }`}</style>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const pending   = transfers.filter(t => t.status === 'PENDING').length
  const approved  = transfers.filter(t => t.status === 'APPROVED').length

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '24px 28px', maxWidth: '100%' }}>

      {/* ── KPI Strip ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <KpiCard
          label="Total Transfers"
          value={transfers.length}
          sub="All transfer requests"
          accent="#10b981"
        />
        <KpiCard
          label="Pending Approval"
          value={pending}
          sub="Awaiting review"
          accent="#f59e0b"
        />
        <KpiCard
          label="Approved"
          value={approved}
          sub={transfers.length > 0 ? `${Math.round((approved / transfers.length) * 100)}% approval rate` : '—'}
          accent="#3b82f6"
        />
      </div>

      {/* ── Error banner ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      {error && (
        <div style={{
          background: '#fef2f2',
          border: '1px solid rgba(254,202,202,0.8)',
          borderRadius: 12,
          padding: '12px 16px',
          marginBottom: 16,
          fontSize: 13,
          color: '#991b1b',
        }}>
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Main card ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div style={{
        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
        WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
        border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
        borderRadius: 20,
        boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}>

        {/* Card header */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '16px 20px',
          borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.7)',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
        }}>
          <div>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>
              Pending Transfers
            </p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '2px 0 0' }}>
              {pending} transfer{pending !== 1 ? 's' : ''} awaiting approval
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Table */}
        {transfers.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{
            padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14,
          }}>
            No pending transfers. All caught up.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            <table style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: 13, borderCollapse: 'collapse' }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                  <TH>Material</TH>
                  <TH>Qty</TH>
                  <TH>From Lab</TH>
                  <TH>To Lab</TH>
                  <TH>Reason</TH>
                  <TH>Requested By</TH>
                  <TH>Date</TH>
                  <TH>Status</TH>
                  <TH>Action</TH>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {transfers.map((t, i) => (
                  <tr
                    key={t.id}
                    style={{
                      borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
                      background: i % 2 === 0 ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)',
                      transition: 'background 0.15s ease',
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => {
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLTableRowElement).style.background = 'rgba(236,253,245,0.7)'
                    }}
                    onMouseLeave={e => {
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLTableRowElement).style.background =
                        i % 2 === 0 ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)'
                    }}
                  >
                    <TD>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f172a' }}>{t.fromItem.name}</span>
                    </TD>
                    <TD>
                      <span style={{ fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
                        {t.quantity} <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>{t.fromItem.unit}</span>
                      </span>
                    </TD>
                    <TD>
                      <span style={{
                        display: 'inline-block',
                        padding: '2px 8px',
                        borderRadius: 6,
                        fontSize: 11,
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        background: 'rgba(239,246,255,0.8)',
                        color: '#1d4ed8',
                      }}>
                        {t.fromItem.lab.name}
                      </span>
                    </TD>
                    <TD>
                      <span style={{
                        display: 'inline-block',
                        padding: '2px 8px',
                        borderRadius: 6,
                        fontSize: 11,
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        background: 'rgba(240,253,244,0.8)',
                        color: '#15803d',
                      }}>
                        {t.toItem.lab.name}
                      </span>
                    </TD>
                    <TD>{t.reason ?? <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>—</span>}</TD>
                    <TD>{t.requestedBy.name}</TD>
                    <TD>
                      <span style={{ color: '#64748b' }}>
                        {new Date(t.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                          day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
                        })}
                      </span>
                    </TD>
                    <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                      <StatusBadge status={t.status} />
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                      {t.status === 'PENDING' && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleApprove(t.id)}
                          disabled={acting === t.id}
                          style={{
                            padding: '5px 16px',
                            background: acting === t.id ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.5)' : '#10b981',
                            color: '#fff',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: 8,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            cursor: acting === t.id ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                            transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                          }}
                          onMouseEnter={e => {
                            if (acting !== t.id)
                              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = '#059669'
                          }}
                          onMouseLeave={e => {
                            if (acting !== t.id)
                              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = '#10b981'
                          }}
                        >
                          {acting === t.id ? 'Approving…' : 'Approve'}
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Footer */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '12px 20px',
          borderTop: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.5)',
        }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>
            {transfers.length} total transfer request{transfers.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\inventory\transfers\index.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

type Transfer = {
  id: number
  quantity: number
  reason: string | null
  status: string
  createdAt: string
  fromItem: { name: string; unit: string; lab: { name: string } }
  toItem:   { name: string; unit: string; lab: { name: string } }
  requestedBy: { name: string }
}

// ── KPI Card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function KpiCard({
  label, value, sub, accent,
}: { label: string; value: number | string; sub?: string; accent: string }) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      flex: 1,
      minWidth: 140,
      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      border: '1px solid rgba(229,231,235,0.8)',
      borderRadius: 16,
      padding: '18px 22px',
      boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4,
      borderTop: `3px solid ${accent}`,
    }}>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.07em', margin: 0 }}>
        {label}
      </p>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
        {value}
      </p>
      {sub && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>{sub}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ── Status Badge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

                          onMouseLeave={e => {
                            if (acting !== t.id)
                              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = '#10b981'
                          }}
                        >
                          {acting === t.id ? 'Approving…' : 'Approve'}
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Footer */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '12px 20px',
          borderTop: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.5)',
        }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>
            {transfers.length} total transfer request{transfers.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\reports\monthly.tsx =====

// pages/reports/monthly.tsx
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter }  from 'next/router'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

type MonthlyReportData = {
  month:             string
  year:              number
  totalSubmitted:    number
  totalCompleted:    number
  completionRate:    number
  avgTurnaroundDays: number
  onTimePct:         number
  byLab:             Array<{ labName: string; submitted: number; completed: number }>
  topReagents:       Array<{ name: string; unit: string; totalUsed: number }>
  byPhase:           Record<string, number>
}

const MONTHS = [
  'January','February','March','April','May','June',
  'July','August','September','October','November','December',
]

export default function MonthlyReportPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const now    = new Date()

  const [year,    setYear]    = useState(now.getFullYear())
  const [month,   setMonth]   = useState(now.getMonth() + 1)
  const [data,    setData]    = useState<MonthlyReportData | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'unauthenticated') { router.push('/'); return }
    const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role
if (status === 'authenticated' && !['BIOTECHNOLOGIST', 'INVENTORY_MANAGER', 'ADMIN', 'MANAGEMENT'].includes(role as string)) {
  router.push('/')
    }
  }, [session, status, router])

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    let alive = true
    void (async () => {
      if (alive) setLoading(true)
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `/api/reports/monthly?year=${year}&month=${month}`,
          { signal: controller.signal },
        )
        if (res.ok && alive) setData((await res.json()) as MonthlyReportData)
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name !== 'AbortError') console.error(err)
      } finally {
        if (alive) setLoading(false)
      }
    })()
    return () => { alive = false; controller.abort() }
  }, [year, month])

  function downloadPDF() {
    window.open(`/api/reports/monthly-pdf?year=${year}&month=${month}`, '_blank')
  }

  const cardStyle = {
    background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
    backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
    border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)',
    borderRadius: '16px',
    boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
  }

  const KPI_META = [
    { label: 'Submitted',       key: 'totalSubmitted',    accent: '#3b82f6', bg: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.07)',  fmt: (v: number) => String(v) },
    { label: 'Completed',       key: 'totalCompleted',    accent: '#10b981', bg: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.07)',  fmt: (v: number) => String(v) },
    { label: 'Completion Rate', key: 'completionRate',    accent: '#8b5cf6', bg: 'rgba(139,92,246,0.07)', fmt: (v: number) => `${v}%`   },
    { label: 'Avg Turnaround',  key: 'avgTurnaroundDays', accent: '#f59e0b', bg: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.07)', fmt: (v: number) => `${v}d`   },
  ] as const

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '32px', minHeight: '100vh', background: '#f8fafc', fontFamily: 'inherit' }}>
      {/* Header row */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '28px', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '16px' }}>
        <div>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>
            Monthly Operations Report
          </h1>
          <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', marginTop: '4px' }}>
            Summary of lab activity and reagent consumption.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          {/* Month select */}
          <select
            value={month}
            onChange={e => setMonth(Number(e.target.value))}
            style={{
              border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.9)', borderRadius: '8px',
              padding: '8px 12px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#374151',
              background: '#fff', outline: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
            }}
          >
            {MONTHS.map((m, i) => <option key={m} value={i + 1}>{m}</option>)}
          </select>

          {/* Year select */}
          <select
            value={year}
            onChange={e => setYear(Number(e.target.value))}
            style={{
              border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.9)', borderRadius: '8px',
              padding: '8px 12px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#374151',
              background: '#fff', outline: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
            }}
          >
            {[0, 1, 2].map(d => {
              const y = now.getFullYear() - d
              return <option key={y} value={y}>{y}</option>
            })}
          </select>

          {/* Download PDF */}
          <button
            onClick={downloadPDF}
            style={{
              background: '#3b82f6', color: '#fff', border: 'none',
              borderRadius: '8px', padding: '8px 18px', fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer',
              boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(59,130,246,0.25)',
            }}
          >
            ⬇ Download PDF
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div style={{ ...cardStyle, padding: '48px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
          Loading report…
        </div>
      ) : !data ? null : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>

          {/* KPI cards */}
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: '14px' }}>
            {KPI_META.map(card => (
              <div key={card.label} style={{
                ...cardStyle,
                padding: '20px 18px',
                background: card.bg,
                border: `1px solid ${card.accent}22`,
              }}>
                <p style={{
                  fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, color: card.accent,
                  textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', margin: 0,
                }}>
                  {card.label}
                </p>
                <p style={{ fontSize: '28px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '8px 0 0', lineHeight: 1 }}>
                  {card.fmt(data[card.key] as number)}
                </p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Top Reagents */}
          <div style={{ ...cardStyle, padding: '24px', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
            <h2 style={{
              fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a',
              textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', margin: '0 0 16px',
            }}>
              Top Reagents Consumed
            </h2>
            <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                  {['Reagent', 'Unit', 'Total Used'].map((h, i) => (
                    <th key={h} style={{
                      padding: '10px 16px', textAlign: i === 2 ? 'right' : 'left',
                      fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#64748b',
                      textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                    }}>{h}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.topReagents.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={3} style={{ padding: '24px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                      No reagent usage recorded this month.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : data.topReagents.map((reagent, i) => (
                  <tr
                    key={reagent.name}
                    style={{
                      borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.5)',
                      background: i % 2 === 1 ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)' : 'transparent',
                      transition: 'background 0.15s',
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(236,253,245,0.7)')}
                    onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = i % 2 === 1 ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)' : 'transparent')}
                  >
                    <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', fontWeight: 500, color: '#0f172a' }}>{reagent.name}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', color: '#64748b' }}>{reagent.unit}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f172a' }}>{reagent.totalUsed}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          {/* Performance by Lab */}
          <div style={{ ...cardStyle, padding: '24px', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
            <h2 style={{
              fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a',
              textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', margin: '0 0 16px',
            }}>
              Performance by Lab
            </h2>
            <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: '#f8fafc', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)' }}>
                  {['Lab', 'Submitted', 'Completed', 'Rate'].map((h, i) => (
                    <th key={h} style={{
                      padding: '10px 16px', textAlign: i === 0 ? 'left' : 'right',
                      fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#64748b',
                      textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                    }}>{h}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.byLab.map((lab, i) => {
                  const rate = lab.submitted > 0
                    ? Math.round((lab.completed / lab.submitted) * 100)
                    : null
                  const rateColour = rate === null ? '#94a3b8' : rate >= 80 ? '#10b981' : rate >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444'
                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={lab.labName}
                      style={{
                        borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.5)',
                        background: i % 2 === 1 ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)' : 'transparent',
                        transition: 'background 0.15s',
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(236,253,245,0.7)')}
                      onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = i % 2 === 1 ? 'rgba(248,250,252,0.4)' : 'transparent')}
                    >
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', fontWeight: 500, color: '#0f172a' }}>{lab.labName}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', textAlign: 'right', color: '#374151' }}>{lab.submitted}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', textAlign: 'right', color: '#374151' }}>{lab.completed}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 16px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                        {rate === null ? (
                          <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>—</span>
                        ) : (
                          <span style={{
                            display: 'inline-block',
                            background: `${rateColour}14`,
                            color: rateColour,
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            fontSize: '12px',
                            padding: '2px 10px',
                            borderRadius: '20px',
                          }}>
                            {rate}%
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== pages\requests\index.tsx =====

// pages/requests/index.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
import Link from 'next/link'

type LabRequest = {
  id: number
  requestCode: string
  sampleName: string
  crop: string | null
  season: string | null
  year: number | null
  program: string | null
  purpose: string | null
  materialStage: string | null
  projectCode: string | null
  project: string | null
  testType: string
  analysisToBeDone: string | null
  traitGene: string | null
  markersPerSample: number | null
  markerType: string | null
  predictedSamples: number | null
  predictedDataPoints: number | null
  tentativeSamplingDate: string | null
  priority: number
  expectedByDate: string | null
  notes: string | null
  actualSamples: number | null
  actualDataPoints: number | null
  dateOfSampling: string | null
  dateOfDispatch: string | null
  dodCommittedByLab: string | null
  dnaExtractionDate: string | null
  genotypingDate: string | null
  resultsCommunicationDate: string | null
  status: string
  currentPhase: string
  submittedAt: string
  completedAt: string | null
  submittedBy: { name: string; email: string }
  assignedLab: { name: string } | null
}

function fmt(d: string | null) {
  if (!d) return '—'
  return new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
  })
}

const PAGE_SIZE = 10
const TOTAL_COLS = 29

// ── Brand teal tokens ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const TEAL_ACCENT   = '#14b8a6'
const TEAL_BORDER   = '#99f6e4'
const TEAL_SEP      = 'rgba(153,246,228,0.55)'

// ── Shared table header ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const TH = ({
  children,
  style,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode
  style?: React.CSSProperties
}) => (
  <th style={{
    padding: '11px 12px',
    textAlign: 'left',
    fontWeight: 700,
    fontSize: 11,
    color: '#64748b',
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
    letterSpacing: '0.06em',
    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
    background: '#f8fafc',
    borderRight: `1px solid ${TEAL_SEP}`,
    ...style,
  }}>
    {children}
  </th>
)

const TD = ({
  children,
  maxW,
  style,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode
  maxW?: number
  style?: React.CSSProperties
}) => (
  <td style={{
    padding: '12px 12px',
    fontSize: 13,
    color: '#374151',
    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
    maxWidth: maxW ?? undefined,
    overflow: maxW ? 'hidden' : undefined,
    textOverflow: maxW ? 'ellipsis' : undefined,
    ...style,
  }}
    title={maxW && typeof children === 'string' ? children : undefined}
  >
    {children}
  </td>
)

const STATUS_STYLES: Record<string, { bg: string; color: string; dot: string }> = {
  COMPLETED:     { bg: '#f0fdf4', color: '#15803d', dot: '#22c55e' },
  SUBMITTED:     { bg: '#eff6ff', color: '#1d4ed8', dot: '#60a5fa' },
  RECEIVED:      { bg: '#f0f9ff', color: '#0369a1', dot: '#38bdf8' },
  DNA_ISOLATION: { bg: '#fefce8', color: '#a16207', dot: '#facc15' },
  IN_PROGRESS:   { bg: '#fefce8', color: '#a16207', dot: '#facc15' },
  GENOTYPING:    { bg: '#fdf4ff', color: '#7e22ce', dot: '#c084fc' },
  ANALYSIS:      { bg: '#fff7ed', color: '#c2410c', dot: '#fb923c' },
  PENDING:       { bg: '#fef9c3', color: '#854d0e', dot: '#fbbf24' },
  CANCELLED:     { bg: '#fef2f2', color: '#b91c1c', dot: '#f87171' },
}

function StatusBadge({ status, subLabel }: { status: string; subLabel?: string }) {
  const s = STATUS_STYLES[status] ?? { bg: '#f1f5f9', color: '#475569', dot: '#94a3b8' }
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'inline-flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 2 }}>
      <span style={{
        display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5,
        padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600,
        background: s.bg, color: s.color, letterSpacing: '0.03em',
        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
      }}>
        <span style={{ width: 5, height: 5, borderRadius: '50%', background: s.dot, flexShrink: 0 }} />
        {status.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
      </span>
      {subLabel && (
        <span style={{
          fontSize: 10, color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: 500,
          paddingLeft: 10, letterSpacing: '0.03em',
        }}>
          {subLabel}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function KpiCard({ label, value, sub, accent }: {
  label: string; value: number | string; sub?: string; accent: string
}) {
  return (
    <div style={{
      flex: 1, minWidth: 140,
      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
      border: '1px solid rgba(229,231,235,0.8)',
      borderRadius: 16, padding: '18px 22px',
      boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4,
      borderTop: `3px solid ${accent}`,
    }}>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.07em', margin: 0 }}>
        {label}
      </p>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>
        {value}
      </p>
      {sub && <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>{sub}</p>}
    </div>
  )
}

const LEFT_PROJECT  = 0
const LEFT_ANALYSIS = 200
const LEFT_TRAIT    = 360

export default function RequestsPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [requests, setRequests]         = useState<LabRequest[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading]           = useState(true)
  const [showArchived, setShowArchived] = useState(false)
  const [statusF, setStatusF]           = useState('ALL')
  const [yearF, setYearF]               = useState('ALL')
  const [seasonF, setSeasonF]           = useState('ALL')
  const [cropF, setCropF]               = useState('ALL')
  const [projectCodeF, setProjectCodeF] = useState('')
  const [analysisF, setAnalysisF]       = useState('ALL')
  const [markerTypeF, setMarkerTypeF]   = useState('ALL')
  const [page, setPage]                 = useState(1)
  const [total, setTotal]               = useState(0)
  const [selected, setSelected]         = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
  const [bulkLoading, setBulkLoading]   = useState(false)
  const [years, setYears]               = useState<number[]>([])
  const [kpiTotal, setKpiTotal]         = useState(0)
  const [kpiCompleted, setKpiCompleted] = useState(0)
  const [kpiPending, setKpiPending]     = useState(0)

  const role = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role as string

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/requests?distinct=years')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => { if (Array.isArray(data)) setYears(data) })
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    fetch('/api/requests?page=1&limit=9999&archived=false')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (data?.requests) {
          const all: LabRequest[] = data.requests
          setKpiTotal(data.total ?? all.length)
          setKpiCompleted(all.filter(r => r.status === 'COMPLETED').length)
          setKpiPending(all.filter(r => r.status !== 'COMPLETED' && r.status !== 'CANCELLED').length)
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [session])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session && status !== 'loading') router.push('/login')
  }, [session, status, router])

  function buildUrl() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    if (statusF !== 'ALL')     params.set('status',      statusF)
    if (yearF !== 'ALL')       params.set('year',        yearF)
    if (seasonF !== 'ALL')     params.set('season',      seasonF)
    if (cropF !== 'ALL')       params.set('crop',        cropF)
    if (projectCodeF)          params.set('projectCode', projectCodeF)
    if (analysisF !== 'ALL')   params.set('analysis',    analysisF)
    if (markerTypeF !== 'ALL') params.set('markerType',  markerTypeF)
    params.set('archived', String(showArchived))
    params.set('page',     String(page))
    params.set('limit',    String(PAGE_SIZE))
    return `/api/requests?${params.toString()}`
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return
    const controller = new AbortController()
    async function fetchRequests() {
      setLoading(true)
      try {
        const res  = await fetch(buildUrl(), { signal: controller.signal })
        const json = await res.json()
        setRequests(json.requests)
        setTotal(json.total)
      } catch (err) {
        if ((err as Error).name !== 'AbortError') console.error(err)
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }
    fetchRequests()
    return () => controller.abort()
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [session, statusF, yearF, seasonF, cropF, projectCodeF, analysisF, markerTypeF, showArchived, page])

  function toggleOne(id: number) {
    setSelected(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev)
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id); else next.add(id)
      return next
    })
  }

  function toggleAll() {
    if (selected.size === requests.length) setSelected(new Set())
    else setSelected(new Set(requests.map(r => r.id)))
  }

  async function doBulk(action: 'cancel' | 'archive') {
    if (selected.size === 0) return
    if (!confirm(`${action === 'cancel' ? 'Cancel' : 'Archive'} ${selected.size} request(s)?`)) return
    setBulkLoading(true)
    const res = await fetch('/api/requests/bulk', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action, requestIds: Array.from(selected) }),
    })
    if (res.ok) { setSelected(new Set()); setPage(1) }
    setBulkLoading(false)
  }

  if (loading) return (
    <div style={{ padding: 32, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14 }}>
      <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
        style={{ animation: 'spin 1s linear infinite' }}>
        <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" strokeOpacity={0.3}/>
        <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 00-9-9" strokeLinecap="round"/>
      </svg>
      Loading requests…
      <style>{`@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }`}</style>
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '24px 28px', maxWidth: '100%' }}>

      {/* ── KPI Strip ── */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <KpiCard label="Total Requests" value={kpiTotal}     sub="All active requests"    accent="#10b981" />
        <KpiCard label="In Progress"    value={kpiPending}   sub="Awaiting completion"    accent="#f59e0b" />
        <KpiCard label="Completed"      value={kpiCompleted}
          sub={kpiTotal > 0 ? `${Math.round((kpiCompleted / kpiTotal) * 100)}% completion rate` : '—'}
          accent="#3b82f6" />
        <KpiCard label="Showing"        value={total}        sub="Matching current filters" accent="#8b5cf6" />
      </div>

      {/* ── Bulk toolbar ── */}
      {selected.size > 0 && (
        <div style={{
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 16,
          padding: '10px 16px', background: 'rgba(239,246,255,0.9)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(191,219,254,0.8)', borderRadius: 12,
        }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: '#1e40af' }}>{selected.size} selected</span>
          <button onClick={() => doBulk('cancel')} disabled={bulkLoading} style={{
            fontSize: 12, padding: '5px 14px', background: '#dc2626', color: '#fff',
            border: 'none', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 600,
          }}>Cancel Selected</button>
          <button onClick={() => doBulk('archive')} disabled={bulkLoading} style={{
            fontSize: 12, padding: '5px 14px', background: '#d97706', color: '#fff',
            border: 'none', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 600,
          }}>Archive Selected</button>
          <button onClick={() => setSelected(new Set())} style={{
            fontSize: 12, color: '#6b7280', background: 'none', border: 'none',
            cursor: 'pointer', marginLeft: 'auto', textDecoration: 'underline',
          }}>Clear selection</button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Main card ── */}
      <div style={{
        background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
        border: '1px solid rgba(226,232,240,0.8)', borderRadius: 20,
        boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0 10px 40px rgba(15,23,42,0.06)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}>

        {/* ── Filter bar ── */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '16px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 14, alignItems: 'flex-end',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.7)',
        }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Project Code</label>
            <input type="text" placeholder="e.g. ICTP002" value={projectCodeF}
              onChange={e => { setProjectCodeF(e.target.value); setPage(1) }}
              style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 140 }} />
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Year</label>
            <select value={yearF} onChange={e => { setYearF(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} style={inputStyle}>
              <option value="ALL">All Years</option>
              {years.map(y => <option key={y} value={String(y)}>{y}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Season</label>
            <select value={seasonF} onChange={e => { setSeasonF(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} style={inputStyle}>
              <option value="ALL">All Seasons</option>
              <option value="RABI">Rabi</option>
              <option value="SUMMER">Summer</option>
              <option value="Kharif">Kharif</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Crop</label>
            <select value={cropF} onChange={e => { setCropF(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} style={inputStyle}>
              <option value="ALL">All Crops</option>
              <option value="Rice">Rice</option>
              <option value="Cotton">Cotton</option>
              <option value="Corn">Corn</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Analysis</label>
            <select value={analysisF} onChange={e => { setAnalysisF(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} style={inputStyle}>
              <option value="ALL">All Analysis</option>
              <option value="Foreground">Foreground</option>
              <option value="Background">Background</option>
              <option value="Hybridity_Confirmation">Hybridity Confirmation</option>
              <option value="Purity_Analysis">Purity Analysis</option>
              <option value="Line Fingerprinting">Line Fingerprinting</option>
              <option value="Gene Profiling">Gene Profiling</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Marker Type</label>
            <select value={markerTypeF} onChange={e => { setMarkerTypeF(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} style={inputStyle}>
              <option value="ALL">All</option>
              <option value="SNP">SNP</option>
              <option value="SSR">SSR</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Status</label>
            <select value={statusF} onChange={e => { setStatusF(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} style={inputStyle}>
              <option value="ALL">All Status</option>
              <option value="SUBMITTED">Submitted</option>
              <option value="RECEIVED">Received</option>
              <option value="DNA_ISOLATION">DNA Isolation</option>
              <option value="GENOTYPING">Genotyping</option>
              <option value="ANALYSIS">Analysis</option>
              <option value="COMPLETED">Completed</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Archived</label>
            <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: 13,
              color: '#374151', padding: '7px 0', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
              <input type="checkbox" checked={showArchived}
                onChange={e => { setShowArchived(e.target.checked); setPage(1) }}
                style={{ accentColor: TEAL_ACCENT, width: 14, height: 14 }} />
              Show archived
            </label>
          </div>
          {(role === 'MANAGEMENT' || role === 'ADMIN') && (
            <div style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', alignSelf: 'flex-end' }}>
              <Link href="/reports/monthly" style={{
                fontSize: 13, color: '#059669', fontWeight: 600,
                textDecoration: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4,
              }}>
                Monthly Report
                <svg width="14" height="14" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M13.5 4.5L21 12m0 0l-7.5 7.5M21 12H3" />
                </svg>
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ── Table ── */}
        <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
          <table className="requests-table" style={{
            fontSize: 13, borderCollapse: 'collapse',
            tableLayout: 'fixed', width: 'max-content', minWidth: '100%',
          }}>
            <colgroup>
              <col style={{ width: 40 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 60 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 80 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 80 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 100 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 300 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 300 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 120 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 200 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 160 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 160 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 160 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 110 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 100 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 120 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 140 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 130 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 70 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 120 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 140 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 110 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 130 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 130 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 130 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 110 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 120 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 110 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 120 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 120 }} />
            </colgroup>

            <thead>
              <tr style={{
                background: '#f8fafc',
                borderTop: `3px solid ${TEAL_ACCENT}`,
                borderBottom: `1px solid ${TEAL_BORDER}`,
              }}>
                <th style={{
                  padding: '11px 12px',
                  background: '#f8fafc',
                  borderRight: `1px solid ${TEAL_SEP}`,
                }}>
                  <input type="checkbox"
                    checked={selected.size === requests.length && requests.length > 0}
                    onChange={toggleAll}
                    style={{ accentColor: TEAL_ACCENT, width: 14, height: 14 }} />
                </th>

                <TH>Year</TH>
                <TH>Season</TH>
                <TH>Crop</TH>
                <TH>Program</TH>
                <TH>Nursery Name</TH>
                <TH>Purpose</TH>
                <TH>Material Stage</TH>

                <th data-sticky="true" style={{
                  padding: '11px 12px',
                  textAlign: 'left', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 11,
                  color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase' as const, letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  position: 'sticky', left: LEFT_PROJECT, zIndex: 3,
                  borderRight: `1px solid ${TEAL_SEP}`,
                }}>
                  Project Code
                </th>

                <TH>Project</TH>

                <th data-sticky="true" style={{
                  padding: '11px 12px',
                  textAlign: 'left', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 11,
                  color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase' as const, letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  position: 'sticky', left: LEFT_ANALYSIS, zIndex: 3,
                  borderRight: `1px solid ${TEAL_SEP}`,
                }}>
                  Analysis
                </th>

                <th data-sticky="true" style={{
                  padding: '11px 12px',
                  textAlign: 'left', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 11,
                  color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase' as const, letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  position: 'sticky', left: LEFT_TRAIT, zIndex: 3,
                  borderRight: `1px solid ${TEAL_SEP}`,
                }}>
                  Trait/Gene
                </th>

                <TH>Markers/Sample</TH>
                <TH>Marker Type</TH>
                <TH>Predicted Samples</TH>
                <TH>Predicted Data Points</TH>
                <TH>Tentative Sampling</TH>
                <TH>Priority</TH>
                <TH>Expected By</TH>
                <TH>Remarks</TH>
                <TH>Actual Samples</TH>
                <TH>Actual Data Points</TH>
                <TH>Date of Sampling</TH>
                <TH>Date of Dispatch</TH>
                <TH>DOD (Lab)</TH>
                <TH>DNA Extraction</TH>
                <TH>Genotyping</TH>
                <TH>Results Comm.</TH>
                <th style={{
                  padding: '11px 12px',
                  textAlign: 'left',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  fontSize: 11,
                  color: '#64748b',
                  textTransform: 'uppercase',
                  letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  background: '#f8fafc',
                }}>
                  Status
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {requests.map((r, i) => {
                const evenBg = '#ffffff'
                const oddBg  = '#f8fafc'
                const rowBg  = i % 2 === 0 ? evenBg : oddBg
                const rowClass = ['inv-row', selected.has(r.id) ? 'inv-row-selected' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')

                return (
                  <>
                    <tr
                      key={r.id}
                      className={rowClass}
                      onClick={() => {
                        const next = new Set(selected)
                        if (next.has(r.id)) next.delete(r.id); else next.add(r.id)
                        setSelected(next)
                      }}
                      style={{
                        borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
                        background: rowBg,
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                      }}
                    >
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 12px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                        <input type="checkbox"
                          checked={selected.has(r.id)}
                          onChange={() => toggleOne(r.id)}
                          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
                          style={{ accentColor: TEAL_ACCENT, width: 14, height: 14 }} />
                      </td>
                      <TD>{r.year ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{r.season ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{r.crop ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD maxW={100}>{r.program ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD maxW={200}>{r.sampleName}</TD>
                      <TD maxW={300}>{r.purpose ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD maxW={120}>{r.materialStage ?? '—'}</TD>

                      <td
                        data-sticky="true"
                        style={{
                          padding: '12px 12px',
                          fontSize: 13,
                          whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                          position: 'sticky',
                          left: LEFT_PROJECT,
                          zIndex: 2,
                          background: rowBg,
                        }}
                      >
                        <Link href={`/requests/${r.id}`} style={{
                          color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: 'none',
                        }}>
                          {r.projectCode ?? '—'}
                        </Link>
                      </td>

                      <TD maxW={160}>{r.project ?? '—'}</TD>

                      <td
                        data-sticky="true"
                        style={{
                          padding: '12px 12px',
                          fontSize: 13,
                          color: '#374151',
                          whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                          maxWidth: 160,
                          overflow: 'hidden',
                          textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                          position: 'sticky',
                          left: LEFT_ANALYSIS,
                          zIndex: 2,
                          background: rowBg,
                        }}
                        title={r.analysisToBeDone ?? undefined}
                      >
                        {r.analysisToBeDone ?? '—'}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        data-sticky="true"
                        style={{
                          padding: '12px 12px',
                          fontSize: 13,
                          color: '#374151',
                          whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                          maxWidth: 160,
                          overflow: 'hidden',
                          textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                          position: 'sticky',
                          left: LEFT_TRAIT,
                          zIndex: 2,
                          background: rowBg,
                        }}
                        title={r.traitGene ?? undefined}
                      >
                        {r.traitGene ?? '—'}
                      </td>

                      <TD>{r.markersPerSample ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{r.markerType ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{r.predictedSamples ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{r.predictedDataPoints ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.tentativeSamplingDate)}</TD>
                      <TD>
                        {/* FIX: priority=2 was displayed as "Medium" here, but the submission
                            form labels it "Normal" and the approvals page labels it "Normal".
                            All three surfaces now use High / Normal / Low consistently. */}
                        {r.priority === 3 ? (
                          <span style={{ display:'inline-block', padding:'2px 9px', borderRadius:20, fontSize:11, fontWeight:700, whiteSpace:'nowrap', background:'rgba(254,226,226,0.8)', color:'#b91c1c' }}>High</span>
                        ) : r.priority === 2 ? (
                          <span style={{ display:'inline-block', padding:'2px 9px', borderRadius:20, fontSize:11, fontWeight:700, whiteSpace:'nowrap', background:'rgba(219,234,254,0.8)', color:'#1d4ed8' }}>Normal</span>
                        ) : (
                          <span style={{ display:'inline-block', padding:'2px 9px', borderRadius:20, fontSize:11, fontWeight:700, whiteSpace:'nowrap', background:'rgba(241,245,249,0.8)', color:'#475569' }}>Low</span>
                        )}
                      </TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.expectedByDate)}</TD>
                      <TD maxW={140}>{r.notes ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{r.actualSamples ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{r.actualDataPoints ?? '—'}</TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.dateOfSampling)}</TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.dateOfDispatch)}</TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.dodCommittedByLab)}</TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.dnaExtractionDate)}</TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.genotypingDate)}</TD>
                      <TD>{fmt(r.resultsCommunicationDate)}</TD>
                      <td style={{ padding: '12px 12px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                        <StatusBadge status={r.currentPhase || r.status} />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  </>
                )
              })}

              {requests.length === 0 && (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={TOTAL_COLS} style={{
                    padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14,
                  }}>
                    No requests found.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {/* ── Pagination ── */}
        <div style={{
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
          padding: '14px 20px', borderTop: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
          background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.5)',
        }}>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0, fontWeight: 500 }}>
            Showing{' '}
            <span style={{ color: '#374151', fontWeight: 600 }}>
              {Math.min((page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1, total)}–{Math.min(page * PAGE_SIZE, total)}
            </span>
            {' '}of{' '}
            <span style={{ color: '#374151', fontWeight: 600 }}>{total}</span>
          </p>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
            <button disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)} style={{
              padding: '6px 18px', border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
              borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 13, cursor: 'pointer',
              background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151', fontWeight: 500,
              opacity: page <= 1 ? 0.4 : 1, transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
            }}>← Prev</button>
            <button disabled={page * PAGE_SIZE >= total} onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)} style={{
              padding: '6px 18px', border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
              borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 13, cursor: 'pointer',
              background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151', fontWeight: 500,
              opacity: page * PAGE_SIZE >= total ? 0.4 : 1, transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
            }}>Next →</button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ── Style constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const labelStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b',
  textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em',
}

const inputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: '7px 11px', fontSize: 13,
  borderRadius: 10, border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)',
  background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)', color: '#374151', outline: 'none',
}


===== pages\requests\new.tsx =====

// pages/requests/new.tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'

const THIRTY_DAY_ANALYSES = ['background', 'line fingerprinting', 'line_fingerprinting']

function getTestType(analysisToBeDone: string): '15-DAY' | '30-DAY' {
  const lower = analysisToBeDone.toLowerCase()
  return THIRTY_DAY_ANALYSES.some(a => lower.includes(a)) ? '30-DAY' : '15-DAY'
}

function getDodDays(testType: '15-DAY' | '30-DAY'): number {
  return testType === '30-DAY' ? 30 : 15
}

function calcDodDate(testType: '15-DAY' | '30-DAY'): string {
  const days = getDodDays(testType)
  const d = new Date(Date.now() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0]
}

const ANALYSIS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: 'Foreground (15-day)',              value: 'Foreground',             days: 15 },
  { label: 'Hybridity Confirmation (15-day)', value: 'Hybridity_Confirmation', days: 15 },
  { label: 'Purity Analysis (15-day)',         value: 'Purity_Analysis',        days: 15 },
  { label: 'Gene Profiling (15-day)',          value: 'Gene Profiling',         days: 15 },
  { label: 'Background (30-day)',              value: 'Background',             days: 30 },
  { label: 'Line Fingerprinting (30-day)',     value: 'Line Fingerprinting',    days: 30 },
]

const MARKER_TYPE_OPTIONS = ['SNP', 'SSR', 'BOTH']
const CROP_OPTIONS        = ['Rice', 'Cotton', 'Corn']
const SEASON_OPTIONS = [
  { label: 'Rabi',   value: 'RABI'   },
  { label: 'Summer', value: 'SUMMER' },
  { label: 'Kharif', value: 'Kharif' },
]

const APPROVAL_PRIORITY_OPTIONS = [
  { level: 'HIGH'   as const, color: '#b91c1c', bg: 'rgba(254,226,226,0.8)', desc: 'Urgent — processed first' },
  { level: 'NORMAL' as const, color: '#1d4ed8', bg: 'rgba(219,234,254,0.8)', desc: 'Standard processing order' },
  { level: 'LOW'    as const, color: '#475569', bg: 'rgba(241,245,249,0.8)', desc: 'Lower urgency — last'      },
]

  sampleName:            string
  crop:                  string

const IC = [
