)

// ─── Sub-components ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function SectionCard({ icon, title, accent, children, headerRight }: {
  icon: React.ReactNode
  title: string
  accent?: string
  children: React.ReactNode
  headerRight?: React.ReactNode
}) {
  const color = accent || '#059669'
  return (
    <div style={{
      background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
      border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
      borderRadius: 20,
      boxShadow: '0 8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)',
      overflow: 'hidden',
    }}>
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
        padding: '18px 24px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(241,245,249,0.9)',
        background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.6)',
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
          <span style={{
            width: 34, height: 34,
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
            background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${color}18, ${color}10)`,
            borderRadius: 10, color,
          }}>
            {icon}
          </span>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: '#1e293b', margin: 0, letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}>
            {title}
          </h2>
        </div>
        {headerRight}
      </div>
      <div style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function DataGrid({ items }: { items: [string, string | null | undefined][] }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: '16px 32px' }}>
      {items.map(([label, value]) => (
        <div key={label}>
          <dt style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: 600, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', marginBottom: 4 }}>
            {label}
          </dt>
          <dd style={{ fontSize: 14, color: value ? '#1e293b' : '#cbd5e1', fontWeight: value ? 600 : 400, margin: 0 }}>
            {value || '—'}
          </dd>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

const inputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box',
  border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 10,
  padding: '9px 12px', fontSize: 13, color: '#1e293b',
  background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)',
  outline: 'none', transition: 'border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s',
}

const readonlyInputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  ...inputStyle,
  background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.8)',
  color: '#64748b',
  cursor: 'not-allowed',
}

const labelStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  display: 'block', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700,
  color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase',
  letterSpacing: '0.07em', marginBottom: 6,
}

function FormField({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label style={labelStyle}>{label}</label>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

// ─── Main Page ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function RequestDetailPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const { id } = router.query

  const [req, setReq]             = useState<RequestDetail | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading]     = useState(true)
  const [error, setError]         = useState('')
  const [advancing, setAdvancing] = useState(false)
  const [editingPart2, setEditingPart2] = useState(false)
  const [savingPart2, setSavingPart2]   = useState(false)
  const [part2Error, setPart2Error]     = useState('')
  const [part2Success, setPart2Success] = useState(false)

  const [part2Form, setPart2Form] = useState({
    analysisToBeDone: '',
    tentativeSamplingDate: '',
    priority: '1',
    expectedByDate: '',
    notes: '',
    actualSamples: '',
    actualDataPoints: '',
    dateOfSampling: '',
    dateOfDispatch: '',
    // Marker Type — editable in Additional Details when not set in Request Details
    markerType: '',
    // BOTH only — manually entered, must sum to actualSamples
    snpCount: '',
    ssrCount: '',
  })

  const role   = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.role   as string
  const userId = (session?.user as Record<string, unknown>)?.id     as string

  const canAdvance     = role === 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST' || role === 'ADMIN'
  const canSeeReagents = role === 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST' || role === 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' || role === 'ADMIN'

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'unauthenticated') router.push('/login')
  }, [status, router])

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    if (!id) return
    fetch(`/api/requests/${id}`)
      .then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Request not found'); return r.json() })
      .then((data: RequestDetail) => {
        setReq(data)
        setLoading(false)
        setPart2Form({
          analysisToBeDone:      data.analysisToBeDone      ?? '',
          tentativeSamplingDate: toInputDate(data.tentativeSamplingDate),
          priority:              String(data.priority ?? 1),
          expectedByDate:        toInputDate(data.expectedByDate),
          notes:                 data.notes                 ?? '',
          actualSamples:         data.actualSamples    != null ? String(data.actualSamples)    : '',
          actualDataPoints:      data.actualDataPoints  != null ? String(data.actualDataPoints) : '',
          dateOfSampling:        toInputDate(data.dateOfSampling),
          dateOfDispatch:        toInputDate(data.dateOfDispatch),
          markerType:            data.markerType ?? '',
          snpCount:              data.snpCount  != null ? String(data.snpCount)  : '',
          ssrCount:              data.ssrCount  != null ? String(data.ssrCount)  : '',
        })
      })
      .catch(e => { setError(e.message); setLoading(false) })
  }, [id])

  useEffect(() => { if (session && id) load() }, [session, id, load])

  const canEditPart2 =
    role === 'ADMIN' || role === 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST' ||
    (role === 'BREEDER' && req?.submittedById === parseInt(userId))

  async function handleAdvance() {
    if (!req) return
    const currentIndex = PHASES.indexOf(req.currentPhase)
    const nextPhase    = PHASES[currentIndex + 1]
    if (!nextPhase) return
    if (!window.confirm(`Advance to: ${PHASE_LABELS[nextPhase]}?`)) return
    setAdvancing(true)
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/requests/${req.id}/phase`, {
        method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ notes: '' }),
      })
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to advance phase')
      load()
    } catch { alert('Could not advance phase. Please try again.') }
    finally { setAdvancing(false) }
  }

  async function handleSavePart2(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setPart2Error(''); setPart2Success(false)

    // The effective marker type: from req if already set, otherwise from the form
    const effectiveMarkerType = (req?.markerType ?? part2Form.markerType).toUpperCase().trim()

    // ── Validate BOTH: snp + ssr must equal actualSamples ──────────────────
    if (effectiveMarkerType === 'BOTH') {
      const actual = parseFloat(part2Form.actualSamples)
      const snp    = parseFloat(part2Form.snpCount)
      const ssr    = parseFloat(part2Form.ssrCount)
      if (!isNaN(actual) && !isNaN(snp) && !isNaN(ssr)) {
        if (snp + ssr !== actual) {
          setPart2Error(
            `No. of SNP (${snp}) + No. of SSR (${ssr}) must equal Actual Samples (${actual}). Currently sums to ${snp + ssr}.`
          )
          return
        }
      }
    }

    setSavingPart2(true)
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/requests/${req!.id}`, {
        method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(part2Form),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed')
      setPart2Success(true); setEditingPart2(false); load()
      setTimeout(() => setPart2Success(false), 3000)
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      setPart2Error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Unknown error')
    } finally { setSavingPart2(false) }
  }

  if (status === 'loading' || loading) return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: 240 }}>
      <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
        <IconSpinner />
        <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 14, marginTop: 12 }}>Loading request...</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )

  if (error || !req) return (
    <div style={{ padding: 32 }}>
      <div style={{
        background: 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)', border: '1px solid rgba(252,165,165,0.5)',
        borderRadius: 14, padding: '16px 20px', color: '#b91c1c', fontSize: 14,
      }}>
        {error || 'Request not found'}
      </div>
      <button onClick={() => router.push('/requests')}
        style={{ marginTop: 16, color: '#059669', fontSize: 13, background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
        ← Back to requests
      </button>
    </div>
  )

  // ── req is non-null past this point ──────────────────────────────────────
  const r = req

  const currentIndex = PHASES.indexOf(r.currentPhase)
  const nextPhase    = PHASES[currentIndex + 1]

  const badgePhase = r.status === 'CANCELLED' ? 'CANCELLED' : r.currentPhase
  const sc = PHASE_BADGE[badgePhase] ?? { bg: 'rgba(241,245,249,0.8)', color: '#475569' }
  const scLabel = r.status === 'CANCELLED'
    ? 'Cancelled'
    : (PHASE_LABELS[r.currentPhase] ?? r.currentPhase)

  // ── Predicted SNP/SSR (Request Details — read-only display) ───────────────
  //   SNP  → snp = predictedSamples, ssr = 0
  //   SSR  → snp = 0,                ssr = predictedSamples
  //   BOTH → snp = stored snpCount,  ssr = stored ssrCount  (manual split)
  const predDerived = derivePredictedSnpSsr(r.markerType, r.predictedSamples, r.snpCount, r.ssrCount)

  // ── Effective marker type for the edit form ───────────────────────────────
  // Locked to req.markerType if already set; otherwise user can select in form.
  const formMarkerType     = r.markerType ?? part2Form.markerType
  const isBoth             = formMarkerType.toUpperCase() === 'BOTH'
  const markerTypeIsLocked = !!r.markerType

  // ── Live sum feedback for BOTH validation in edit mode ────────────────────
  const formActualSamples = part2Form.actualSamples !== '' ? parseFloat(part2Form.actualSamples) : null
  const formSnp           = part2Form.snpCount      !== '' ? parseFloat(part2Form.snpCount)      : null
  const formSsr           = part2Form.ssrCount      !== '' ? parseFloat(part2Form.ssrCount)      : null
  const formBothSum       = (formSnp ?? 0) + (formSsr ?? 0)
  const formBothValid     =
    formActualSamples !== null && formSnp !== null && formSsr !== null
      ? formBothSum === formActualSamples
      : true   // don't show error until all three are filled

  // ── Read-only actual SNP/SSR display helpers ──────────────────────────────
  //   SNP  → snp = actualSamples (auto), ssr = 0 (auto)
  //   SSR  → snp = 0 (auto),             ssr = actualSamples (auto)
  //   BOTH → snp = stored snpCount,       ssr = stored ssrCount  (manual split)
  function readonlySnpDisplay(): string {
    if (!r.markerType) return '—'
    const mt = r.markerType.toUpperCase()
    if (mt === 'SNP')  return r.actualSamples != null ? String(r.actualSamples) : '—'
    if (mt === 'SSR')  return '0'
    if (mt === 'BOTH') return r.snpCount != null ? String(r.snpCount) : '—'
    return '—'
  }

  function readonlySsrDisplay(): string {
    if (!r.markerType) return '—'
    const mt = r.markerType.toUpperCase()
    if (mt === 'SNP')  return '0'
    if (mt === 'SSR')  return r.actualSamples != null ? String(r.actualSamples) : '—'
    if (mt === 'BOTH') return r.ssrCount != null ? String(r.ssrCount) : '—'
    return '—'
  }

  return (
    <>
      <style>{`
        @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg) } }
        input:focus, select:focus, textarea:focus {
          border-color: #10b981 !important;
          box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(16,185,129,0.12) !important;
        }
      `}</style>

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 900, margin: '0 auto', padding: '32px 24px 48px' }}>

        {/* ── Page header ── */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 28 }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => router.push('/requests')}
            style={{
              display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
              fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: '#64748b',
              background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
              padding: '6px 0', marginBottom: 16, transition: 'color 0.15s',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#0f172a')}
            onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#64748b')}
          >
            <IconBack /> Back to Requests
          </button>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 16 }}>
            <div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                <h1 style={{
                  fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 900, color: '#0f172a',
                  letterSpacing: '-0.03em', margin: 0, lineHeight: 1.1,
                }}>
                  {r.projectCode ?? r.sampleName}
                </h1>
                <span style={{
                  padding: '4px 12px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                  background: sc.bg, color: sc.color,
                }}>
                  {scLabel}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#64748b', marginTop: 6, marginBottom: 0 }}>
                {r.sampleName} · Submitted by {r.submittedBy.name} · {fmt(r.submittedAt)}
              </p>
            </div>

            {canAdvance && nextPhase && r.status !== 'COMPLETED' && (
              <button
                onClick={handleAdvance}
                disabled={advancing}
                style={{
                  display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                  padding: '10px 20px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                  border: 'none', borderRadius: 12, cursor: advancing ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                  background: advancing
                    ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.4)'
                    : 'linear-gradient(135deg, #059669 0%, #10b981 50%, #06b6d4 100%)',
                  color: '#fff',
                  boxShadow: advancing ? 'none' : '0 4px 14px rgba(16,185,129,0.35)',
                  transition: 'all 0.15s',
                }}
                onMouseEnter={e => { if (!advancing) e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)' }}
                onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'none' }}
              >
                {advancing ? <IconSpinner /> : '→'}
                {advancing ? 'Advancing…' : `Advance to ${PHASE_LABELS[nextPhase]}`}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20 }}>

          {/* ── Phase timeline ── */}
          <SectionCard icon={<IconPhase />} title="Phase Progress" accent="#6366f1">
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
              {PHASES.map((phase, i) => {
                const done    = i < currentIndex
                const current = i === currentIndex
                const hist    = r.phaseHistory.find(h => h.phase === phase)
                return (
                  <div key={phase} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', flex: i < PHASES.length - 1 ? 1 : 'none' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                      <div style={{
                        width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: '50%',
                        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                        fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700,
                        background: done    ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#059669,#10b981)'
                                  : current ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#6366f1,#8b5cf6)'
                                  : '#f1f5f9',
                        color: done || current ? '#fff' : '#94a3b8',
                        boxShadow: current ? '0 4px 12px rgba(99,102,241,0.35)' : 'none',
                        flexShrink: 0,
                      }}>
                        {done ? '✓' : i + 1}
                      </div>
                      <span style={{
                        fontSize: 10, marginTop: 6, textAlign: 'center', width: 72,
                        color: current ? '#6366f1' : done ? '#059669' : '#94a3b8',
                        fontWeight: current ? 700 : 500,
                        lineHeight: 1.3,
                      }}>
                        {PHASE_LABELS[phase]}
                      </span>
                      {hist && (
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 9, color: '#cbd5e1', marginTop: 3, textAlign: 'center' }}>
                          {fmt(hist.startedAt)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    {i < PHASES.length - 1 && (
                      <div style={{
                        flex: 1, height: 2, marginTop: 17, marginLeft: 4, marginRight: 4,
                        background: done
                          ? 'linear-gradient(90deg,#059669,#10b981)'
                          : '#e2e8f0',
                        borderRadius: 2,
                        transition: 'background 0.3s',
                      }} />
                    )}
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </SectionCard>

          {/* ── Part 1: Request Details (read-only) ── */}
          {/*
            PREDICTED SNP / SSR rules:
              SNP  → No. of SNP = Predicted Samples,       No. of SSR = 0
              SSR  → No. of SNP = 0,                       No. of SSR = Predicted Samples
              BOTH → No. of SNP = stored snpCount (split), No. of SSR = stored ssrCount (split)
                     (manual split entered at submission — snpCount + ssrCount = predictedSamples)
          */}
          <SectionCard icon={<IconInfo />} title="Request Details" accent="#0ea5e9">
            <DataGrid items={[
              ['Year',               r.year?.toString()],
              ['Season',             r.season],
              ['Crop',               r.crop],
              ['Program',            r.program],
              ['Nursery Name',       r.sampleName],
              ['Purpose',            r.purpose],
              ['Material Stage',     r.materialStage],
              ['Project Code',       r.projectCode],
              ['Project',            r.project],
              ['Analysis',           r.analysisToBeDone],
              ['Trait / Gene',       r.traitGene],
              ['Markers per Sample', r.markersPerSample?.toString()],
              ['Marker Type',        r.markerType],
              ['No. of SNP (Predicted)',
                r.markerType ? String(predDerived.snp) : undefined],
              ['No. of SSR (Predicted)',
                r.markerType ? String(predDerived.ssr) : undefined],
              ['Predicted Samples',  r.predictedSamples?.toString()],
              ['Predicted Data Pts', r.predictedDataPoints?.toString()],
              ['Submitted By',       r.submittedBy.name],
            ]} />
          </SectionCard>

          {/* ── Part 2: Additional Details ── */}
          {/*
            ACTUAL SNP / SSR rules:
              SNP  → No. of SNP (Actual) = Actual Samples (auto), No. of SSR (Actual) = 0 (auto)
              SSR  → No. of SNP (Actual) = 0 (auto),              No. of SSR (Actual) = Actual Samples (auto)
              BOTH → No. of SNP (Actual) + No. of SSR (Actual) = Actual Samples
                     (manually entered split, e.g. 30 SNP + 70 SSR = 100 actual)
          */}
          <SectionCard
            icon={<IconEdit />}
            title="Additional Details"
            accent="#f59e0b"
            headerRight={
              canEditPart2 && !editingPart2 ? (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setEditingPart2(true)}
                  style={{
                    display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
                    padding: '6px 14px', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                    border: '1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.3)',
                    borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer',
                    background: 'rgba(254,243,199,0.6)', color: '#92400e',
                    transition: 'all 0.15s',
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(254,243,199,0.9)')}
                  onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(254,243,199,0.6)')}
                >
                  <IconEdit /> Edit
                </button>
              ) : undefined
            }
          >
            {part2Success && (
              <div style={{
                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                background: 'rgba(209,250,229,0.8)', border: '1px solid rgba(52,211,153,0.4)',
                borderRadius: 10, padding: '10px 14px',
                color: '#065f46', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, marginBottom: 20,
              }}>
                ✓ Saved successfully
              </div>
            )}

            {!editingPart2 ? (
              // ── Read-only view ──────────────────────────────────────────────
              <DataGrid items={[
                ['Priority',           r.priority === 3 ? 'High' : r.priority === 2 ? 'Medium' : 'Low'],
                ['Date Required By',   fmt(r.expectedByDate)],
                ['Date of Sampling',   fmt(r.dateOfSampling)],
                ['Date of Dispatch',   fmt(r.dateOfDispatch)],
                ['Actual Samples',     r.actualSamples?.toString()],
                ['Actual Data Points', r.actualDataPoints?.toString()],
                ['Marker Type',        r.markerType],
                // Always show SNP/SSR rows — '—' when not yet recorded
                ['No. of SNP (Actual)', readonlySnpDisplay()],
                ['No. of SSR (Actual)', readonlySsrDisplay()],
                ['Remarks',            r.notes],
              ]} />
            ) : (
              // ── Edit form ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
              <form onSubmit={handleSavePart2}>
                {part2Error && (
                  <div style={{
                    background: 'rgba(254,242,242,0.9)', border: '1px solid rgba(252,165,165,0.5)',
                    borderRadius: 10, padding: '10px 14px',
                    color: '#b91c1c', fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 20,
                  }}>
                    {part2Error}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* ── Updated Submission Details ── */}
                <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.08em', marginBottom: 14 }}>
                  Updated Submission Details
                </p>


                {/* ── Lab Details ── */}
                <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.08em', marginBottom: 14 }}>
                  Lab Details
                </p>
                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3,1fr)', gap: '14px 20px', marginBottom: 20 }}>
                  <FormField label="Priority">
                    <select value={part2Form.priority}
                      onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, priority: e.target.value }))}
                      style={inputStyle}>
                      <option value="1">Low</option>
                      <option value="2">Medium</option>
                      <option value="3">High</option>
                    </select>
                  </FormField>
                  <FormField label="Date Required By">
                    <input type="date" value={part2Form.expectedByDate}
                      onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, expectedByDate: e.target.value }))}
                      style={inputStyle} />
                  </FormField>
                  <FormField label="Actual Samples">
                    <input type="number" min="0" value={part2Form.actualSamples}
                      onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, actualSamples: e.target.value }))}
                      placeholder="e.g. 180" style={inputStyle} />
                  </FormField>
                  <FormField label="Actual Data Points">
                    <input type="number" min="0" value={part2Form.actualDataPoints}
                      onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, actualDataPoints: e.target.value }))}
                      placeholder="e.g. 900" style={inputStyle} />
                  </FormField>
                  <FormField label="Date of Sampling">
                    <input type="date" value={part2Form.dateOfSampling}
                      onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, dateOfSampling: e.target.value }))}
                      style={inputStyle} />
                  </FormField>
                  <FormField label="Date of Dispatch">
                    <input type="date" value={part2Form.dateOfDispatch}
                      onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, dateOfDispatch: e.target.value }))}
                      style={inputStyle} />
                  </FormField>
                </div>

                <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9', marginBottom: 20 }} />

                {/* ── Actual SNP / SSR Counts — always visible in edit mode ── */}
                <p style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#94a3b8', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.08em', marginBottom: 14 }}>
                  Actual SNP / SSR Counts
                </p>

                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3,1fr)', gap: '14px 20px', marginBottom: 4 }}>

                  {/* Marker Type — read-only if already set in Request Details, editable otherwise */}
                  <FormField label={markerTypeIsLocked ? 'Marker Type (from Request)' : 'Marker Type *'}>
                    {markerTypeIsLocked ? (
                      <>
                        <input type="text" readOnly value={r.markerType!} style={readonlyInputStyle} />
                        <p style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '4px 0 0' }}>
                          Set in Request Details — cannot be changed here
                        </p>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <select
                          value={part2Form.markerType}
                          onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({
                            ...f,
                            markerType: e.target.value,
                            // Reset counts when marker type changes
                            snpCount: '',
                            ssrCount: '',
                          }))}
                          style={inputStyle}
                        >
                          <option value="">Select marker type…</option>
                          {MARKER_TYPE_OPTIONS.map(mt => (
                            <option key={mt} value={mt}>{mt}</option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#f59e0b', margin: '4px 0 0', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                          Not set in Request Details — select here
                        </p>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </FormField>

                  {/* No. of SNP (Actual) */}
                  <FormField label={isBoth ? 'No. of SNP (Actual) *' : 'No. of SNP (Actual)'}>
                    {isBoth ? (
                      // BOTH → manually entered
                      <input
                        type="text" inputMode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]*"
                        value={part2Form.snpCount}
                        onChange={e => {
                          const val = e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')
                          setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, snpCount: val }))
                        }}
                        placeholder="e.g. 30"
                        style={{
                          ...inputStyle,
                          borderColor: !formBothValid ? '#fca5a5' : undefined,
                        }}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      // SNP or SSR → auto-calculated, read-only
                      <div style={{
                        ...readonlyInputStyle,
                        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        padding: '9px 12px',
                      }}>
                        <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f766e' }}>
                          {formMarkerType.toUpperCase() === 'SNP'
                            ? (formActualSamples !== null && !isNaN(formActualSamples) ? formActualSamples : (r.actualSamples ?? '—'))
                            : formMarkerType.toUpperCase() === 'SSR'
                            ? 0
                            : '—'}
                        </span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: 500, background: 'rgba(241,245,249,0.8)', padding: '2px 6px', borderRadius: 4 }}>
                          auto
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '4px 0 0' }}>
                      {!formMarkerType
                        ? 'Select Marker Type first'
                        : isBoth
                        ? 'Enter manually — SNP + SSR must equal Actual Samples'
                        : formMarkerType.toUpperCase() === 'SNP'
                        ? '= Actual Samples (Marker Type is SNP)'
                        : 'Always 0 (Marker Type is SSR)'}
                    </p>
                  </FormField>

                  {/* No. of SSR (Actual) */}
                  <FormField label={isBoth ? 'No. of SSR (Actual) *' : 'No. of SSR (Actual)'}>
                    {isBoth ? (
                      // BOTH → manually entered
                      <input
                        type="text" inputMode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]*"
                        value={part2Form.ssrCount}
                        onChange={e => {
                          const val = e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')
                          setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, ssrCount: val }))
                        }}
                        placeholder="e.g. 70"
                        style={{
                          ...inputStyle,
                          borderColor: !formBothValid ? '#fca5a5' : undefined,
                        }}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      // SNP or SSR → auto-calculated, read-only
                      <div style={{
                        ...readonlyInputStyle,
                        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        padding: '9px 12px',
                      }}>
                        <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f766e' }}>
                          {formMarkerType.toUpperCase() === 'SSR'
                            ? (formActualSamples !== null && !isNaN(formActualSamples) ? formActualSamples : (r.actualSamples ?? '—'))
                            : formMarkerType.toUpperCase() === 'SNP'
                            ? 0
                            : '—'}
                        </span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: 500, background: 'rgba(241,245,249,0.8)', padding: '2px 6px', borderRadius: 4 }}>
                          auto
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <p style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#94a3b8', margin: '4px 0 0' }}>
                      {!formMarkerType
                        ? 'Select Marker Type first'
                        : isBoth
                        ? 'Enter manually — SNP + SSR must equal Actual Samples'
                        : formMarkerType.toUpperCase() === 'SSR'
                        ? '= Actual Samples (Marker Type is SSR)'
                        : 'Always 0 (Marker Type is SNP)'}
                    </p>
                  </FormField>
                </div>

                {/* Live sum feedback for BOTH */}
                {isBoth && formSnp !== null && formSsr !== null && formActualSamples !== null && (
                  <div style={{
                    display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
                    marginBottom: 16, marginTop: 4,
                    padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600,
                    background: formBothValid ? 'rgba(209,250,229,0.7)' : 'rgba(254,226,226,0.8)',
                    color:      formBothValid ? '#065f46'               : '#991b1b',
                    border: `1px solid ${formBothValid ? 'rgba(52,211,153,0.3)' : 'rgba(252,165,165,0.4)'}`,
                  }}>
                    {formBothValid
                      ? `✓ ${formSnp} + ${formSsr} = ${formActualSamples} — Totals match`
                      : `✗ ${formSnp} + ${formSsr} = ${formBothSum} — Must equal ${formActualSamples}`}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Warning banner when no marker type selected at all */}
                {!formMarkerType && (
                  <div style={{
                    display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                    marginBottom: 16,
                    padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 500,
                    background: 'rgba(254,243,199,0.7)',
                    color: '#92400e',
                    border: '1px solid rgba(251,191,36,0.4)',
                  }}>
                    ⚠ Select a Marker Type above to enable SNP / SSR entry
                  </div>
                )}

                <FormField label="Remarks">
                  <textarea rows={2} value={part2Form.notes}
                    onChange={e => setPart2Form(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
                    placeholder="Any additional notes…"
                    style={{ ...inputStyle, resize: 'vertical' }} />
                </FormField>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, justifyContent: 'flex-end', marginTop: 20 }}>
                  <button type="button"
                    onClick={() => { setEditingPart2(false); setPart2Error('') }}
                    style={{
                      padding: '9px 20px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,
                      border: '1px solid rgba(209,213,219,0.8)', borderRadius: 10,
                      cursor: 'pointer', background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)', color: '#374151',
                    }}>
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button type="submit" disabled={savingPart2}
                    style={{
                      display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
                      padding: '9px 24px', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700,
                      border: 'none', borderRadius: 10,
                      cursor: savingPart2 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                      background: savingPart2
                        ? 'rgba(245,158,11,0.4)'
                        : 'linear-gradient(135deg,#f59e0b,#fbbf24)',
                      color: '#fff',
                      boxShadow: savingPart2 ? 'none' : '0 4px 14px rgba(245,158,11,0.35)',
                    }}>
                    {savingPart2 && <IconSpinner />}
                    {savingPart2 ? 'Saving…' : 'Save Changes'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </SectionCard>

          {/* ── Phase History ── */}
          {r.phaseHistory.length > 0 && (
            <SectionCard icon={<IconHistory />} title="Phase History" accent="#8b5cf6">
              <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
                <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: 13 }}>
                  <thead>
                    <tr style={{ background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.8)', borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9' }}>
                      {['Phase', 'Date', 'Notes'].map(h => (
                        <th key={h} style={{ padding: '10px 14px', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em' }}>
                          {h}
                        </th>
                      ))}
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {r.phaseHistory.map((h, i) => (
                      <tr key={h.id} style={{ borderBottom: i < r.phaseHistory.length - 1 ? '1px solid #f8fafc' : 'none' }}>
                        <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#1e293b' }}>
                          {PHASE_LABELS[h.phase] ?? h.phase}
                        </td>
                        <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', color: '#64748b' }}>{fmt(h.startedAt)}</td>
                        <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>{h.notes || '—'}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </SectionCard>
          )}

          {/* ── Reagent Logs ── */}
          {canSeeReagents && (
            <SectionCard icon={<IconReagent />} title={`Reagents Used (${r.reagentLogs.length})`} accent="#059669">
              {r.reagentLogs.length === 0 ? (
                <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13, margin: 0 }}>No reagents logged for this request yet.</p>
              ) : (
                <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
                  <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: 13 }}>
                    <thead>
                      <tr style={{ background: 'rgba(248,250,252,0.8)', borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9' }}>
                        {['Reagent', 'Qty Used', 'Purpose', 'Used By', 'Date'].map(h => (
                          <th key={h} style={{ padding: '10px 14px', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em' }}>
                            {h}
                          </th>
                        ))}
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {r.reagentLogs.map((log, i) => (
                        <tr key={log.id} style={{ borderBottom: i < r.reagentLogs.length - 1 ? '1px solid #f8fafc' : 'none' }}>
                          <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#1e293b' }}>{log.item.name}</td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', color: '#475569' }}>{log.quantityUsed} {log.item.unit}</td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', color: '#64748b' }}>{log.purpose}</td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', color: '#64748b' }}>{log.usedBy.name}</td>
                          <td style={{ padding: '12px 14px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>{fmt(log.usedAt)}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              )}
            </SectionCard>
          )}

        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}


===== pages\requests\[id]\print.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/router'
 
type PhaseHistory = { id: number; phase: string; notes: string | null; startedAt: string }
type ReagentLog   = {
  id: number; quantityUsed: number; purpose: string; usedAt: string
  item: { name: string; unit: string }; usedBy: { name: string }
}
type RequestDetail = {
  id: number; requestCode: string; sampleName: string
  crop: string | null; season: string | null; year: number | null
  program: string | null; purpose: string | null
  materialStage: string | null; project: string | null
  projectCode: string | null; testType: string
  analysisToBeDone: string | null; traitGene: string | null
  predictedSamples: number | null; actualSamples: number | null
  status: string; currentPhase: string; notes: string | null
  submittedAt: string; expectedByDate: string; completedAt: string | null
  submittedBy: { name: string; email: string }
  assignedLab: { name: string } | null
  phaseHistory: PhaseHistory[]
  reagentLogs: ReagentLog[]
}
 
const PHASE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  SUBMITTED: 'Submitted', RECEIVED: 'Received',
  DNA_ISOLATION: 'DNA Isolation', GENOTYPING: 'Genotyping',
  ANALYSIS: 'Analysis', COMPLETED: 'Completed',
}
 
function fmt(d: string | null) {
  if (!d) return '—'
  return new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric'
  })
}
 
function Row({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <tr style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>
      <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', color: '#6b7280', width: '35%',
                   fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500 }}>{label}</td>
      <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', color: '#1f2937', fontSize: 13 }}>{value}</td>
    </tr>
  )
}
 
export default function PrintRequestPage() {
  const { data: session, status } = useSession()
  const router = useRouter()
  const { id } = router.query
 
  const [req, setReq]       = useState<RequestDetail | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
 
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'unauthenticated') router.push('/login')
  }, [status, router])
 
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session || !id) return
    fetch(`/api/requests/${id}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => { setReq(data); setLoading(false) })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false))
  }, [session, id])
 
  if (loading || !req) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 40, color: '#9ca3af' }}>Loading...</div>
  }
 
  const printedOn = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit', month: 'long', year: 'numeric'
  })
 
  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: 'Arial, sans-serif', maxWidth: 800,
                  margin: '0 auto', padding: 32 }}>
 
      {/* Print button — hidden when printing */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 24, display: 'flex', gap: 12 }}
           className="no-print">
        <button
          onClick={() => window.print()}
          style={{ background: '#1d4ed8', color: '#fff', border: 'none',
                   padding: '8px 20px', borderRadius: 6, cursor: 'pointer',
                   fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600 }}
        >
          Print / Save as PDF
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => router.back()}
          style={{ background: '#f3f4f6', color: '#374151', border: 'none',
                   padding: '8px 20px', borderRadius: 6, cursor: 'pointer',
                   fontSize: 14 }}
        >
          ← Back
        </button>
      </div>
 
      {/* Print styles */}
      <style>{`
        @media print {
          .no-print { display: none !important; }
          body { margin: 0; }
        }
        @page { margin: 1.5cm; }
      `}</style>
 
      {/* Report header */}
      <div style={{ borderBottom: '3px solid #1d4ed8', paddingBottom: 16,
                    marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#6b7280', marginBottom: 4 }}>
          GenomicLab Management System
        </div>
        <h1 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 22, color: '#1e3a8a', fontWeight: 700 }}>
          {req.requestCode}
        </h1>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#374151', marginTop: 4 }}>
          {req.sampleName} &nbsp;·&nbsp; {req.testType}
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#9ca3af', marginTop: 8 }}>
          Printed on {printedOn}
        </div>
      </div>
 
      {/* Sample details */}
      <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#374151', marginBottom: 8,
                   fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase',
                   letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
        Sample Information
      </h2>
      <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse',
                      marginBottom: 24, border: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>
        <tbody>
          <Row label="Nursery Name"   value={req.sampleName} />
          <Row label="Crop"           value={req.crop        || '—'} />
          <Row label="Season"         value={req.season      || '—'} />
          <Row label="Year"           value={req.year?.toString() || '—'} />
          <Row label="Program"        value={req.program     || '—'} />
          <Row label="Purpose"        value={req.purpose     || '—'} />
          <Row label="Material Stage" value={req.materialStage || '—'} />
          <Row label="Project Code"   value={req.projectCode || '—'} />
          <Row label="Project"        value={req.project     || '—'} />
          <Row label="Analysis"       value={req.analysisToBeDone || '—'} />
          <Row label="Trait / Gene"   value={req.traitGene   || '—'} />
          <Row label="Pred. Samples"  value={req.predictedSamples?.toString() || '—'} />
          <Row label="Actual Samples" value={req.actualSamples?.toString()    || '—'} />
          <Row label="Submitted By"   value={req.submittedBy.name} />
          <Row label="Submitted At"   value={fmt(req.submittedAt)} />
          <Row label="Expected By"    value={fmt(req.expectedByDate)} />
          <Row label="Completed At"   value={fmt(req.completedAt)} />
          <Row label="Status"         value={req.status.replace(/_/g, ' ')} />
        </tbody>
      </table>
 
      {/* Phase history */}
      <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#374151', marginBottom: 8,
                   fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase',
                   letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
        Phase History
      </h2>
      <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse',
                      marginBottom: 24, border: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>
        <thead>
          <tr style={{ background: '#eff6ff' }}>
            <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 12,
                         color: '#1e40af', fontWeight: 600 }}>Phase</th>
            <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 12,
                         color: '#1e40af', fontWeight: 600 }}>Date</th>
            <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 12,
                         color: '#1e40af', fontWeight: 600 }}>Notes</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {req.phaseHistory.map(h => (
            <tr key={h.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>
              <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13,
                           fontWeight: 500 }}>
                {PHASE_LABELS[h.phase] ?? h.phase}
              </td>
              <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13,
                           color: '#6b7280' }}>{fmt(h.startedAt)}</td>
              <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13,
                           color: '#6b7280' }}>{h.notes || '—'}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
 
      {/* Reagent logs */}
      {req.reagentLogs.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#374151', marginBottom: 8,
                       fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase',
                       letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
            Reagents Used
          </h2>
          <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse',
                          marginBottom: 24, border: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: '#eff6ff' }}>
                {['Reagent','Qty','Unit','Purpose','Used By','Date'].map(h => (
                  <th key={h} style={{ padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: 'left',
                                       fontSize: 12, color: '#1e40af',
                                       fontWeight: 600 }}>{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {req.reagentLogs.map(log => (
                <tr key={log.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>
                  <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13 }}>{log.item.name}</td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13 }}>{log.quantityUsed}</td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13 }}>{log.item.unit}</td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13 }}>{log.purpose}</td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13 }}>{log.usedBy.name}</td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: 13,
                               color: '#6b7280' }}>{fmt(log.usedAt)}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </>
      )}
 
      {req.notes && (
        <>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#374151', marginBottom: 8,
                       fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase',
                       letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>Remarks</h2>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#374151', lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            {req.notes}
          </p>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}



===== prisma\clear.ts =====

// prisma/clear.ts
import 'dotenv/config'
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'

const prisma = new PrismaClient()

async function main() {
  await prisma.reagentLog.deleteMany({})
  await prisma.inventoryTransfer.deleteMany({})
  await prisma.inventoryItem.deleteMany({})
  await prisma.phaseHistory.deleteMany({})
  await prisma.auditLog.deleteMany({})
  await prisma.request.deleteMany({})
  await prisma.auditLog.deleteMany({})

  // Reset PostgreSQL sequences
  await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`ALTER SEQUENCE "ReagentLog_id_seq" RESTART WITH 1`)
  await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`ALTER SEQUENCE "InventoryTransfer_id_seq" RESTART WITH 1`)
  await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`ALTER SEQUENCE "InventoryItem_id_seq" RESTART WITH 1`)
  await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`ALTER SEQUENCE "Request_id_seq" RESTART WITH 1`)
  await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`ALTER SEQUENCE "PhaseHistory_id_seq" RESTART WITH 1`)

  console.log('✅ Cleared and reset ID sequences')
}

main().catch(console.error).finally(() => prisma.$disconnect())


===== prisma\schema.prisma =====

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

model Lab {
  id             Int             @id @default(autoincrement())
  name           String
  location       String
  InventoryItems InventoryItem[]
  requests       Request[]
  users          User[]
}

model User {
  id                    Int                    @id @default(autoincrement())
  email                 String                 @unique
  password              String
  name                  String
  role                  String
  labId                 Int?
  auditLogs             AuditLog[]
  consumptionDeductions ConsumptionDeduction[]
  transfersApproved     InventoryTransfer[]    @relation("TransferApproved")
  transfersRequested    InventoryTransfer[]    @relation("TransferRequested")
  reagentLogs           ReagentLog[]
  submittedRequests     Request[]
  lab                   Lab?                   @relation(fields: [labId], references: [id])
  approvedRequests      Request[]              @relation("RequestApprovedBy")
}

model Request {
  id                       Int                    @id @default(autoincrement())
  requestCode              String                 @unique
  sampleName               String
  crop                     String?
  season                   String?
  year                     Int?
  program                  String?
  purpose                  String?
  materialStage            String?
  project                  String?
  projectCode              String?
  testType                 String
  analysisToBeDone         String?
  traitGene                String?
  markersPerSample         Int?
  predictedSamples         Int?
  actualSamples            Int?
  priority                 Int                    @default(1)
  submittedAt              DateTime               @default(now())
  expectedByDate           DateTime
  tentativeSamplingDate    DateTime?
  dateOfSampling           DateTime?
  dateOfDispatch           DateTime?
  dodCommittedByLab        DateTime?
  dnaExtractionDate        DateTime?
  genotypingDate           DateTime?
  resultsCommunicationDate DateTime?
  completedAt              DateTime?
  currentPhase             String                 @default("SUBMITTED")
  status                   String                 @default("PENDING")
  notes                    String?
  submittedById            Int
  assignedLabId            Int?
  actualDataPoints         Int?
  archivedAt               DateTime?
  archived                 Boolean                @default(false)
  markerType               String?
  snpCount                 Int?
  ssrCount                 Int?
  predictedDataPoints      Int?
  phaseHistory             PhaseHistory[]
  reagentLogs              ReagentLog[]
  consumptionDeductions    ConsumptionDeduction[]
  assignedLab              Lab?                   @relation(fields: [assignedLabId], references: [id])
  submittedBy              User                   @relation(fields: [submittedById], references: [id])
  // Approval queue fields
  approvalStatus           String                 @default("PENDING_APPROVAL")
  approvalPriority         String                 @default("NORMAL")
  approvalDate             DateTime?
  approvedById             Int?
  queuePosition            Int?
  totalSamples             Int?
  approvedSamples          Int                    @default(0)
  rejectedSamples          Int                    @default(0)
  rejectionReason          String?
  approvedBy               User?                  @relation("RequestApprovedBy", fields: [approvedById], references: [id])
}

model PhaseHistory {
  id        Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  phase     String
  notes     String?
  startedAt DateTime @default(now())
  requestId Int
  request   Request  @relation(fields: [requestId], references: [id])
}

model InventoryItem {
  id                Int                      @id @default(autoincrement())
  name              String
  category          String
  quantity          Float
  unit              String
  minThreshold      Float
  expiryDate        DateTime?
  notes             String?
  lowStockThreshold Float?
  labId             Int
  createdAt         DateTime                 @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime                 @updatedAt
  brand             String?
  manufacturingDate DateTime?
  type              String                   @default("CHEMICAL")
  clusterItems      ConsumptionClusterItem[]
  experiments       ExperimentItem[]
  lab               Lab                      @relation(fields: [labId], references: [id])
  transfersFrom     InventoryTransfer[]      @relation("TransferFrom")
  transfersTo       InventoryTransfer[]      @relation("TransferTo")
  reagentLogs       ReagentLog[]
}

model ReagentLog {
  id           Int           @id @default(autoincrement())
  quantityUsed Float
  purpose      String
  usedAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  itemId       Int
  requestId    Int?
  usedById     Int
  item         InventoryItem @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id])
  request      Request?      @relation(fields: [requestId], references: [id])
  usedBy       User          @relation(fields: [usedById], references: [id])
}

model InventoryTransfer {
  id            Int           @id @default(autoincrement())
  quantity      Float
  reason        String?
  status        String        @default("PENDING")
  fromItemId    Int
  toItemId      Int
  requestedById Int
  approvedById  Int?
  createdAt     DateTime      @default(now())
  completedAt   DateTime?
  approvedBy    User?         @relation("TransferApproved", fields: [approvedById], references: [id])
  fromItem      InventoryItem @relation("TransferFrom", fields: [fromItemId], references: [id])
  requestedBy   User          @relation("TransferRequested", fields: [requestedById], references: [id])
  toItem        InventoryItem @relation("TransferTo", fields: [toItemId], references: [id])
}

model Experiment {
  id       Int                  @id @default(autoincrement())
  name     String
  clusters ConsumptionCluster[]
  items    ExperimentItem[]
}

model ExperimentItem {
  id           Int           @id @default(autoincrement())
  experimentId Int
  itemId       Int
  experiment   Experiment    @relation(fields: [experimentId], references: [id])
  item         InventoryItem @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id])
}

model AuditLog {
  id            Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  action        String
  entityType    String
  entityId      Int
  detail        String?
  performedAt   DateTime @default(now())
  performedById Int
  performedBy   User     @relation(fields: [performedById], references: [id])
}

model ConsumptionCluster {
  id           Int                      @id @default(autoincrement())
  name         String
  description  String?
  unit         String                   @default("sample")
  experimentId Int?
  sortOrder    Int                      @default(0)
  createdAt    DateTime                 @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime                 @updatedAt
  experiment   Experiment?              @relation(fields: [experimentId], references: [id])
  items        ConsumptionClusterItem[]
  deductions   ConsumptionDeduction[]
}

model ConsumptionClusterItem {
  id         Int                @id @default(autoincrement())
  qtyPerUnit Float
  clusterId  Int
  itemId     Int
  cluster    ConsumptionCluster @relation(fields: [clusterId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  item       InventoryItem      @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id])
}

model ConsumptionDeduction {
  id            Int                @id @default(autoincrement())
  units         Float
  notes         String?
  performedAt   DateTime           @default(now())
  clusterId     Int
  performedById Int
  // requestId is intentionally nullable: deductions may be performed
  // outside of a formal request (e.g. lab maintenance, calibration runs).
  // Do NOT add NOT NULL here. Use projectCode for traceability when no
  // request exists.
  requestId     Int?
  projectCode   String?
  cluster       ConsumptionCluster @relation(fields: [clusterId], references: [id])
  performedBy   User               @relation(fields: [performedById], references: [id])
  request       Request?           @relation(fields: [requestId], references: [id])
}

model CapacityTracking {
  id          Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  weekStart   DateTime
  serviceType String
  capacity    Int      @default(10000)
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([weekStart, serviceType])
}



===== prisma\seed.ts =====

import 'dotenv/config'
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs'

const prisma = new PrismaClient()

async function main() {
  console.log('ENV CHECK:', process.env.DATABASE_URL)

  const lab1 = await prisma.lab.upsert({
    where: { id: 1 },
    update: {},
    create: { name: 'Lab 1', location: 'Location 1' }
  })

  await prisma.lab.upsert({
    where: { id: 2 },
    update: {},
    create: { name: 'Lab 2', location: 'Location 2' }
  })

  const hashedPw = await bcrypt.hash('password123', 10)

  // BREEDER — submits and views all requests
  await prisma.user.upsert({
    where: { email: 'breeder1@lab.com' },
    update: { role: 'BREEDER' },
    create: {
      email: 'breeder1@lab.com',
      password: hashedPw,
      name: 'Breeder One',
      role: 'BREEDER'
    }
  })

  // BIOTECHNOLOGIST — advances phases, logs reagent usage
  await prisma.user.upsert({
    where: { email: 'biotechnologist1@lab.com' },
    update: { role: 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST' },
    create: {
      email: 'biotechnologist1@lab.com',
      password: hashedPw,
      name: 'Biotechnologist 1',
      role: 'BIOTECHNOLOGIST',
      labId: lab1.id
    }
  })

  // INVENTORY_MANAGER — manages stock, transfers, Excel import
  await prisma.user.upsert({
    where: { email: 'inv@lab.com' },
    update: { role: 'INVENTORY_MANAGER' },
    create: {
      email: 'inv@lab.com',
      password: hashedPw,
      name: 'Inventory Manager',
      role: 'INVENTORY_MANAGER',
      labId: lab1.id
    }
  })

  // MANAGEMENT — dashboard and requests read-only
  await prisma.user.upsert({
    where: { email: 'mgmt@lab.com' },
    update: { role: 'MANAGEMENT' },
    create: {
      email: 'mgmt@lab.com',
      password: hashedPw,
      name: 'Director',
      role: 'MANAGEMENT'
    }
  })

  // ADMIN — full access to everything
  await prisma.user.upsert({
    where: { email: 'admin@lab.com' },
    update: { role: 'ADMIN' },
    create: {
      email: 'admin@lab.com',
      password: hashedPw,
      name: 'System Admin',
      role: 'ADMIN'
    }
  })

  console.log('✅ Seed complete')
}

main()
  .catch((e) => {
    console.error(e)
    process.exit(1)
  })
  .finally(async () => {
    await prisma.$disconnect()
  })

===== scripts\fix-approval-status.js =====

// scripts/fix-approval-status.js

const { PrismaClient } = require('@prisma/client')
const prisma = new PrismaClient()


const APPROVAL_SYSTEM_GO_LIVE = new Date('2026-06-02T07:03:40Z')

async function main() {
  console.log('🔧 Fixing approval status for existing requests...\n')
  console.log(`   Legacy cutoff: all requests submitted before ${APPROVAL_SYSTEM_GO_LIVE.toISOString()}\n`)

  // Check column exists first
  const colCheck = await prisma.$queryRaw`
    SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_name = 'Request' AND column_name = 'approvalStatus'
  `
  if (colCheck.length === 0) {
    console.log('❌ approvalStatus column not found. Run the migration first.')
    return
  }

  // Fix 1: All COMPLETED requests → APPROVED
  const r1 = await prisma.$executeRaw`
    UPDATE "Request"
    SET
      "approvalStatus"  = 'APPROVED',
      "approvedSamples" = COALESCE("predictedSamples", "actualSamples", 0),
      "totalSamples"    = COALESCE("predictedSamples", "actualSamples", 0),
      "approvalDate"    = COALESCE("completedAt", "submittedAt"),
      "queuePosition"   = NULL
    WHERE "status" = 'COMPLETED'
      AND "approvalStatus" = 'PENDING_APPROVAL'
  `
  console.log(`✅ Completed requests fixed: ${r1}`)

  // Fix 2: PENDING/IN_PROGRESS requests already past SUBMITTED phase → APPROVED
  // These were already in the lab before the approval system existed.
  const r2 = await prisma.$executeRaw`
    UPDATE "Request"
    SET
      "approvalStatus"  = 'APPROVED',
      "approvedSamples" = COALESCE("predictedSamples", "actualSamples", 0),
      "totalSamples"    = COALESCE("predictedSamples", "actualSamples", 0),
      "approvalDate"    = "submittedAt",
      "queuePosition"   = NULL
    WHERE "status" IN ('PENDING', 'IN_PROGRESS')
      AND "currentPhase" != 'SUBMITTED'
      AND "approvalStatus" = 'PENDING_APPROVAL'
  `
  console.log(`✅ In-progress requests fixed: ${r2}`)

  // Fix 3: Remaining PENDING_APPROVAL requests submitted BEFORE the approval
  // system went live → approve as legacy.
  // FIX: Previously used "submitted > 2 hours ago" which would auto-approve
  // any legitimately pending request if this script was re-run.
  // Now uses a hard cutoff date so only pre-system records are affected.
  const r3 = await prisma.$executeRaw`
    UPDATE "Request"
    SET
      "approvalStatus"  = 'APPROVED',
      "approvedSamples" = COALESCE("predictedSamples", "actualSamples", 0),
      "totalSamples"    = COALESCE("predictedSamples", "actualSamples", 0),
      "approvalDate"    = "submittedAt",
      "queuePosition"   = NULL
    WHERE "approvalStatus" = 'PENDING_APPROVAL'
      AND "submittedAt" < ${APPROVAL_SYSTEM_GO_LIVE}
  `
  console.log(`✅ Legacy pre-system requests fixed: ${r3}`)

  // Fix 4: Backfill totalSamples on all approved requests
  const r4 = await prisma.$executeRaw`
    UPDATE "Request"
    SET "totalSamples" = COALESCE("predictedSamples", "actualSamples", 0)
    WHERE "approvalStatus" = 'APPROVED'
      AND "totalSamples" IS NULL
  `
  console.log(`✅ totalSamples backfilled: ${r4}`)

  // Show final counts
  const counts = await prisma.$queryRaw`
    SELECT "approvalStatus", COUNT(*)::int AS count
    FROM "Request"
    GROUP BY "approvalStatus"
    ORDER BY "approvalStatus"
  `
  console.log('\n📊 Final counts:')
  for (const row of counts) {
    console.log(`   ${row.approvalStatus}: ${row.count}`)
  }

  console.log('\n✨ Done! Refresh your Approvals page.')
}

main()
  .catch(e => { console.error('❌ Error:', e.message); process.exit(1) })
  .finally(() => prisma.$disconnect())

  // Background rows (IRTI001-C7 and IRTI004-C3 appear twice with different analysis — handled below)
  // Note: ICTP001 appears twice (T2 HSP and T1 HSP) — update both via requestCode if needed
]

// ── Rows that have duplicate projectCodes (Background analysis entries) ──────
// These share a projectCode with a Foreground entry. We update ALL matching records.
const BACKGROUND_DATA: Array<{
  projectCode: string
  markersPerSample: number | null
  markerType: string | null
  predictedSamples: number | null
  predictedDataPoints: number | null
  actualSamples: number | null
  actualDataPoints: number | null
  analysisToBeDone: string
}> = [
  { projectCode: 'IRTI001-C7', analysisToBeDone: 'Background', markersPerSample: 84, markerType: 'SNP', predictedSamples: 28,  predictedDataPoints: 2352, actualSamples: 23, actualDataPoints: 1932 },
  { projectCode: 'IRTI004-C3', analysisToBeDone: 'Background', markersPerSample: 95, markerType: 'SNP', predictedSamples: 25,  predictedDataPoints: 2375, actualSamples: 17, actualDataPoints: 1615 },
  { projectCode: 'IRTI006-C3', analysisToBeDone: 'Background', markersPerSample: 103, markerType: 'SNP', predictedSamples: 65, predictedDataPoints: 6695, actualSamples: 64, actualDataPoints: 6592 },
  // IRTP001 Foreground (BLB genes, 4 markers) — different from Purity_Analysis entry
  { projectCode: 'IRTP001', analysisToBeDone: 'Foreground', markersPerSample: 4, markerType: 'SNP', predictedSamples: 3600, predictedDataPoints: 14400, actualSamples: 3404, actualDataPoints: 13616 },
  { projectCode: 'IRTP002', analysisToBeDone: 'Foreground', markersPerSample: 3, markerType: 'SNP', predictedSamples: 1800, predictedDataPoints: 5400,  actualSamples: 1484, actualDataPoints: 4452  },
  // ICTP001 T1 HSP (different actual samples)
  { projectCode: 'ICTP001', analysisToBeDone: 'Foreground', markersPerSample: 2, markerType: 'SNP', predictedSamples: 3500, predictedDataPoints: 7000, actualSamples: 1131, actualDataPoints: 2262 },
]

async function main() {
  console.log('Starting fix-requests script...\n')
  let updated = 0
  let notFound = 0

  // ── Update standard rows (unique projectCode) ─────────────────────────────
  for (const row of DATA) {
    const code = row.projectCode.trim()
    try {
      const result = await prisma.request.updateMany({
        where: { projectCode: { equals: code, mode: 'insensitive' } },
        data: {
          markersPerSample:    row.markersPerSample,
          markerType:          row.markerType,
          predictedSamples:    row.predictedSamples,
          predictedDataPoints: row.predictedDataPoints,
          actualSamples:       row.actualSamples,
          actualDataPoints:    row.actualDataPoints,
        },
      })
      if (result.count === 0) {
        console.warn(`  ⚠ NOT FOUND: ${code}`)
        notFound++
      } else {
        console.log(`  ✓ ${code} — updated ${result.count} record(s)`)
        updated += result.count
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`  ✗ ERROR for ${code}:`, err)
    }
  }

  // ── Update background/duplicate rows (match by projectCode + analysis) ────
  for (const row of BACKGROUND_DATA) {
    const code = row.projectCode.trim()
    try {
      const result = await prisma.request.updateMany({
        where: {
          projectCode:     { equals: code, mode: 'insensitive' },
          analysisToBeDone: { contains: row.analysisToBeDone, mode: 'insensitive' },
        },
        data: {
          markersPerSample:    row.markersPerSample,
          markerType:          row.markerType,
          predictedSamples:    row.predictedSamples,
          predictedDataPoints: row.predictedDataPoints,
          actualSamples:       row.actualSamples,
          actualDataPoints:    row.actualDataPoints,
        },
      })
      if (result.count === 0) {
        console.warn(`  ⚠ NOT FOUND: ${code} (${row.analysisToBeDone})`)
        notFound++
      } else {
        console.log(`  ✓ ${code} (${row.analysisToBeDone}) — updated ${result.count} record(s)`)
        updated += result.count
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`  ✗ ERROR for ${code}:`, err)
    }
  }

  console.log(`\nDone. Updated: ${updated} | Not found: ${notFound}`)
}

main()
  .catch(console.error)
  .finally(() => prisma.$disconnect())

   DARK MODE: sticky cells
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
html.dark td[style*='sticky'],
html.dark th[style*='sticky'] {
  background: #1f2937 !important;
  color: #f9fafb !important;
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   DARK MODE: navbar
───────────────────────────────────────────── */
html.dark nav {
  border-color: rgba(55, 65, 81, 0.5) !important;
}


html, body, #__next {
  height: 100%;
}


html, body, #__next {
  height: 100%;
  max-width: 100%;
}

/* Sticky column cells — fully opaque, no bleed-through on scroll */
.requests-table td[data-sticky],
.requests-table th[data-sticky] {
  position: sticky;
  will-change: transform;
  /* Force a new stacking context so backdrop-filter cannot bleed through */
  isolation: isolate;
}
 
/* Solid backgrounds that override any semi-transparent rowBg */
.requests-table tbody tr:nth-child(odd)  td[data-sticky] { background: #ffffff; }
.requests-table tbody tr:nth-child(even) td[data-sticky] { background: #f8fafc; }
.requests-table thead th[data-sticky]                    { background: #f8fafc; }
 
/* Keep them solid on hover too */
.requests-table tbody tr:hover td[data-sticky] { background: #ccfbf1 !important; }
 
/* Subtle right shadow so the sticky edge is visible */
.requests-table td[data-sticky],
.requests-table th[data-sticky] {
  box-shadow: 2px 0 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.08);
}

// styles/globals.d.ts
declare module '*.css'


===== types\next-auth.d.ts =====

// types/next-auth.d.ts
import { DefaultSession } from "next-auth"

declare module "next-auth" {
  interface Session {
    user: {
      id: string
      role: string
    } & DefaultSession["user"]
  }

  interface User {
    id: string
    role: string
  }
}

declare module "next-auth/jwt" {
  interface JWT {
    id: string
    role: string
  }
}

// ── Canonical global Socket.IO declaration ───────────────────────────────────
declare global {
  var io: import('socket.io').Server | undefined
}
